Yuktabhavadeva of Bhavadevamiśra
Edited by M. L. Gharote and V. K. Jha. Lonavla: The Lonavla Yoga Institute, 2002
This text can be dated to śaka 1545 = 1623AD
Based on 4 Mss.
1. Bhārta Itihāsa Samśodhana Maṇḍala, pune. Ms. No. 46/42
2. Bhāratīya Sanskriti Kendra, Pune. Dated: śaka 1741 (1829AD)
3. Vaidaka Saṃśodhana Maṇḍala, Pune. Ms. No. 8632
4. Lālbhāi Dalpatbhāi Bhāratīya Sanskrita Vidyāmandira, Ahmedābād. Ms. No. 9566. Dated saṃvit 1950 (1893 AD).  

Chapters 1-2 typed by Jason Birch. Proofread by Ramya
Chapters 3-11 types by Ramya for the Haṭha Yoga Project. Not Proofread. 

[p.1]
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ |
śrīsarasvatyai namaḥ |
oṃ namas tasmai brahmaṇe |

tribhuvanavapur ekaṃ yogibhir dṛṣṭam uccais 
triguṇam api guṇebhyo yat padaṃ prāhur īḍyāḥ |
tad aham ahahavañcaj jñānamokṣasvarūpaṃ 
diśi diśi bahuśaktiṃ brahma sambhāvayāmi ||1||

praṇatajanahitāya brahmavidbhir munīndraiḥ 
prakaṭitam anuvāraṃ yogaśāstrasya sāram |
haribhavakulamauleḥ kṛṣṇadevasya sūnur
vilikhati bhavadevaḥ satyavatyāḥ samṛddhyai ||2||

satyaṃ brahma samīritaṃ tribhuvane tadbhāvanā tadvatī
prāptā saiva samṛddhivṛddhamatibhir brahmeti saṃgīyate |
tanmātrasphuradātmadīpakalikājyotiḥsvarūpātmikā
yāvasthā pratipādayanti munayas tām eva muktiṃ parāṃ ||3||

bhavena yukto yad devo viṣṇur atra nigadyate |
tad yuktabhavadeveti granthanāma prakāśitam ||4||

prajñā satyavatī nāmnī granthe yā brahmabhāvanā |
proktā sā yogasādhyeti yogo 'tra parikīrtyate ||5||

[p.2]
tatra prādhānyena yogo nāmāntaḥkaraṇavṛttinirodha eva |
In this text, yoga is chiefly just the cessation of mental activity.

tad uktaṃ pātañjale - 
yogaś cittavṛttinirodhaḥ |
tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpe 'vasthānam ity ayaṃ bhāvaḥ ||6||

sakaladīkkālavarttisukhasākṣātkārasvarūpa ātmā |
tasya svarūpeṇāvasthānaṃ muktidaśā |
nityaṃ vijñānam ānandaṃ brahmaṇo rūpaṃ tac ca mokṣe pratiṣṭhitaṃ ity ādiśruteḥ ||7||

avidyākṛtaghaṭādiviṣayākārākāritāntaḥkaraṇavṛttiḥ cittavṛttir | etasyām avasthāyām antaḥkaraṇasya viṣayāntarasañcāreṇātmanaḥ svasvarūpānandasākṣātkārāvasthā bhraṃśo bhavati | sa eva bandha ity ucyate | muktidaśānandasākṣātkāravigamāt ||8||

tad uktaṃ bhagavatā vaśiṣṭhena -
svarūpāvasthitir muktis tadbhraṃśo bandha ucyate iti |  sā ca vṛttiḥ sattvarajastamomūlakatvāt trividhā svasvakāryeṇa paricchidyate ||1||

tatkāryaṃ coktaṃ vyāsādibhiḥ - 

sattvodaye sukhaṃ śāntiḥ prītir lajjāṅgalāghavam |
kṣamā dhṛtirakārpaṇyaṃ damo jñānaṃ prasannatā ||10||

rajoguṇodaye labhaḥ santāpaḥ krodhavigrahau |
abhimāno mṛṣāvādaḥ pravṛttir dambha ity api ||11||

tamoguṇodaye tandrā moho nidrāṅgagauravam |
ālasyam aprabodhaś ca pramādaś caivamādyaḥ ||12||

[p.3]
śrīmadbhagavadgītāyām -
sattvaṃ rajas tama iti guṇāḥ prakṛtisambhavāḥ |
nibadhnanti mahābāho dehe dehinam avyayam  ||13|| (14.5)

tatra sattvaṃ nirmalatvāt prakāśakanirāmayam |
sukhasaṅgena badhnāti jñānasaṅgena cānagha ||14|| (14.6)

rajo rāgātmakaṃ viddhi tṛṣṇāsaṅgasamudbhavam |
tan nibadhnāti kaunteya karmasaṅgena dehinam ||15|| (14.7)

tamas tv ajñānajaṃ viddhi mohanaṃ sarvadehinām
pramādālasyanidrābhis tan nibadhnāti bhārata ||16|| (14.8)

sattvaṃ sukhe saṃjayati rajaḥ karmaṇi bhārata
jñānam āvṛtya tu tamaḥ pramāde saṃjayaty uta ||17|| ityādi(14.9)

atra sukhaṃ dvividhaṃ - sāhajikaṃ viṣayasambandhajam ca | tatrādyaṃ brahmasvarūpaṃ bhagavadbhaktiprasūtaṃ tatprasādasākṣātkaraṇīyaṃ brahmāviṣṇuśivasanakasanandananāradādibhir aharniśam upāsyate ||18||

dvitīyaṃ tu yajñadānatapojanyādṛṣṭajanyaṃ vijñātabrahmasukhair upekṣyam | duḥkhaduritānuviddhatayā madhuviṣasampṛktānnabhojanavadvivekibhir anādṛtam avivekibhir yathāśakti samupāsyate || vaiṣayikasukhāpekṣayā brahmasukhe prakarṣaḥ prasiddha eva ||19||

tathā bhāgavate bhagavān uddhavaṃ pratyuvaca -
[BhP_11.14.08-14]

manmāyāmohitadhiyaḥ puruṣāḥ puruṣarṣabha |
śreyo vadanty anekāntaṃ yathākarma yathāruci ||20|| 

[p.4]
dharmam eke yaśaś cānye kāmaṃ satyaṃ damaṃ śamam |
anye vadanti svārthaṃ vai aiśvaryaṃ tyāgabhojanam ||21||

kecid yajñaṃ tapo dānaṃ vratāni niyamān yamān |
ādyantavanta evaiṣāṃ lokāḥ karmavinirmitāḥ ||22||

duḥkhodarkās tamoniṣṭhāḥ kṣudrānandāḥ śucārpitāḥ |
mayy arpitātmanaḥ satye nirapekṣasya sarvataḥ |
mayātmanā sukhaṃ yat tat kutaḥ syād viṣayātmanām ||23||

akiñcanasya dāntasya śāntasya samacetasaḥ |
mayā santuṣṭamanasaḥ sarvāḥ sukhamayā diśaḥ ||24||

na pārameṣṭhyaṃ na mahendradhiṣṇyaṃ na sārvabhaumaṃ na rasādhipatyam |
na yogasiddhir apunarbhavaṃ vā mayy arpitātmecchati mad vinānyat ||25|| ityādi ||

upaniṣad api -

sa yo manuṣyāṇāṃ rāddhaḥ samṛddho bhavati | anyeṣām adhipatis sarvair mānuṣyakaiḥ kāmaiḥ sampannatamaḥ ||26||
[BrhUp_4,3.33]

sa manuṣyāṇāṃ parama ānandaḥ ity abhidhāya tacchataguṇaṃ pitṛṇāṃ tacchataguṇaṃ karmadevānāṃ tacchataguṇaṃ ājātadevānām tacchataguṇaṃ devaloke tacchataguṇaṃ gandharvaloke tacchataguṇaṃ prajāpatiloke ānandaṃ proktam āha ||27||

atha ete śataṃ prajāpatiloke ānandāḥ | sa eko brahmaloke ānando yaś ca śrotriyo vṛjino kāmahata ityādi ||28||
[p.5.]
ātmānaṃ ced vijānīyād ayam asmīti puruṣaḥ |
kim icchan kasya kāmāya śarīram anu sañcaran ||29|| [BāU 4.4.12; PD 14.5] 

tam etaṃ vedānuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti | brahmacaryeṇa yajñena tapasā śraddhayānāśakena caitam eva viditvā munir bhavati | etam eva pravrājino lokam īpsantaḥ pravrajanti || BrhUp_4,4.22 || etad dha sma caitat ||30||

pūrve brāhmaṇā anūcānā vidvāṃsaḥ prajāṃ na kāmayante kiṃ prajayā kariṣyāmo yeṣāṃ no 'yamātmāyaṃ loka iti | te ha sma putraiṣaṇāyāś ca jāyeṣaṇāyāś ca vittaiṣaṇāyāś ca lokaiṣaṇāyāś ca vyutthāya bhikṣācaryaṃ carantītyaadi. [BāU 
4.4.22] 

ata eva nirūpādhikam ātmasvarūpaṃ sukham etad vivakṣayā | na putrā putrāya priyā bhavanti ātmane vai putrāḥ priyā bhavanti evamādy ātmaupādhikatvavarṇanam anyeṣām iti dik ||32||

tasmād brahmasvarūpaparamānandasākṣātkāravirodhitvenāntaḥkaraṇavṛttir [vṛttiṃ?] nirodhayet || tatra sarvāntaḥkaraṇavṛttinirodhapūrvako 'yam ātmasākṣātkāraḥ sa eva nirvikalpaḥ samādhiḥ sadā savikalpakajñānābhāvāt || sa eva ca rājayogaḥ || 33||

anāyāsasiddaparamopādeyayogatvāt || yamādyaṣṭayogaprādhānyāc ca || tadīyātyantāntaraṅgaḥ vikalpakātmasākṣātkārarūpaḥ savikalpaḥ samādhiḥ ||34||

tadabhyāsavaśodbhūtā nandatirohitāyāṃ viśeṣaṇajñānādiviśiṣṭajñāna sāmagryāṃ svayam eva nirvikalpakātmasākṣātkāraḥ samullasatīti prapañcitaṃ prajñā satyavatyām || ātmaviṣayakajñanābhyāsaviṣaya vairāgyābhyāṃ caiṣa jāyate || tathā saty eva antaḥkaraṇasyaikāgryāt ||35||
[p.6]
tathā ca gītā - [6.35cd]
abhyāsena ca kaunteya vairāgyeṇa ca gṛhyate || 36|| iti||

pātañjale 'pi - [1.12-16]
abhyāsavairāgyābhyāṃ tannirodhaḥ || tatra sthitau yatno 'bhyāsaḥ || tatra dīrghakālanairantaryādāsevito dṛḍhabhūmiḥ || dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayavitṛṣṇasya vaśīkāro vairāgyam || tatparaṃ puruṣakhyāter guṇavaitṛṣṇyam || 37|| ityādi

evaṃ copadravarahite sthāne sukhāsanenopaviṣṭasyātmani mano dhārayato yogino rājayoge catvāro antarāyā bhavanti || te ca layavikṣepakaṣāyarasāsvādāḥ ||38|| [= Vedāntasāra 212]

layas tāvad akhaṇḍavastvanavalambena cittavṛtter nidrā || vikṣepas tu akhaṇḍavastvanavalambena cittavṛtter anyāvalambanam || kaṣāyas tu layavikṣepābhāve 'pi cittavṛtter rāgādivāsanayāstabdhībhāvād akhaṇḍavastvanavalambanaṃ ||39||

rasāsvādas tu akhaṇḍavastvanavalambane 'pi cittavṛtteḥ savikalpakarasāsvādanaṃ || anenāntarāyacatuṣṭayena rahitaṃ cittaṃ nirvātadīpavad acalaṃ sad akhaṇḍacaitanyānandamātram avatiṣṭhate yadā tadā rājayogaḥ sampadyate ||40|| [approx. Vedāntasāra 213]

tad uktam-

laye sambodhayec cittaṃ vikṣiptaṃ śamayet punaḥ ||
sakaṣāyaṃ vijānīyāt samaprāptaṃ na cālayet ||41|| [Gauḍapāda's Kārika 3.44] 
nāsvādayedrasaṃ tatra niḥsaṅgaḥ prajñayā bhavet | 
yatha dīpo nivātastho neṅgate sopamāsmṛutā ||42|| iti ca ||[GK 3.45]  [also quoted in the Vedāntasāra]
[p.7]
pātañjale 'pi - [1.30-32, 23, 25, 27, 28, 29]

vyādhistyānasaṃśayapramādālasyāviratibhrāntidarśanālabdhabhūmikatvāni vikṣepās te 'ntarāyāḥ || duḥkhadaurmanasyāṅgamejayatvaśvāsapraśvāsā vikṣepasahabhuvaḥ || tatpratiṣedhārtham ekatattvābhyāsaḥ || īśvarapraṇidhānād vā || tatra niratiśayaṃ sarvajñabījam || tasya vācakaḥ praṇavo vācya īśvaraḥ || praṇavasya tajjapas tadarthabhāvanaṃ ca|| tataḥ pratyakcetanādhigamo 'py antarāyābhāvaś ceti ||

gītāpi - [6.24-32]

saṃkalpaprabhavān kāmān tyaktvā sarvān aśeṣataḥ |
manasaivendriyagrāmaṃ viniyamya samantataḥ ||44||

śanaiḥ śanair uparamed buddhyā dhṛtigṛhītayā
ātmasaṃsthaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā na kiṃcid api cintayet ||45||

yato yato niścarati manaś cañcalam asthiram
tatas tato niyamyaitad ātmany eva vaśaṃ nayet ||46||

praśāntamanasaṃ hy enaṃ yoginaṃ sukham uttamam
upaiti śāntarajasaṃ brahmabhūtam akalmaṣam ||47||

evaṃ yuñjan sadātmānaṃ yogī vigatakalmaṣaḥ
sukhena brahmasaṃsparśam atyantaṃ sukham aśnute ||48||

sarvabhūtastham ātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni cātmani
īkṣate yogayuktātmā sarvatra samadarśanaḥ ||49||

yo māṃ paśyati sarvatra sarvaṃ ca mayi paśyati
tasyāhaṃ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati ||50||

[p.8]
sarvabhūtasthitaṃ yo māṃ bhajaty ekatvam āsthitaḥ
sarvathā vartamāno 'pi sa yogī mayi vartate ||51||

ātmaupamyena sarvatra samaṃ paśyati yo 'rjuna
sukhaṃ vā yadi vā duḥkhaṃ sa yogī paramo mataḥ ||52|| ityādi ||

śrutirapi - [Br.a.upaniṣad 4.4.23]
tad etad ṛcābhyuktam - 
eṣa nityo mahimā brāhmaṇasya na karmaṇā vardhate no kanīyān tasyaivātmā || padavittaṃ viditvā na lipyate karmaṇā pāpakeneti ||53||

tasmād evaṃ vidic chānto dānta uparatas titikṣuḥ śraddhāvitto bhūtvātmany evātmāni paśyet sarvam evam paśyet sarvasyātmā bhavati sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ tarati | nainaṃ pāpmā tarati | sarvaṃ pāpmānaṃ nayati | nainaṃ pāpmā nayati | vipāpo virajo 'vijivitso 'pipāso brāhmaṇo bhavati ya evaṃ veda iti ||54||

yogarājam adhikṛtya kulārṇave - śrīśiva uvāca - 

atha yogaṃ pravakṣyāmi sākṣātsaṃvitpradāyakam |
jīvātmābhedayuktena manasā paramātmanaḥ ||55||

yogo yogas tu vijñeyo devatādhyānato bhavet ||
dhyānaṃ tu dvividhaṃ proktaṃ sthūlasūkṣmavibhedataḥ ||56|| [from here onwards, approx Kulārnava 9.3-48 

sākāraṃ sthūlam ity uktaṃ nirākāraṃ tu sūkṣmakam |
sthairyārthaṃ manasaḥ ke cit sthūlaṃ dhyānaṃ pracakṣate ||57|| 

sthūle cen niścalaṃ ceto bhavet sūkṣme tu susthitam |
karapādodarākṣyādirahitaṃ parameśvaram ||58||

[p.9]
sarvatejomayaṃ dhyāyet saccidānandalakṣaṇam |
nodeti nāstam abhyeti na vṛddhiṃ yāti na kṣayam ||59|| 

svayaṃ vibhāty athānyāni bhāsayet sādhanaṃ vinā |
anastamitaṃ bhārūpaṃ sattāmātram agocaram |
vacasām ātmasamvedyaṃ taj jñānaṃ brahmasaṃjñakam ||60||

praṇaṣṭavāyusañcāraḥ pāṣāṇa iva niścalaḥ | 
parajīvaikyamarmajño yogī yogavid ucyate ||61|| [parajīvaikadhāmajño?]

yadarthamātranirbhāsaṃ stimitodadhivat sthitam | 
svarūpaśūnyaṃ dhyānaṃ yat tat samādhir vidhīyate ||62||

nakiñciccintanād eva svayaṃ tattvaṃ prakāśate | 
svayaṃ prakāśite tattve tatkṣaṇāt tanmayo bhavet ||63||

svapnajāgradavasthāyāṃ suptavadyo 'vatiṣṭhate | 
niśvāsocchvāsahīnastu niścitaṃ mukta eva saḥ ||64||

niṣpandakaraṇagrāmaḥ svātmalīnamanolayaḥ | [svātmalīnamano'nilaḥ?]
ya āste mṛtavat sākṣāj jīvanmuktaḥ sa ucyate ||65||

na śṛṇoti na vā ghrāti na trasyati na paśyati | [spṛśati?]
na jānāti sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ na saṅkalpayate manaḥ ||66||

na cābhimanyate kiñcinna ca budhyati kāṣṭhavat | 
evaṃ śive vilīnātmā samādhistha ihocyate ||67||

[p.10]
yathā jale jalaṃ kṣiptaṃ kṣīre kṣīraṃ ghṛte ghṛtam | 
aviśeṣo bhavet tadvaj jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ ||68||

yathā dhyānasya sāmarthyāt kīṭako bhramarāyate | 
tathā samādhisāmarthyād brahmarūpo bhavennaraḥ ||69||

kṣīrodbhūtaṃ ghṛtaṃ yadvat tatra kṣiptaṃ na pūrvavat | 
pṛthagbhūto guṇābhyāsyād ātmā tadvad ihāpi ca ||70|| [guṇebhyaḥ syād?]

yathā gāḍhāndhakārastho na kiñcidapi paśyati | 
amanaskas tathā yogī prapañcaṃ naiva paśyati ||71||

yathā nimīlane kāle prapañcaṃ naiva paśyati | 
tathaivonmīlane 'pi  syāccaitat dhyānasya lakṣaṇam ||72||

janaḥ svadehakaṇḍūtiṃ vijānāti yathā tathā | 
parabrahmasvarūpī ca vetti viśvaṃ viceṣṭitam ||73||

vidite tu pare tattve varṇātīte hy avikriye | 
kiṅkaratvaṃ hi gacchanti mantrā mantrādhipaiḥ saha ||74||

ātmaikabhāvaniṣṭhasya yā yā ceṣṭā tad arcanam | 
yo yo jalpaḥ sa tan mantras tad dhyānaṃ yan nirīkṣaṇam ||75|| [sanmantras?]

dehābhimāne galite vijñāte paramātmani | 
yatra yatra mano yāti tatra tatra samādhayaḥ ||76||

bhidyate hṛdayagranthiś chidyante sarvasaṃśayāḥ | 
kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi tasmin dṛṣṭe parāvare ||77||

[p.11]
yogīndreṇa yadā prāptaṃ  nirmalaṃ paramaṃ padam | 
devāsurapadaṃ yat tat prāptaṃ cātiricyate ||78||

yaḥ paśyet sarvagaṃ śāntam ānandamātmānam avyayam | 
na tasya kiñ cid āptavyaṃ jñātavyaṃ cāvaśiṣyate ||79||

pare brahmaṇi vijñāte samastair niyamair alam | 
tālavṛntena kiṃ kāryaṃ labdhe malayamārute ||80||

āśikhābandhanaṃ nāsti nāsikābandhanaṃ na hi | [āsikābandhanaṃ?]
na yamo niyamo nāsti svayam ātmani paśyatām ||81||

kṣaṇaṃ brahmāham asmīti yaḥ kuryād ātmacintanam | 
tat sarvaṃ pātakaṃ  hanyāt tamaḥ  sūryodayo yathā ||82||

vratakratustapastīrthadānadevārcanādiṣu	 | 
yat phalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ tad avāpnoti tattvavit	 ||83||

uttamā sahajāvasthā madhyamā dhyānadhāraṇā | 
japastutiḥ syād adhamā homapūjā 'dhamādhamā ||84||

uttamā tattvacintā syāj japacintā tu madhyamā | 
śāstracintā 'dhamā jñeyā lokacintā 'dhamādhamā ||85||

pūjākoṭiphalaṃ stotraṃ stotrakoṭiphalo japaḥ | 
japakoṭiphalaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyānakoṭiphalo layaḥ 
layāt parataraṃ nāsti sā kāṣṭhā sā parā kalā ||86||
p.12
na hi nādāt paro mantro na devaḥ svātmanaḥ paraḥ | 
nānusandheḥ parā pūjā na hi tṛpteḥ paraṃ phalam || 87 ||

mantrodakair vinā sandhyāṃ japahomair vinā tapaḥ | 
upacārair vinā pūjāṃ yogī nityaṃ samācaret ||88||

deho devālayo prokto  jīvo devaḥ sadāśivaḥ | 
tyajed ajñānanirmālyaṃ so 'hambhāvena bhāvayet ||89||

jīvaḥ śivaḥ śivo jīvaḥ sa jīvaḥ kevalaḥ śivaḥ | 
pāśabaddhaḥ smṛto  jīvaḥ pāśamuktaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 90 ||

agnau tiṣṭhati viprāṇāṃ hṛdi devo manīṣiṇām | 
pratimā svalpabuddhīnāṃ sarvatra viditātmanām || 91 ||

yo nindāstutiśītoṣṇasukhaduḥkhādivastuṣu | 
sama syāt sa tu yogīndro harṣāharṣavivarjītaḥ || 92 ||

nispṛho nityasantuṣṭaḥ samadarśī jitendriyaḥ | 
āste dehe pravāsīva sa yogī paramatvavit || 93 ||

yathā paṅgvandhabadhiraklīvamūkajaḍādayaḥ	 | 
nivasantīha saṃsāre tathā yogī sa tattvavit	 || 94 ||

na vidhir na niṣedhaś ca na puṇyaṃ nāpi pātakam | 
na svargo naiva narako yogināṃ tattvadarśinām || 95 ||

[p.13]
yathā mantrabalopetaḥ krīḍan sarpair na dṛśyate |
tathā na dṛśyate yogī krīḍann indriyapannagaiḥ ||96|| [not in the published Kulārṇava]

sarvayāyī yathā sūryaḥ sarvabhakṣī yathānalaḥ |
yogī bhuktvā 'khilān bhogān tathā pāpair na lipyate ||97||

yathā hasati loko 'yaṃ jugupsati ca kutsati | 
vilokya dūrato yāti tathā yogī pravarttate ||98|| [KA 9.73]

sarvasparśī  yathā  vāyur sarvaṅga ca yathā nabhaḥ | 
sarve yathā nadīsnātās tathā yogī sadā śuciḥ ||99||  [KA 9.77]

yathā grāmagataṃ toyaṃ nadīyuktaṃ bhavec chuci | 
tathā mlecchagṛhānnādi yogihastārpitaṃ śuci ||100||  [KA 9.78]

jñānino 'jñānino vāpi yāvat dehasya dhāraṇā | 
tāvad varṇāśramācāraḥ karttavyaḥ karmamuktaye ||101||   [KA 9.122]

karmaṇonmūlite 'jñāne jñānena śivatāṃ vrajet | 
śivaikyabhāvo muktiḥ syād ataḥ karma samācaret ||102||[KA 9.123-27]

kuryād anindyakarmāṇi  nindyakarmāṇi nācaret | 
ihāmutra sukhākāṅkṣī kaṇṭhasṭhāṇujpi druvaṃ  ||103||

sarvakarmāṇi santyaktuṃ na śakyaṃ dehadhāriṇā | 
tyajet karmaphalaṃ yogī sa tyāgīty abhidhīyate ||104||
[p.14]
svakāryeṣu pravarttante karaṇānīti cintayan | 
ahambhāvam apāsyaiva yaḥ kuryāt sa na lipyate ||105||

kriyamāṇāni karmāṇi jñānaprāpter anantaram | 
na spṛśantīha tattvajñaṃ jalaṃ padmadalaṃ yathā ||106||

bhārate śāntiparvaṇi mokṣadharme - [12.289.11-12,19-21,56-61]

rāgaṃ mohaṃ tathā snehaṃ kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca pañcamam
yogāc chittvā tato doṣān pañcaitān prāpnuvanti tat ||107||

yathaivānimiṣāḥ sthūlāḥ jālaṃ chittvā punar jalam
prāpnuvanti tathā yogāt tat padaṃ vītakalmaṣāḥ ||108||

alpakaś ca yathā rājan vahniḥ śāmyati durbalaḥ
anvito 'pīndhanaiḥ sthūlais tadvad yogo 'balaḥ prabho ||109||

sa eva ca yadā rājan vahnir jātabalaḥ punaḥ
samīraṇasakhaḥ kṣipraṃ dahet kṛtsnāṃ mahīm api ||110||

tadvaj jātabalo yogī dīptatejā mahābalaḥ
antakāla ivādityaḥ kṛtsnaṃ saṃśoṣayej jagat ||111||

yas tu tiṣṭhati kaunteya dhāraṇāsu yathāvidhi
maraṇaṃ janma duḥkhaṃ ca sukhaṃ ca sa vimuñcati ||112||

nānāśāstreṣu niṣpannaṃ yogeṣv idam udāhṛtam
paraṃ yogasya yat kṛtyaṃ niścitaṃ tad dvijātiṣu ||113||

[p.15]
paraṃ hi tad brahmamayaṃ mahātman brahmāṇam īśaṃ varadaṃ ca viṣṇum
bhavaṃ ca dharmaṃ ca ṣaḍānanaṃ ca tān brahmaputrāṃś ca mahānubhāvān||114||

tamaś ca kaṣṭaṃ sumahad rajaś ca; sattvaṃ ca śuddhaṃ prakṛtiṃ parāṃ ca
siddhiṃ ca devīṃ varuṇasya patnīṃ; tejaś ca kṛtsnaṃ sumahac ca dhairyam||115||

tārādhipaṃ vai vimalaṃ satāraṃ; viśvāṃś ca devān uragānśca pitṝṃ
śailāṃś ca kṛtsnān udadhīṃś ca ghorān; nadīś ca sarvāḥ savanāṃś ca nāgān ||116||

sarākṣasān pakṣigaṇān diśaś ca; gandharvasaṃghān puruṣān striyaś ca
parasparaṃ prāpya mahān mahātmā; viśeta yogī na cirād vimuktaḥ ||117||

tathā - (Mahābhārata 12.289.25-41)

na yamo nāntakaḥ kruddho na mṛtyur bhīmavikramaḥ
īśate nṛpate sarve yogasyāmitatejasaḥ ||118||

ātmanāṃ ca sahasrāṇi bahūni bharatarṣabha
yogaḥ kuryād balaṃ prāpya taiś ca sarvair mahīṃ caret ||119||

prāpnuyād viṣayān kaiś cit punaś cograṃ tapaś caret
saṃkṣipec ca punas tāni sūryatejā guṇāni ca ||120||

balasthasya hi yogasya bandhaneśasya pārthiva
vimokṣe prabhaviṣṇutvam ubhayatra na saṃśayaḥ ||121||

balāni yogaprāptāni mayetāni viśāṃ pate
nidarśanārthaṃ sūkṣmāṇi vakṣyāmi ca punas tava ||122||

ātmanaś ca samādhāne dhāraṇāṃ prati vā vibho
apramatto yathā dhanvī lakṣyaṃ hanti samāhitaḥ ||123||

yuktaḥ samyak tathā yogī mokṣaṃ prāpnoty asaṃśayam
snehapūrṇe yathā pātre mana ādhāya niścalam ||124||

puruṣo naktam ārohet sopānaṃ yuktamānasaḥ
yogas tathāyam ātmānaṃ yuktaḥ pārthiva niścalam 
karoty svayamātmānaṃ bhāskaropamadarśanam ||125||

yathā ca nāvaṃ kaunteya karṇadhāraḥ samāhitaḥ
mahārṇavagatāṃ śīghraṃ nayet pārthiva sattama ||126||

tadvad ātmasamādhānaṃ yuktvā yogena tattvavit
durgamaṃ saṃsthānam āpnoti hitvā deham imaṃ nṛpa ||127||

sārathiś ca yathā yuktvā sadaśvān susamāhitaḥ
deśam iṣṭaṃ nayaty āśu dhanvinaṃ puruṣarṣabha||128||
tathaiva nṛpate yogī dhāraṇāsu samāhitaḥ
prāpnoty āśu paraṃ sthānaṃ lakṣaṃ mukta ivāśugaḥ||129||
praveśyātmani cātmānaṃ yogī tiṣṭhati yo 'calaḥ
pāpaṃ hanti ca pūrveṣāṃ padamāpnoti so 'jaram||130||
nābhyāṃ kaṇṭhe ca śīrṣe ca hṛdi vakṣasi pārśvayoḥ
darśane sparśane cāpi ghrāṇe cāmitavikramaḥ||131
P.17
sthāneṣv eteṣu yo yogī mahāvrataparāyaṇaḥ
 ātmanā samyagātmānaṃ sūkṣmaṃ samāhitaḥ||132|
sa śīghram acalaprakhyaṃ karma dagdhvā śubhāśubham
uttamaṃ yogam āsthāya yadīcchati vimucyate ||133||
yudhiṣṭhira uvāca
āhārān kīdṛśān kṛtvā kāni jitvā ca bhārata
yogī balam avāpnoti tad bhavān vaktum arhati||134||
bhīṣma uvāca
kaṇānāṃ bhakṣaṇe yuktaḥ piṇyākasya ca bhārata
snehānāṃ varjane yukto yogī balam avāpnuyāt||135||
bhuñjāno yāvakaṃ rūkṣaṃ dīrghakālam ariṃdama
ekārāmo viśuddhātmā yogī balam avāpnuyāt||136||
pakṣān māsān ṛtūṃś caitān divassān vatsarāṃs tathā
apaḥ pītvā payomiśrā yogī balam avāpnuyāt||137||
akhaṇḍam api vā māsaṃ satataṃ manujeśvara
upoṣya samyak śuddhātmā yogī balam avāpnuyāt||138||
kāmaṃ jitvā tathā krodhaṃ śītoṣṇe varṣam eva ca
sparśaṃ nidrāṃ tathā durjayāṃ  nṛpasattama ||139||
dīpayanti mahātmānaḥ sūkṣmam ātmānam ātmanā
vītarāgā mahāprājñā dhyānādhyayanasaṃpadā ||140||
P.18
durgas tv eṣa mataḥ panthā brāhmaṇānāṃ vipaścitām
na kaś cid vrajati hy asmin kṣemeṇa bharatarṣabha||141||
yathā kaś cid vanaṃ ghoraṃ bahusarpasarīsṛpam
śvabhravat toyahīnaṃ ca durgamaṃ bahukaṇṭakam||142||
yogamārgaṃ tathāso 'pi yaḥ kaś cid vrajate dvijaḥ
kṣemeṇoparamen mārgād bahudoṣo hi sa smṛtaḥ ||143||
sustheyaṃ kṣuradhārāsu niśitāsu mahīpate
dhāraṇāsu tu yogasya duḥstheyam akṛtātmabhiḥ ||144||
vipannā dhāraṇās tāta nayanti na śubhāṃ gatim
netṛhīnā yathā nāvaḥ puruṣān arṇave nṛpa ||145||

tathā (MBh 12.290.54-55)

kāmakrodhau bhayaṃ nidrā pañcamaḥ śvāsa ucyate
ete doṣāḥ śarīreṣu dṛśyante sarvadehinām ||146||

chindanti kṣamayā krodhaṃ kāmaṃ saṃkalpavarjanāt
sattvasaṃsevanānnidrām apramādād bhayaṃ tathā
chindanti pañcamaṃ śvāsaṃ lpāhāratayā nṛpa ||147|| ityādi||

tathā (MBh 12,289.6-8, 12,290.3)

sāṅkhyajñānayogasādhāraṇo ya yogi vicārārthamuktam tatraiva |
śiṣṭānāṃ hi grāhyaṃ tadvidhaiḥ śiṣṭasammataiḥ |
pratyakṣadhenavā yogāḥ sāṅkhy[āḥ] śāstraviniścitāḥ ||148|| [pratyakṣahetavo]

P.19
ubhe caite mate tattve nṛpate śiṣṭasammate |
anuṣṭhite yathāśāstraṃ nayetāṃ paramāṃ gatim || 149||

tulyaṃ śaucaṃ tayor yuktaṃ dayā bhūteṣu cānagha
vratānāṃ dhāraṇaṃ tulyaṃ darśanaṃ ca tayoḥ samaṃ||150||

śṛṇu me tvam idaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sāṅkhyānāṃ viditātmanām |
vihitaṃ yatibhiḥ sarvaiḥ kapilādibhir īśvaraiḥ ||151||

yasmin na vibhramaḥ ke cid dṛśyante manujarṣabha |
guṇāś ca yasmin bahavo doṣahāniś ca kevalā ||152||

jñānena parisaṃkhyāya sadoṣān viṣayān nṛpa
mānuṣān durjayān kṛtsnān paiśācān viṣayāṃs tathā||153||
rākṣasān viṣayān ñātvā yakṣāṃśca viṣayāṃs tathā
rājarviṣayān jñātvā brahmarṣiviṣayāṃs tathā
viṣayānauragān jñātvā gāndharvaviṣayāṃs tathā ||154||
pitṝṇāṃ viṣayān jñātvā tiryakṣu caratāṃ nṛpa
suparṇaviṣayān jñātvā marutāṃ viṣayāṃs tathā ||155||
āsurān viṣayāñ jñātvā vaiśvadevāṃs tathaiva ca
devarṣiviṣayān jñātvā yogānām api ceśvarān ||156||
prajāpatināṃ viṣayān brahmaṇo viṣayāṃstathā ||
āyuṣaś ca paraṃ kālaṃ loke vijñāya tattvāṃ vara||157||
[P.20]
sukhasya ca paraṃ tattvaṃ vijñāya vadatāṃ vara
prāpte kāle ca yad duḥkhaṃ satatāṃ viṣayaiṣiṇām ||158||
tiryak ca patatāṃ duḥkhaṃ patane narake ca yat
svargasya ca guṇān kṛtsnān doṣān sarvāṃś ca bhārata ||159||
vedavāde ca ye doṣā guṇā ye cāpi vaidikāḥ
jñānayoge ca ye doṣā guṇā yoge ca ye nṛpa ||160||
sattvaṃ daśaguṇaṃ jñātvā rajo navaguṇaṃ tathā
tamaś cāṣṭaguṇaṃ jñātvā buddhiṃ saptaguṇāṃ tathā||161||
ṣaḍguṇaṃ ca nabho jñātvā nanbhaḥ pañcaguṇaṃ tathā
buddhiṃ caturguṇāṃ jñātvā tamaś ca triguṇaṃ tathā||162||
dviguṇaṃ ca rajo jñātvā sattvam ekaguṇaṃ punaḥ
mārgaṃ vijñāya tattvena pralaye prekṣaṇaṃ tathā ||163||
jñānavijñānasaṃpannāḥ kāraṇair bhāvitāḥ śubhaiḥ
prāpnuvanti śubhaṃ mokṣaṃ sūkṣmā iha nabhaḥ param ||164||
rūpeṇa dṛṣṭiṃ saṃyuktāṃ ghrāṇaṃ gandhaguṇena ca
śabde saktaṃ tathā śrotraṃ jihvāṃ rasaguṇeṣu ca||165||
tvcaṃ sparśe tathā saktāṃ vāyuṃ nabhasi cāśritam
mohaṃ tamasi saṃsaktaṃ lobham artheṣu saṃśritam||166||
[P.21]
viṣṇuṃ krānte bale śakraṃ koṣṭhe saktaṃ tathānalam
apsu devīṃ tathā samāsaktāmā pas tejasi sāṃśritāḥ||167||
tejo vāyau tu saṃsaktaṃ vāyuṃ nabhasi cāśritam
nabho manasi saṃyuktaṃ mano buddhau ca saṃśritam||168||
rajaḥ sattve tathā saktaṃ sattvaṃ saktaṃ tathātmani
saktam ātmānam īśe ca deve nārāyaṇe tathā ||169||
devaṃ mokṣe ca saṃsaktaṃ mokṣaṃ saktaṃ tu na kva cit
indriyāṇīndriyārthāṃś ca sarvān ātmani saṃśritān||170||
durlabhatvaṃ ca mokṣasya vijñāya śrutipūrvakam
prāṇāpānau samānaṃ ca vyānodānau ca tattvataḥ ||171||
adhaś caivānilaṃ jñātvā pravahaṃ cālitaṃ punaḥ
sapta vātāṃs tathā jñātvā rājarṣīṃśca parantapa ||172||
surarṣīn mahataś cānyān maharṣīn sūryasaṃnibhān
aiśvaryān cyāvitān druṣṭvā kālena mahatā nṛpa||173||
mahatāṃ bhūtasaṃghānāṃ śrutvā nāśaṃ ca pārthiva
gatiṃ cāpy aśubhāṃ jñātvā nṛpate pāpakarmaṇām ||174||
vaitaraṇyāṃ ca yad duḥkhaṃ patitānāṃ yamakṣaye
yonīṣu ca vicitrāsu saṃsārān aśubhāṃs tathā||175||
[P.22]
jaṭhare cāśubhe vāsaṃ śoṇitodakabhājane
śleṣmamūtrapurīṣe ca tīvragandhasamanvite||176||
śukraśoṇitasaṃghāte majjāsnāyuparigrahe
sirāśatasamākīrṇe navadvāre pure 'śucau tathā ||177||
vijñāyāhitam ātmānaṃ yogāṃś ca vividhān nṛpa
tāmasānāṃ ca jantūnāṃ ramaṇīy hatātmanām ||178||
upadravāṃs tathā ghorāñ śaśinas tejasas tathā
tārāṇāṃ patanaṃ dṛṣṭvā nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca paryayam ||179||
dvaṃdvānāṃ viprayogaṃ ca vijñāya kṛpaṇaṃ nṛpa
anyonyabhakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhūtānām api cāśubham ||180||
bālye mohaṃ ca vijñāya kṣayaṃ dehasya cāśubham
rāge mohe ca saṃprāpte kva cit sattvaṃ samāśritam ||181||
sahasreṣu naraḥ kaś cin mokṣabuddhiṃ samāśritaḥ
durlabhatvaṃ ca mokṣasya vijñāya śrutipūrvakam ||182||
viṣayāṇāṃ ca daurātmyaṃ vijñāya nṛpate punaḥ
gatāsūnāṃ ca kaunteya dehān dṛṣṭvā tathāśubhān ||183||
vāsaṃ kuleṣu jantūnāṃ duḥkhaṃ vijñāya bhārata
brahmaghnānāṃ gatiṃ jñātvā patitānāṃ sudāruṇām ||184||
surāpāne ca saktānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ durātmanām
gurudāraprasaktānāṃ gatiṃ vijñāya cāśubhān||185||
sadevakeṣu lokeṣu vartanti yena mānavāḥ
tena jñānena vijñāya gatiṃ cāśubhakarmaṇām||186||
vṛddhiṃ dṛṣṭvā samudrāṇāṃ kṣayaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathā punaḥ
kṣayaṃ dhanānāṃ ca punar vṛddhiṃ tathaiva ca||187||
saṃyogānāṃ kṣayaṃ dṛṣṭvā yugānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ
jarāmṛtyuṃ tathā janma dṛṣṭvā duḥkhāni caiva hi||188|| 
dehadoṣāṃs tathā jñātvā teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ ca tattvataḥ
ātmadoṣāṃstatha jñātvā sarvān ātmani saṃśritān
svadehād utthitān jñātvā sāṅkhyayogau samāśrayed iti ||189||

tathā (MBh 12.290.57 - 75)
apāṃ phenodbhavaṃ lokaṃ viṣṇor māyāśatāvṛtam |
cittabhittipratīkāśaṃ gasāram anarthakam ||190||
tamaḥ śvabhranibhaṃ dṛṣṭvā varṣabudbudasannibham
nāśaprāyaṃ sukhād dhīnaṃ nāśottaram abhāvaśam ||191||
rajas tamasi saṃmagnaṃ paṅke dvipam ivāvaśam
sāṃkhyā rājan mahāprājñās tyaktvā snehaṃ prajākṛtam ||192||
jñānajñeyena sāṃkhyena vyāpinā mahatā nṛpa
rājasān aśubhān gandhāṃs tāmasāṃś ca tathāvidhān ||193||
puṇyāṃś ca sāttvikān gandhān sparśajān dehasaṃbhān
chittvāśu jñānaśastreṇa tapodaṇḍena bhārata ||194||
tato duḥkhodakaṃ ghoraṃ cintāśokamahāhradam
vyādhimṛtyumahāgrāhaṃ mahābhayamahoragam ||195||
tamaḥ kūrmaṃ rajo mīnaṃ prajñayā saṃtaraṃ punaḥ
snehapaṅkaṃ jarādurgaṃ jñānadīpam ariṃdama ||196||
karmāgādhaṃ satyatīraṃ sthitavratamar indaṃ 
hiṃsāśīghramahāvegaṃ nānārasasamaākaram ||197||
nānāprītimahāratnaṃ duḥkhajvarasamīraṇam
śokatṛṣṇāmahāvartaṃ tīvravyadhimahāgajam ||198||
asthisaṃghātasaṃghāṭṭaṃ śleṣmaphenam arindama
dānamuktākaraṃ bhīmaṃ śoṇitahradavidrumam||199||
hasitotkruṣṭanirghoṣaṃ nānājñānasudustaram
rodanāśrumalakṣāraṃ saṅgatyāgaparāyaṇam ||200||
ahiṃsāsatyamaryādaṃ prāṇatyāgamahormmiṇam 
vedāntagamanadvīpaṃ sarvabhūtadayodadhim ||201||
mokṣadurlābaviṣayaṃ vaḍavāmukhasāgaram
taranti yatayaḥ siddhāḥ jñānayonena bhārata ||202||
tīrtvā ca dustaraṃ janma viśanti vimalaṃ nabhaḥ
tatas tān satkṛtān sāṃkhyān sūryo vahati raśmibhiḥ ||203||
padmatantuvad āviśritya pravahan viṣayān nṛpa
tatra tān pravaho vāyuḥ pratigṛhṇāti bhārata ||204||
vītarāgān yatīn siddhān vīryayuktāṃs tapodhanān
sūkṣmaḥ śītaḥ sugandhī ca sukhasparśaś ca bhārata||205||
saptānāṃ marutāṃ śreṣṭho lokān gacchati yaḥ śubhān
sa tān vahati kaunteya nabhasaḥ paramāṃ gatim ||206||
nabho vahati rājendra tamasaḥ paramāṃ gatim
tamo vahati lokeśa rajasaḥ paramāṃ gatim ||207||
rajo vahati rājendra sattvasya paramāṃ gatim
sattvaṃ vahati śuddhātman paraṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prabhum ||208||0
prabhur vahati śuddhātmā paramātmagatiṃ parām |
satyārjavaratānāṃ vai sarvabhūtadayāvatām ||209||

tad ayaṃ sakalaśrutipurāṇetihāsayogaśāstrasiddho 'rthaḥ | sati sambhave sukhāsane samupaviśya yathā tathā vā sthito vītarāgadveṣakrodhalobhamohanidrālajjābhayādidoṣaḥ sakaladikkālavarttini caitanyānandasvarūpe brahmaṇi śravaṇamanananididhyāsanakrameṇa mano dhārayet || 210||

tac ca mano yadi prācīnakāmakrodhādivāsanāvaśāt tattadanurūpaṃ viṣayaṃ vrajati | tadā taddoṣānusandhānabrahmamanodhāraṇaguṇānusandhānābhyāṃ tatas tat samākṛṣya yatnato brahmaṇy avasthāpayet ||211||

evaṃ cābhyāse kriyamāṇe duritadurvāsanādau ca kṣīyamāṇe prasannībhavati bhagavati  śrīpuruṣottame tathā brahmasvarūpaḥ paramānandaviśeṣaḥ prādurbhavati yathā manaḥ svayam eva prāptamahānidhir daridra iva sthirībhavati cāsmin mahāyogasiddhayaḥ prādurbhavanti ||212||

uktāś ca – tās tadanurūpadhāraṇāviśeṣajanyā[ḥ] siddhisaubhāgyavatyām [ḥ] || tāś ca durlabhā api pareṣāṃ yogināṃ mahāyogeśvaraprasādād atyantaṃ sulabhāḥ ||213||

atyāsaṅgenopabhujyamānā nirvikalpakabrahmasākṣātkārātmakākhilayogamahāphale 'ntarāyā bhavantīti | brahmāsaktamanobhir evendrajālādivat kautukitayā mahāyogibhir vibhāvanīyā ity alaṃ pallavena ||214||

tathā ca gorakṣanāthaḥ – [Vivekamārtaṇda 1]
etad vimuktisopānam etat kālasya vañcanam |
yad vyāvṛttaṃ mano bhogād āsaktaṃ paramātmani ||215||

bhāgavate bhagavān uddhavam uvāca –
janmauṣadhitapomantrair yāvatīr iha siddhayaḥ |
yogenāpnoti tāḥ sarvāḥ nānyair yogagatiṃ vrajet | 216|| [bhā.pu. 11.15.34]

antarāyān vadanty etān yogasiddhiṣu yoginaḥ |
mayi sampadyamānasya kālakṣepaṇahetavaḥ || 217|| [approx. bhā.pu. 11.15.33]

pātañjale 'pi – [3.51]
sthānyupanimantraṇe vismayākaraṇam ityādi || sthānināṃ tattatsthānādhikāriṇāṃ devādīnām ihāgamyatām ihāramyatām iha vaḥ puṇyopārjitā srakcandanavanitādirūpābhogasāmagrīty evaṃ rūpe upanimantraṇe vismayo 'bhimāno na kartavyaḥ ||218||

kintu svasti vo 'stu vayaṃ svakāryaṃ sādhayāma ity abhidhāya brahmamanodhāraṇātmakaṃ svakāryam eva kāryam iti bhāvaḥ || ayaṃ ca yogaḥ saṅkalpavikalpatyāgāt sambhavati || tattyāgo 'haṃkāratyāgād utpadyate iti ||219||

ahaṃkāro nirūpyate –
ayam ahamityādyadhyavasāyo 'haṃkāraḥ | sa ca caturdhā || dehamātraviṣayako dehāntavarttisūkṃacaitanyaviṣayakaḥ || sarvaviṣayakaḥ || śūnyaviṣayakaś ceti ||220||

tatra mātṛpitrādisambandhakatayā yo rāgādidvārā saṃsārasampādakaḥ | sāttvikarājasatāmasabhedā mātṛpitrādisambandhakatayā trividhatayā trividho 'pi mumukṣubhis tyājyaḥ saṅkalpādijanakatvāt ||221||

anye ca trayaḥ sukhaprakāśasampādakatayā sāttvikā eva || vilakṣaṇasambandhaviṣayakatvena rāgadveṣādyajanakatayā aiśvaryasampādanapūrvakamokṣahetutayā ca || tathā mumukṣubhir abhyāsataḥ sampādanīyāḥ ||222||

tad uktaṃ yogavāsiṣṭhe – [5.17.14-15]
āpādamastakam ahaṃ pitṛmātṛvinirmitaḥ |
ity eko niścayo rāma bandhāyāsadvilokanāt ||
atītaḥ sarvabhāvebhyo bālāgrād apy ahaṃ tanuḥ || iti ||223||
dvitīyo mokṣāya niścayo jāyate satām ||

naiyyāyikādīnām || 
jagajjālapadārthātmā sarvam evāham akṣayaḥ || 
tṛtīyo niścayo 'py evaṃ mokṣāyaiva raghūttama ||224|| [=yogavāsiṣṭha 5.17.16]

yathāsmadgurūṇām –
jagad asad idaṃ śūnyaṃ śūnyamāyopamaṃ sadā |
evam eva caturtho 'pi niścayo yogasiddhaye ||225|| [=yogavāsiṣṭha 5.17]

tathā śūnyayoginām –
eteṣāṃ prathamaḥ proktaḥ tṛṣṇayā bandhayogyayā |
śuddhatṛṣṇās trayaḥ svacchā jīvanmuktavilāsinaḥ ||226|| [=yogavāsiṣṭha 5.17]

sarvamatmāhameveti niścayo yo mahāmate |
tam ādāya vivādāya na bhūyo yāti me matiḥ ||227|| viṣādāya na bhūyo jāyate

śūnyaprakṛtir māyeti brahmavijñānam ity api | 
śivaḥ puruṣa īśāno nityam ātmeti kathyate ||228||

dvaitādvaitasamudbhedair jagannirmāṇalīlayā |
paramātmamayī śaktir advaitaiva vijṛmbhate ||229||

sarvātītapadālambī pūrṇenduśaśirāśayaḥ | paripūrṇaikacinmayaḥ
nodvegī na ca tuṣṭātmā saṃsāre nāvasīdati ||230||

prathamāhaṃkāratyāgo 'pi dvividhaḥ – dhyeyo jñeyaś ca || tatra mama dehādinā padārthe na samaṃ kaś cit sambandhaviśeṣa iti bhāvite sati rāgadveṣarahitayā buddhyā līlayaiva kriyāḥ kurvato yo vāsanāviśeṣarūpāhaṃkāratyāgaḥ sa dhyeyanāmakaḥ ||231||

jīvanmuktir iti cocyate || yathā śrīrāmajanakavasiṣṭhaśukādīnāṃ bhedarahitatayā buddhyā yaṃ vāsanātyāgaṃ kṛtvā sthūlasūkṣmakāraṇabhedena trividhaṃ śarīraṃ vismṛtya yogī jahāti sa jñeyanāmakaḥ paramamuktir ity ucyate ||232||

tad uktaṃ tatraiva – [=yogavāsiṣṭha 5.16]
aham eṣā[ṃ] padārthānām ete ca mama jīvitam || 
nāham ebhir vinā kaś cin na mayaite vinā kila ||233|| 

itthaṃ taṃ niścayaṃ tyaktvā vicārya manasā saha |
nāhaṃ padārthasya na me padārtha iti bhāvite ||234||

antaḥśītalayā buddhyā kurvato līlayā kriyāḥ |
yo nūnaṃ vāsanātyāgo dhyeyo nāma sa kīrtitaḥ ||235||

sarvaṃ samastayā buddhyā yaṃ kṛtvā vāsanākṣayam |
jahāti nirmanā dehaṃ jñeyo 'sau vāsanākṣayaḥ ||236||

ahaṅkāramayīṃ tyaktvā vāsanāṃ līlayaiva yaḥ |
tiṣṭhati dhyeyasantyāgī sa jīvanmukta ucyate ||237||

nirmūlakalatāṃ tyaktvā vāsanāṃ yaẖ śamaṃ gataḥ |
jñeyatyāgamayaṃ viddhi muktaṃ taṃ raghunandana ||238||

dvāv etau rāghava tyāgau samau muktapade sthitau  |
dvāv eva brahmatāṃ yātau dvāv eva vigatajvarau ||239||

āpatatsu yathākālaṃ sukhaduẖkheṣv anāratam |
na hṛṣyati mlāyati yaḥ sa mukta iti kathyate ||240||

evaṃ prathamāhaṃkārasya dhyeyanāmakaṃ tyāgaṃ kṛtvā 'parāhaṃkāratritayam abhyaset || śuddhāṃ cittam [śuddhārcitaṃ] eva vā sarvatra sarvadā sarvarūpeṇa vibhāvayet || yataś cid evāhaṃkārāvacchinnā jīvātmarūpā ||241||

anavacchinnā paramātmarūpā kim bahunā saiva ghaṭādyākārākāritāntaḥkaraṇādyavacchinā ghaṭādirūpaprapañcātmikā || avidyāmāyādipadābhidheyā tathābhūtavṛttyanavicchinnā vidyātattvasākṣātkārarājayogamuktyādipadābhidheyā ||242||

tad uktaṃ vāsiṣṭhe –
 ko 'haṃ kas tvaṃ ko vā jagadrūpeti puṣṭena baliṃ prati śukreṇa –
 viddhi haikāsti cinmātram idaṃ cinmayam eva ca | [cid ihāsti hi]
 cit tvaṃ cid aham ete ca lokāś cid iti saṅgrahaḥ ||243|| [5.26.11]
 
bhavyo 'si cet tad etasmāt sarvam āpnoṣi niścayāt |
no cet tad vahv api proktaṃ tvayi bhasmani hūyate ||244||{26.13}

ciccetyakalanā bandhas tanmuktir muktir ucyate |
cid acetyā kilātmeti eṣa siddhāntasaṅgrahaḥ ||245||

evaṃ niścayam ādāya vilokaya dhiyeddhayā |
svayam evātmanātmānam anantaṃ padam āpsyasi ||246||

cet yo ghaṭādiḥ || tathā ca yathā suvarṇakuṇḍalādau suvarṇam || mṛdghaṭādau mṛttikā || gaṅgātaraṅgādau gaṅgāpravāhaḥ || tathā sarvatropādānakāraṇatādinā cid bhāvanīyā ca ||247||

yady api sarvasyaiva vilokanaṃ cidvilokanam eva cidaṃ vinā 'nyasyālīkatvāt || tathāpi cidaṃ vilokayāmi ity anuvyavasāyābhyāse tātparyaṃ anuvyavasāyasyaiva vyavasāyaviśeṣasākṣitvāt ||248||

na hi rajataraṅgatvādinā paśyato 'pi rajataṃ paśyāmīty anuvyavasāyānarho rajatasākṣātkāro rajatārthitājanyapravṛttyādiphalāyālam || tasmāt tathābhyasanīyam || yathā bhavati yasya kasyāpi vilokane  cidaṃ vilokayāmīty anuvyasāya ity eṣā dik ||249||

atha kathañ cid viruddhakāṭhinyādidharmavatvena dṛśyamānasya pṛthivyādiprapañcasya rūpasparśasaṃyogādirahitaśuddhacidrūpateti cet ucyate sarvasyaiva prapañcasya svāpnikaprapañcasyaiva cidrūpatā || citsvarūpabrahmavivarttatvāt ||250||

tad uktaṃ vāsiṣṭhe –
avidyamānam evedaṃ vidyamānam iva sthitam ||
ābhāsamātraṃ trijagan nānyad asti munīśvara ||251|| [6.45.27-

yo 'sau brahmādiśabdārthaḥ saṃvidam viddhi kevalam ||
sā vedyam avagacchantī yāti cinnamayogyatām ||252||

kṣaṇād bhāvitavedyatvād ahantām anutiaṣṭhati |
pumān bhāvanayā svapne navavāraṇatām iva ||253||

asyā ahantārūpāyā deśatāṃ kālatāṃ gatāḥ |
sampatsyante svayaṃ śūnyarūpiṇyaḥ sakhya eva tāḥ ||254||

tābhiḥ sarvānvitā saiva sattā jīvābhidhātmikā |
bhūtvā buddhitvam āyāti nityam ajñapade sthite || iti ||255||

evaṃ cid eva saṅkalpādiviṣayatāviśiṣṭamanaḥ | ahantādiviṣayatāviśiṣṭāhaṃkāraḥ || niścayātmakaviṣayatāviśiṣṭā buddhiḥ || āntaraviṣayakanirvikalpakasmṛtyādiviṣayatāviśiṣṭam cittam ||256||

netragolakādidvārabahirgatarūpādyākārākāritāntaḥkaraṇavṛttiviśiṣṭācakṣuḥ || rūpādivāsanayā rūpāditām āpannam ||257||

rūpādivaśīkṛtatamoguṇaviśiṣṭo rudraḥ || vaśīkṛtarajoviśiṣṭo brahmā || vaśīkṛtasattvaviśiṣṭo viṣṇuḥ || vaśīkṛtaguṇaviśiṣṭo īśvaraḥ || guṇātītaṃ brahma || saiva vāsudevasadāśivādipadavācyam || avaśīkṛtatamoguṇādyardito jīvaḥ ||258||

sarvasya traiguṇye 'pi prādhānyam adhīkṛtya tamoguṇādyekaikavyapadeśaḥ || prādhānyena vyapadeśā bhavantīti nyāyāt ||259|| read adhikṛtya

indro 'hamityādyavadhāraṇātmakāhaṃkāraviśiṣṭaḥ indrādiḥ || ata eva yogibhir vartamānāhaṃkāraviparītāhaṃkārāvadhāraṇābhyāsāt tattadahaṃkārānurūpaṃ devatvādi prāpyate ||260|| 

tathā ca bṛhadāraṇyakopaniṣadi –

brahma vā idam agra āsīt || tam ātmānam eva vedāhaṃ brahmāsmīti |
tasmāt tat sarvam abhavat |
tad yo yo devānāṃ pratyabudhyata sa eva tad abhavat |
tatharṣīnām | tathā manuṣyāṇām | tad dhai taddhai tat paśyann ṛṣir vāmadevaḥ pratipede ||
ahaṃ manur abhavaṃ sūryaś ceti | tad idam apy etarhi ya evaṃ vidāhaṃ brahmāsmīti sa idaṃ sarvaṃ bhavati |
tasya ha devāś ca tā bhūtyā īśate |
ātmā hy eṣāṃ sa bhavati yā yonyā devatām upāste 'nyo 'sāv abhyo 'ham asmīti na sa veda |
yathā paśur ityādi || tathā tan naiva [BrhUp_1,4.10]

sa vāyam ātmā brahma || vijñānamayo manovāṅmayo prāṇamayaś cakṣurmayaḥ śrotramayo ākāśamayo vāyumayastejomaya āpomayaḥ pṛthivīmayaḥ  krodhamayo 'krodhamayo dharmamayo harṣamayo'harṣamayoḥ dharmamayo'dharmamayaḥ sarvamayastadedaṃmayo iti yathācārī bhavati || 

sādhukārī sādhur bhavati pāpakārī pāpo bhavati | puṇyaḥ puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati pāpaḥ pāpenetyādi || 261|| [BrhUp_4,4.5]
P.33
bhārate mokṣadharme śukānupraśne – [MBh 12,240.003-8ab]
buddhir ātmā manuṣyasya buddhir evātmanātmani ||
yadā vikurute bhāvaṃ tadā bhavati sā manaḥ ||262||

indriyāṇāṃ pṛthagbhāvād buddhir vikriyate svataḥ
śṛṇvatī bhavati śrotraṃ spṛśatī sparśa ucyate ||263||

paśyantī bhavate dṛṣṭī rasatī rasanā smṛtā ||
jighratī bhavati ghrāṇaṃ buddhir vikriyate pṛthak ||264||

indriyāṇīti tu tāny āhur teṣv adarśo hi tiṣṭhati ||
tiṣṭhatī puruṣe buddhis triṣu bhāveṣu vartate ||265||

kadā cil labhate śāntiṃ kadā cid api śocate
na sukhena na duḥkhena kadā cid iha yujyate 
seyaṃ guṇātmikā bhāvāṃs trīn etān ativartate ||266||

tasmāt sarvaṃ cidānandasvarūpaṃ brahmety eva bhāvanīyam || etat padāvadhāraṇam eva yogeśānāṃ devapūjā ||267||

tathā ca vāśiṣṭhe vasiṣṭhaṃ pratipṛṣṭaḥ – [6.43.45]
śrīśiva uvāca – 
sarvadā sarvarūpeṇa yat sarvatra sthitaṃ ca yat ||
sarvaṃ brahmeti niścitya nityāmā vā vrataṃ caret ||268|| nityātmārcāvrataṃ

sa mayā sarvathā buddhyā cinmātraṃ devacinmayam ||
yathāprāptam api prāptaṃ sarvārthena prapūjayate ||269||?

manāg api na kartavyo yatno 'tra pūrvavastūni ||
na vāñchatā na tyajatā jñānaprāptena tattvataḥ || 270||?

saritaḥ sāgareṇaiva bhoktavyā bhogabhūmayaḥ || = 6.43.47cd
samatā madhurā rasyā rasaśaktir atīndriyā |
tayā tad bhāvitaṃ ceto tanmayatvaṃ hi gacchati  ||271|| =6.43.52

etac ca jñānam 1. śubhecchā 2. vicāraṇā 3. tanumānasā 4. sattvāpattiḥ 5. asaṃsaktiḥ 6. padārthābhāvanā 7. turyagā nāmakaṃ saptabhūmikaṃ ||272||

tatra tam ete vedānuvacanena brāhmaṇā vividiṣanti || yajñena dānena tapasānāśakenetyādi brahmapratipāditā brahmajñānecchā ādyā ||273||BrhUp_4,4.22||

adhyātmajñānavicāraḥ || sadācārapravṛttihetur iti dvitīyā ||274||

yasyām avasthāyāṃ śubhecchāvicāraṇābhyāṃ indriyārtheṣu viraktatā udeti sā tanumānasā tṛtīyā rāgādyabhāvenālpasaṅkalpavikalpakatvāt ||275||

etad bhūmikātrayaṃ brahmavidyāyāḥ sādhanamātraṃ na tu brahmajñānāntarbhūtaṃ bhedasatyatā buddher api naṣṭatvāt ||276||

ata evaitat trayaṃ yogāvasthācatuṣṭaye jāgrad ity ucyate || vyavahāraprayojakatvāt || jāgratīva jagadbhedasatyatā buddheḥ sattvāt ca || etat tritayābhyāsāt svarūpamātrāvasthitaḥ sattvāpattināmnī caturthī ||277||

tad uktam – [Yogavāsiṣṭha 6.154.1]
sattāvaśeṣa evāste caturthī bhūmikāṃ gatā iti || eṣā ca brahmajñānāntarbhūtā ||278||

tad uktam – [Yogavāsiṣṭha 6.154.1-2]
samyakjñānodaye citte pūrṇacandrodayopame ||
nirvikalpakam anādyantaṃ yogino yuktacetasaḥ ||
samaṃ sarvaṃ prapaśyanti caturthībhūmikāṃ gatā ||iti||279||

eṣā svapnāvasthā || prapañcabhedasya svāpnikavad avabhāsāt || etac catuṣṭayābhyāsād yasyām avasthāyāṃ paramasūkṣmabrahmānandagrahaṇavaidagdhyarūpaś cittasya camatkāro 'bhyudeti sā asaṃsaktināmnī pañcamī ||280|| 

prapañcāsaṃsakter eva brahmodbodhakatvāt tadā tadvaidagdhyam || tac ca prātibhāsikaviṣayeṣv api rāgābhyāso nivṛttiphalaka ityādy anusandhānam || eṣāvasthā prapañcasyājñānena suṣuptir ucyate ||281||

tad uktam –  [Yogavāsiṣṭha 6.154.1-2]
pañcamī[ṃ] bhūmikām etya suṣuptipadanāmikām ||
śāntāśeṣaviśeṣāṃśas tiṣṭhaty advaitamātrakaḥ ||iti|| 282||

etat pañcakābhyāsāt ātmārāmatayā bāhyānāmābhyantarāṇāṃ ca padārthānāṃ paraprayatnaṃ vinā asphuraṇaṃ padārthābhāvanānāmnī ṣaṣṭhī || eṣā padārthānām atyantabhāvanāt gūḍasuṣuptyākhyā ṣaṣṭhī || 283||

tad uktam – [Yogavāsiṣṭha 6.155.1]
ṣaṣṭhīṃ gūḍasuṣuptyākhyāṃ kramāt patati bhūmikām ||
yatra nāsan na sadrūpo nāhaṃ nāpy anahaṅkṛtiḥ ||284||

yasyām avasthāyāṃ bhūmiṣaḍkābhyāsāt svabhāvaikaniṣṭhatvena svataḥ parataḥ vā samādhyapagamo na bhavati sā turyagā bhūmiḥ saptamī ||285||

proktajāgradādyapekṣayā ca turīyatvād eṣā jīvanmuktavasthā || videhamuktis turyātītā || iti jñānabhūmikāsaptakam ||286||

athaitad virodhitvena heyatvād ajñānabhūmikā saptakam abhidhīyate || sakaladikkālavarti caitanyānandasvarūpasyātmanaḥ svarūpeṇāvasthānaṃ muktis taddhetubhūtaṃ svasvarūpajñānaṃ jñānam ity ucyate ||287||

tad virodhisvarūpabhraṃśalakṣaṇabandhahetuḥ dehātmabuddhyādiprāpañcakajñānam ajñānam ity ucyate || tac ca saptadhā || bījajāgrat 1. jāgrat 2. mahājāgrat 3. jāgratsvapna 4. svapnaḥ 5. svapnajāgrat 6. suṣuptibhedāt 7. ||288||

tatra svapnāt prabuddhasya prathamaṃ nayanonmīlanānantaraṃ jātaṃ pravṛttinivṛttyādivyavahārādisamarthaṃ jñānaṃ bījajāgrat ||289||

jñaptisvarūpād brahmaṇo bimbāt pratibimbāyamānasya jīvasyāhantāmamatāghaṭatvādiprakārako vyavahārasamartho bodho jāgrat ||290||

saṃskārādipūrvako jātismaraṇādilakṣaṇo bodho mahājāgrat ||291||

manorthikaprapañcādiviṣayakaṃ jñānaṃ jāgratsvapnaḥ || dvicandradarśanādy apy etadantargatam eva ca ||292||

prabodhānantaraṃ bādhitatvena jñātuṃ yogyaṃ saṃskārānādhāyakaṃ jñānaṃ svapnaḥ ||293||

cirakālaprarūḍhatayā jāgraty api sphuraṇārūḍhaḥ svapnaḥ svapnajāgrat ||294||

bhāvisukhaduḥkhādibījavāsanānvitaṃ sphuraṇarahitaṃ jñānaṃ suṣuptiḥ ||295||

etāsv avasthāsu || ekaiva śatasaṅkhyā syān nānāvibhavarūpiṇī || tasmād ajñānabhūmikāṃ manasā vivicya jahyāt || jñānabhūmikāṃ samaśrayed yogimāharāja iti ||296||

ata eva pātañjale – [2.27 + Bhāṣya]
tasya saptadhā prāntabhūmiḥ prajñā || tasyeti pratyuditavivekakhyāteḥ sapadheti || parijñānaṃ heyaṃ || nānyasya naḥ parijñeyam asti ||297|| [nāsya punaḥ parijñeyam asti]

kṣīṇā heyahetavo na punar eteṣāṃ kṣetavyam asti 2|| sākṣātkṛtaṃ nirodhasamādhinā hānam 3|| bhāvito vivekakhyātirūpo hānopāya ||298||

iti. eṣā catuṣṭayī kāryā vimuktiḥ prajñāyāḥ cittavimuktis tu trayī || caritādhikārā buddhiḥ || niravasthānā guṇāh svakāraṇe pralayābhimukhās tena sahāstaṃ gacchanti ||299||

na caiṣāṃ pravilīnānāṃ punar asty utdbhāvaḥ prayojanābhāvād || iti saptaprāntabhūmiprajñā[ṃ] || paśyan puruṣaḥ kuśala ity ākhyāyate kevalī bhavatīti ||300||

athaivaṃ cirakālaṃ vibhāvayato yuñjānā yuktapadavīṃ prāpnuvanti || tac ca cirañjīvitvasādhyam iti tatsādhakā mṛtyuñ jayopāyā vakṣyante ||301||

te coktā vasiṣṭhaṃ praty aneka[ka]lpajīvinā vāyasarājena bhuśuṇḍena || [6.24. 5-10] yasya hṛdi doṣaviṣayaphalaprotā vāsanātantusantatīr na gatā ||302||

yasya ca sarvadehalatā ghūrṇā ādhayo na bhavanti || pañcaśārīratarusarpaughāś cintāphaṇāśā na dahanti || pañcarāgadveṣaviṣāghūrṇasvamanomadhyago lobhavyālo na daśati || evaṃ śarīrāmbhodhivāḍavo 'yaṃ krodo na dahati || pañcayantraṃ tilamivānaṅgo na pīḍayati || yena ca kasmiṃś cid ekasmin paramapāvane pade cittaviśrāntir āśritā || taṃ mṛtyur na jighāṃsati ||303||

P.38
mṛtyheturogādihetubhūtapavanonmārgagāmitvanidānībhūtacintā'jīrṇādeḥ rāgadveṣādyabhāvenābhāvāt || athaikasamāhitaṃ ceto nādhyādayo vilumpantīti uktaṃ ||304||

tatraikaṃ saccidānandalakṣaṇaṃ brahma || prāṇādi vā || tatra brahmaṇi cittasamādhānaṃ bhuktimuktihetu[ḥ] pratipadam abhidhīyata eva || prāṇe tu prāṇāyāmaprakaraṇe vaktavyam api ||305|| 

mahāphalatve 'py apariśramasādhyatvena yogirājavallabhatvād rājayogasavidhe kiñ cid ucyate ||306||

hṛtpadme prāṇāpānanāmānau hṛdākāśārkaśaśinau || agnisomasvarūpinau uṣṇaśītalau ūrdhvādhogatī kalevaravāhau śramahīnau nirantaraspandaśaktī jāgradādyavasthāsu prāṇavahāsu nāḍīṣu sañcarataḥ || anayor gatir visatantulavād [-vat] api durlakṣyā ||307||

[cf. Yogavāsiṣṭha 6.26.13.] [apāna = inhalation; prāṇa = exhalation]
tatra yat prāṇārko hṛdayān nāsāgraṃ gatvā dvādaśāṅgulaparyantaṃ bāhyam āyāti || tad bāhyapūraṇād bāhyapūranam ucyate ||308|| [external inhalation = inhaling with external air, which goes out to 12 finger-breaths]

apānavāyau bāhyād antaḥ praviśati yo 'ntaḥpūraṇaḥ prāṇāpānāvayavānāṃ parasparasaṃśleṣaḥ so 'ntaḥpūraṇād antaḥpūraṇa ucyate ||309|| [internal inhalation - inhaling internally which is the mutual contact of prāṇa and apāna when apāna enters in]

apānavāyau hṛdyastaṅgate sati yāvat prāṇo nordhvam abhyudeti tāvad antaḥkumbhakāvasthā || bahirastaṅgate prāṇe yāvan nāpāno narābhimukhyenodgatas tāvad bahiḥkuṃbhakaḥ ||310|| [internal and external retention]

evam apānavāyor antarmukhatvaṃ tadbāhyarecakam || bahiḥkumbhitasya vāyor antarmukhatayā recanāt ||311|| [external exhalation - exhaling external air coming into the mouth]

evaṃ prāṇavāyor udayād yan nāsāvadhi bahirmukhatvaṃ tadābhyantaraṃ recakam || antaḥkumbhitasya vāyor bahirmukhatayā recanāt ṣaḍete ||312|| [internal exhalation - exhaling internal air going out of the mouth]

P.39
prāṇasyāpānam ādāyordhvagamanam || apānasya prāṇam ādāyādhogamanam || dvayaṃ cedam ||313|| [having taken an inhalation, there is the upward flow of exhalation; having taken an exhalation, there is the downward flow of inhalation].

evam aṣṭau gamanasvabhāvāḥ prāṇāpānayor yogibhir bhāvanīyāḥ || tatrāpi pūrakarecakasamaye sujñeyatvāt prāṇāpānāv eva vibhāvanīyau || kumbhakasamaye tayor durjñeyatvād brahmaiva vibhāvanīyam || iyam eva mokṣadaśājanikā vibhāvanā ||314||

etad abhihitaṃ vāsiṣṭhe bhuśuṇḍena – [Yogavāsiṣṭha 6.26.17, 20, 

aṣṭāv ete mahābuddhe rātrin divam anusmṛtāḥ ||
svabhāvād dehavāyūnāṃ kathitā muktidā mayā ||315||

avyagram asmin vyāpāre bāhyaṃ parijahan manaḥ |
dinaiḥ katipayair eva padam āpnoti kevalam ||316||

etadabhyasyataḥ puṃso bāhirviṣayavṛttiṣu |
na vadhnāti ratiṃ cetaḥ brāhmaṇāś cedṛtau yathā ||317|| [śvadṛto brāhmaṇo yathā]

etāṃ dṛṣṭim avaṣṭabhya ye sthitāḥ sthirabuddhayaḥ |
prāptaṃ prāptavyam akhilaṃ tair akhinnās ta eva hi ||ityādi||318||

atra prāṇāpānasañcāradaśāyāṃ tatsañcāre tatsthairyadaśāyāṃ brahmaṇi mano'tisāvadhānatayā nidheyam ity upadeśasāram ||319||

athārogyopāyā haṭhayogaprakaraṇe vakṣyamāṇā api sukaratvamahāphalatvādibhyo 'trāpi ke ced ucyante || te coktāḥ śriśivagorakṣanāthādibhiḥ svasvagranthe ||320|| 

P.40
[Śivasaṃhitā 3.80-87]
rasanāṃ yas tālumūle sthāpayitvātibuddhimān ||
pibet prāṇānilaṃ tasya rogāṇāṃ saṃkṣayo bhavet ||321||

kākacaṃcvā pibed vāyuṃ śītalaṃ yo vicakṣaṇaḥ
prāṇāpānavidhānajñaḥ sa bhaven muktibhājanaḥ ||322||
 
rasanāmūrdhvagāṃ kṛtvā kṣaṇārdhaṃ yas tu tiṣṭhati ||
kṣaṇena mucyate yogī vyādhimṛtyujarādibhiḥ ||323||
 
sarasaṃ yaḥ pibed vāyuṃ pratyahaṃ vidhinā sudhīḥ
naśyanti yoginas tasya śramadāhajvarāmayāḥ ||324||
  
 rasanāmūrdhvagāṃ kṛtvā yaś candrasalilaṃ pibet
 māsamātreṇa yogīndro mṛtyuṃ jayati niścitam ||325||
 
 rājadantabilaṃ gāḍhaṃ saṃpīḍya vidhinā pibet
 dhyātvā kuṇḍalinīṃ devīṃ ṣaṇmāsena kavir bhavet ||326||
 
 kākacañcvā pibed vāyuṃ sandhyayor ubhayor api
 kuṇḍalinyāḥ mukhe dhyātvā kṣayarogasya śāntaye |327||
 
 aharniśaṃ pibed yogī kākacañcvā vicakṣaṇaḥ
 dūraśrutir dūradṛṣṭis tathā syād darśanaṃ khalu ||328||
 
 dantair dantān samāpīḍya pibed vāyuṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ
 ūrdhvajihvaḥ sa medhāvī mṛtyuṃ jayati satvaram ||329||

pratyahaṃ catuḥkālam etadabhyāsataḥ ṣaṇmāsena pāpāni rogāṃś ca jayati || saṃvatsareṇa  mṛtyuṃ jayatīti prathitaṃ yogaśāstreṣu ||330||

[2. brahmavṛkṣakalpa] [cf. AK brahmakalpaḥ; brahmavṛkṣatailakalpa]
atha brahmavṛkṣakalpaḥ –
sa ca sādhakānāṃ cirajīvitvātyantavīryaparākramabuddhyanvitatvādivāñchitāṃ tatsiddhyarthaṃ kārttikeyena pṛṣṭaḥ śrīmaheśvaraḥ provāca ||30||

pūrvaṃ daivāsurair amṛtārthaṃ mathyamāne dugdhābdhau mathanāghātair ye sudhābindavo bahir āgatya patitās tebhyo brahmavṛkṣaḥ palāśanāmnā prasiddhaḥ utpannaḥ ||31||

brahmavṛkṣasya bījāni sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet || ab= AK 1.15.4a,d

P.45
saptarātraṃ prayatnena svedayec ca vicakṣaṇaḥ ||

mudagāpūpopayo gokṣīrāhārābhyāṃ vartate || viśeṣato 'mlalavaṇaṃ ca varjayet || brahmacaryeṇa tiṣṭhet || kulālabhasmaśayyāyāṃ śayītā || tatra sādhakaḥ paricārakaiḥ prayatnato rakṣaṇīyaḥ ||36||

yadā tu palād adhikaṃ sadugdhaṃ tailaṃ pīyate tadā tadaiva mūrcchā bhavati || tathā sati kulālabhasmāgūḍhasya sādhakasya mukhe tūlakena dugdhaṃ deyaṃ muhūrtānantaraṃ jātasaṃjño bhavati ||37||

evaṃ yadi kuḍavamitaṃ pibati tadā saptarātreṇa saṃjñāṃ labhate || tanmadhye 'gre 'py ekaviṃśatidinaparyantaṃ kṣīrāhāraṃ seveta || atha pītaṃ saptarātreṇa tattailaṃ pariṇāmaṃ bhajate || tataḥ phalaṃ labhate ||38||

anyac ca –

aparaṃ ca –
brahmavṛkṣasya komalāni patrāṇi śoṣayitvā cūrṇī kuryāt || taccūrṇaṃ prasṛtamitaṃ śivādīn praṇamya sumuhūrte takreṇa saha pibet || tasmin jīrṇe tu lavaṇāmlasnehavarjitaṃ bhuñjīta || tṛṣitas tu kṣīraṃ śītalaṃ jalaṃ vā pibet || 52||

māsamātraprayogeṇa śuklakeśādayaḥ patanti || dvitīyamāse bhramarādisadṛśā jāyante || sarvavyādhivinirmuktaḥ sahasrāyuś ca bhavati || atra yatheṣṭaṃ ceṣṭāvihārau ||53||

athavā –
brahmavṛkṣasya puṣpāṇi śoṣayitvā cūrṇī kuryāt || taccūrṇaṃ daśapalamitaṃ dvādaśapalamitena sarpiṣā samāloḍya snigdhe bhāṇḍe kṛtvā dhānyarāśau ekaviṃśatyahorātraṃ sthāpayet ||54||

tataḥ sumuhūrte śuddhakāyaḥ śivādīn arcayitvā yathābalaṃ palaparyantaṃ bhuñjīta || payasā ṣaṣṭikaudanaṃ ca bhuñjīta || tṛṣitaś ca khādirakaṣāyamiśritaṃ jalaṃ pibet || viṃśadrātreṇa phalaṃ labhet ||55||

mattamātaṅgavad vīryo vāyutulyaparākramaḥ ||
divyacakṣur mahātejā vahnijvālāsamaprabhaḥ ||

[p. 48]
athavā –
brahmavṛkṣavalkalasya sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ kārayitvā kṣīreṇa saha pibet || laghvāhāro jitendriyaś ca bhavati || māsārdhaprayogeṇa tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ tāmrādikāñcanaṃ bhavati || bhramarasadṛśakeśo dīrghāyuś ca bhavati ||57||

anyac ca –
brahmatailapalaṃ gṛhya ghṛtena saha pācayet ||
sasahāyaḥ pibet prājño mūrcchā tatraiva jāyate || 58||

tataḥ kṣīraṃ piben māsam ūrdhvaṃ tu ṣaṣṭibhojanam ||
saptarātrāt paraṃ martyaḥ chidrāṃ paśyati medinīm ||59||

chāyā na dṛśyate tasya adṛśyaś caiva jāyate ||
nāgininā nodakenāpi mṛtyus tasya prajāyate ||
viṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi jīvanaṃ tu na saṃśayaḥ ||60||
iti brahmavṛkṣakalpaḥ ||

[3. Mayūraśikhākalpa] [some content with KCKT]

atha mayūraśikhākalpaḥ –

pūrvadine sanimantraṇaṃ pūjayitvā puṣyanakṣatre gṛhṇīyāt || sā bālānāṃ kaṇṭhe baddhvā grahabhūtapiśācaḍākinyādibhayaṃ vārayati || dhūpaṃ datvā bhūtajvaraṃ nāśayati || tasyā mūlaṃ śikhāyāṃ baddhvā rājakule vyavahāre yuddhe ca vijayate ||61||

śatrudvāre nikṣepād uccāṭanaṃ bhavati || haste baddhvāpamṛtyuṃ nāśayati || tāmbūlena saha yasmai dīyate sa vaśyo bhavati || sarpadaṣṭasya ghṛtena saha nasyaṃ deyaṃ nirviṣo bhavati || madhunā saha bhuktvā śrutadharo bhavati ||62|| 

iti mayūraśikhākalpaḥ || 
[p.49]
[4. Jyotiṣmatīkalpa]

atha jyotiṣmatīkalpaḥ – [Not in KCKT] [cf AK]
śubhe muhūrte jyotiṣmatīṃ raktasūtreṇa veṣṭayitvā raktacandanapuṣpatāmbūlanaivedyādibhir iṣṭadevatāṃ tatrābhyarcya sādhakaḥ svayaṃ raktamālyāmbaradharo bhūtvā tadbījāni samānīya svālaye śoṣayitvā tebhyas tailayantreṇa tailaṃ triṃśatpalaṃ ṣaṣṭipalaṃ navatipalaṃ śatapalaṃ tato 'py adhikaṃ vā samuddharet ||63||

tatas tattailaṃ tailārdhaṃ ca sarpistailacaturthāṃśaṃ madhutailasamaṃ gokṣīraṃ tailadviguṇaṃ gomūtraṃ ekīkṛtya mṛdvagninā pācayet || tailaśeṣaṃ tu kārayet || 64||

atha tatra jātiphalajāyapatrīkapūrailākaṅkolāni cūrṇayitvā karṣamātraṃ nikṣipet || evaṃ pācitaṃ tailaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe saṃsthāpya dhānyarāśau sthāpayet ||65||

ekaviṃśatidinānantaraṃ dhānyarāśais tailam uddhṛtya sumuhūrte nirvātagṛhe sthitaḥ śuddhakāyaḥ palamitaṃ pibet || atha mūrcchito bhavati || tatas tanmukhe tūlakena gokṣīraṃ dadyāt || muhūrtāt saṃjñāṃ prāpnoti || sandhyānantaraṃ sakṣīraṃ ṣaṣṭikānnaṃ bhuñjīta || tṛṣitas tu gokṣīraṃ pibet | māsena sūryasamaprabho garuḍatulyagatir divyanetraś ca bhavati || 66||

divase tārakāṃ paśyati || sūryagarbhasthaṃ śaśinaṃ madhyāhne paśyati || dūrastho 'pi vācaṃ śṛṇute || viṣaṃ ca tasya jīryate || aṣṭādaśakuṣṭhāni naśyanti || dvitīye māse divārātrau vā bhramato maḍīṃ yakṣarakṣasadṛśo bhavati ||67|| 
iti jyotiṣmatīkalpaḥ || [Looks like a prose rendering of parts of RAK  266 - 275

[5. Aśvagandhākalpa] [Not in KCKT]

athāśvagandhākalpaḥ – [cf. AK]
aśvagandhāyāḥ sapta tālīśasyāṣṭau guḍasyāṣṭau palāni ekīkṛtya dvipalamitaṃ modakaṃ kuryāt || ekaikaṃ modakaṃ prātar utthāya bhuñjīta || [Cannot compare to RAK because RAK 242cd and 243ab missing]

mudgarasasakṣīraṣaṣṭikānnabhojanaṃ kuryāt ||68|| [close in content to RAK 244]
P.50
athavā –
cūrṇasaṃjñāpramāṇaṃ tu palacaturthāṃśamitam ||
saṃcūrṇam aśvagandhāyāḥ sudhātrīrasabhāvitam || ~ RAK 245cd
yaṣṭimadhusamāyuktaṃ madhunā saha lehayet |69|| ~ RAK 246ab

māsadvayaprayogeṇa valīpalitavarjitaḥ | ~RAK 246cd
yojanānāṃ śataṃ gacchen na śramas tasya jāyate ||70|| ~ RAK 247ab

anyac ca –
aśvagandhāpalaṃ gṛhya sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu kārayet |
śatāvarīpalaṃ caiva palam ekaṃ tu bhakṣayet ||
tena bhakṣitamātreṇa valīpalitavarjitaḥ ||71||

aśvagandhāṃ tu saṃgṛhya mātuluṅgarase pacet |
gomūtreṇa samāyuktaṃ māsam ekaṃ tu bhakṣayet |
gātraṃ vā lepitaṃ kuryāt sarvakuṣṭhāni nāśayet ||72||

medhāvī śrutimāṃś caiva kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate |
dṛdhakāyo bhaved devi valīpalitavarjitaḥ ||73||

aśvagandhāpalaṃ gṛhya śarkarābhiḥ samanvitam |
māsadvayaprayogeṇa vṛddho 'pi taruṇo bhavet ||74||

aśvagandhā tilā māṣā madhunā saha bhakṣitā | ~ RAK 249cd
sārdhamāsaprayogeṇa kuñjaraiḥ saha yudhyate  | = RAK 250ab
navanāgasamo vīrye vegena sa turaṅgamaḥ||75||
P.51
aśvagandhābījaṃ toyena peṣayitvā akṣīṇi añjayet || timirapaṭalādikaṃ naśyati ||76||

aśvagandhācūrṇaṃ triphalodakena saha carvitaṃ dantarogaṃ nāśayati || aśvagandhāmūlacūrṇaṃ navanītena saha bhakṣitam unmādahṛt ||77||

tilasaṃhitām aśvagandhāṃ ghṛtamadhubhyāṃ prātar bhakṣayitvā vṛddho 'pi taruṇāyate || atra kalpena kasyāpi parihāraḥ |78|| ity aśvagandhākalpaḥ ||

[6. Pāṭhākalpa] [Not in KCKT]

atha pāṭhākalpaḥ –

pāṭhāmūlaṃ mukhe kṣiptvā khaḍgaṃ stambhayate prahāraś ca na bhavati || pāṭhāmūlaṃ sarpiṣā saha pibet || viṣadaṣṭo nirviṣo bhavati ||79||

pāṭhāmūlam abhimantrya dakṣiṇakare baddhvā vyavahāre jayati || svagṛhe paragṛhe rājagṛhe vā priyo bhavati || lūtāvicarcikādikaṃ pralepena nāśayati || pāṭhāmūlaṃ garbhiṇīṃ vāmapādāṅguṣṭhe baddhva hastapādayor lepayitvā vā sukhena prasūyate ||80||

pāṭhāmūlaṃ chāgīghṛtena saha pītaṃ karṇākṣiśirorogān nāśayati || vṛścikadaṃśādau lepena nirviṣaṃ karoti || oṃ namo māheśvari siddheśvari bandha bandha huṃ phaṭ svāhā || sarvauṣadhābhimantraṇe mantraḥ ||81 || 

iti pāṭhākalpaḥ ||

[7. kākajaṅghakalpaḥ]

atha kākajaṅghākalpaḥ –

kākajaṅghāmūlaṃ tadrasena piṣṭam ajāviṭsahitaṃ vraṇarohaṇaṃ karoti || uṣṇodakena saha pītaṃ vīryaṃ stambhayati ||  ghṛtena saha pītaṃ vīryaṃ stambhayati ||82|| (drinking with warm water, to stop the loss of semen is mentioned in KCKT 12ab, p. 66)

P.52
ghṛtena saha pītaṃ jvaraṃ nāśayati || śītodakena madhunā saha pītaṃ chardi vārayati || kṣīreṇa saha pītaṃ rudhirasrāvaṃ nivartayati || karṇe baddhaṃ netrarogam ||83|| (content is similar to KCKT 13-14 , but no textual parallels)
 
madhuhiṅgūdakābhyāṃ plīhodaraṃ || kuṣṭhenodaraśūlaṃ || kaṭutailena vātarogam || guḍenoṣṇ[o]dakena ca śleṣmāṇam || guḍatakrābhyāṃ mūtrakṛccham || mahiṣīkṣīreṇa kṣayarogam ||84|| (This content is similiar to KCKT 15 - 17ab)
 
bhallātakarasena kuṣṭhaṃ nāśayati | kākajaṅghāmūlena gorocanena sahitaṃ tilakaṃ kṛtvā sarvajanaṃ vaśayati || tatpatrāṇi tu gomūtrasahitāṇi mukhe dhṛtvā mukharogaṃ nāśayati ||85|| (KCKT mentions the relief of Kuṣtha by taking KJ root with bhallātaka; 19cd. The mixing with orpiment for a tilak to control all people is mentioned at KCKT 14cd. The leaves with cow urine for healing mouth diseases is mentioned at KCKT 8ab).
 
kākajaṅghāmūlaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasena peṣayitvā tilatailena pācayet || tattailalepena khalvāṭī uttamacikuro bhavati palitaṃ ca naśyati ||86|| (Combining KJ with bhṛṅgarāja juice and cooking in sesame oil, applying as ointment for baldness etc., is mentioned in KCKT 9-10ab)

iti kākajaṅghākalpaḥ ||

[8. Somarājīkalpa] [Not in KCKT] [cf AK]

atha somarājīkalpaḥ – [uṣṇodakakalpa]

somarājībījāni śuṣkāṇi kārayitvā taccūrṇaṃ uṣṇodakena dinatrayaṃ gomūtreṇa dina ekaṃ kṣīreṇa dinadvādaśakam 12 ghṛtena dinadvādaśakam  12 āmalakīrasena dinatriṃśatparyantaṃ 30 bhāvayet ||87||

atha kṣīrāhāro bhuñjīta || valīpalitanirmukto dīrghāyur jāyate naraḥ ||88||

vākucīṃ sāmulapatrāṃ pañcāṅgāṃ kāñjike peṣayitvā kalkaṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye saptavāraṃ vaṅgaṃ niṣiñcet || śobhanaṃ tāraṃ bhavati ||  vākucītailena pāradena ca samaṃ gandhakaṃ marddayitvā tena kalkitadvādaśāṃśena nāgaṃ nidhāya dhāmyamāno raso vahnau nirbījaṃ kanakaṃ bhavet || 89|| [This seems to be a prose rendering  of RAK 604 - 610, but there are no parallels with the verses below]

anyac ca –
karavīrasamaiḥ patraiḥ nīlāñjanasamaprabhaiḥ |
raktaśākhā sadāpuṣpī bhagnā kṣīrapravāhiṇī ||90||

P.53
tathaiva kṣaṇamātreṇa rasas tiṣṭhati nānyathā |
śulbapatrāṇi lepena sahasrāṃśena vidhyati ||
nirbījaṃ kanakaṃ bhūyād devānām api bhūṣaṇam ||91||

anyac ca –
uṣṇodakaṃ tu yatra syāt tatra snātvā samāhitaḥ |
tatraiva caṇakān kṣiptvā rātrau ced buddhimān naraḥ ||92||

bhavanti komalāḥ prātar bhakṣayet tān dine dine ||
evaṃ māsaprayogeṇa jāyate kanakaprabhaḥ ||93||

sarvavyādhivinirmukto jīved varṣaśatāṣṭakam |
ekavarṣaprayogeṇa jīvec candrārkatārakam ||94||

tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
uṣṇodakaṃ ca kāśīśaṃ gandhapāṣāṇam eva ca ||95|| ~ AK 1,23.468

caturthāṃśena kanakaṃ rasabhāgaṃ ca niḥkṣipet |
khalve ca marddayet sarvaṃ naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ kṣaṇena tu ||96||

tena lepitamātreṇa śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
niṣiktaṃ tena toyena prativāpaṃ dahed budhaḥ ||97|| = AK 1,23.470

śulbaṃ sañjāyate svarṇaṃ candrārkaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavet |
tāraṃ vā tena mārgeṇa siktaṃ kanakam ādiśet || 98||
iti uṣṇodakakalpaḥ ||
P.54
[9. śvetārkakalpa] [Called śvetākalpa in the KCKY -no parallels] [AK śvetārkakalpaḥ; śvetārkamūlakalpaḥ]
atha śvetārkakalpaḥ –
śvetārkamūlasyāṅguṣṭhaparvapramāṇaṃ gaṇeśaṃ kṛtvā dinam ekaṃ raktapuṣpasahasreṇa gandhadhūpadīpanaivedyākṣataiḥ oṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ iti mantreṇa sampūjya dyūte vyavahāre rājadvāre raṇe ca vijayī bhavati ||99||

anenaiva vidhānena sarasvatīṃ kārayitvā sampūjya sarasvatīṃ labhate || śatroś ca mukhaṃ stambhayati ||100|

oṃ hrīṃ vada vada vāgvādinyai namaḥ iti sarasvatimantraḥ || evam eva sūryaṃ kārayitvā oṃ haṃsaḥ sūryāya namaḥ iti mantreṇa pañcaopacāraiḥ sampūjayet || sūryo prasanno bhavati ||101|| [Cannot compare with RAK because 304-314 of the RAK are missing] 

śvetārkaṃ tatsūtreṇa kaṭau baddhaṃ surate śukraṃ stambhayati || śvetārkakṣīrarasābhyāṃ sūtaṃ marddayitvā bhakṣayet || ekaviṃśatidinena ||102|| [~RAK315]

valīpalitanirmukto jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ |
navanāgabalaś caiva sarvajño vijayī bhavet ||103|| [= RAK 316] 

iti śvetārkakalpaḥ ||

[10. śrīphalakalpa]
atha śrīphalakalpaḥ – [no parallels with KCKT]

bilvabījaṃ susūkṣmaṃ cūrṇaṃ kārayitvā niṣpīḍya tattailaṃ gṛhītvā kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ vā vamanavirecanapūrakaṃ karṣaikaṃ pibet ||104||

prathamadine medhāvī dvitīyadine badhiratvaṃ nāśayati || tṛtīyadine sarvaśāstrajñaḥ caturthadine sahasragranthabhāṣī pañcamadine śrutidharaḥ ṣaṣṭamadine brahmatulyaḥ saptamadine khecaro bhavati ||105||

oṃ vīrajananī svāhā iti mantreṇa aṣṭottaraśatam abhimantrya tailaṃ pibet ||106||

iti śriphalakalpaḥ ||
P.55
[11. rudantīkalpa]
atha rudantīkalpaḥ – [no parallels with KCKT] {cf AK]

rudantī śvetā raktā pītā syāmā ceti caturddhā || caṇakākārapatrā harimecakapuṣpā || himākārasravatpatrasthā bindumatī || mayi vidyamānāyāṃ lokāḥ kliśyantīti kṛpayā rodamāneva mahauṣadhī ||107||

śuklapakṣe sumuhūrte tasyāḥ pañcāgaṃ [pañcāṅgaṃ] gṛhītvā kaṭukālāmbukapātre sthāpayitvā madhunāloḍya biḍālapadamātraṃ bhakṣayet || tailānnaṃ bhuñjīta || ekamāsaprayogeṇa sahasrāyur bhavati ||108||

anyac ca –
rudantirasam ādāya madhusarpisamanvitam | =RAK 596cd
saptarātraprayogeṇa vajrakāyo mahābalaḥ | ~RAK 597ab (prayogeṇa vajrakāyo
bhuvanāntaracārī ca divyacakṣuḥ prajāyate ||109||

pañcaṅgāmauṣadhīṃ gṛhya svarṇena saha marddayet | 110b = RAK 599b
drutaṃ śulbe pratīvāpaṃ nirbījaṃ kanakaṃ bhavet ||110|| = RAK 599cd 
iti rudantīkalpaḥ ||

iti saṃkṣepataḥ proktāḥ kalpāḥ śrīsaṅkaroditāḥ ||
sadguruṃ prāpya kartavyāḥ sāvadhānena cetasā ||111|| Interesting verse! Śaiva source [except Muṇḍīkalpa]

sadupāyakṛtā hy evaṃ bhavanty amṛtasannibhāḥ ||
anyathā viparītā syus tasmād gurum upāśrayet ||112||

[12. Muṇḍīkalpa] [AK]
atha muṇḍīkalpaḥ –

bhikṣūttamāṅgaparikalpitanāmadheyaṃ tatpatrapuṣpaphalaśākhasamūlacūrṇam | ~ Khecarīvidyā 4.2cd [113a = KCKT 1a,c in ch. on Muṇḍīkalpa]
takrāranālapayasā madhuśarkarājyair adyāt pṛthak kavalitaṃ rasamaṇḍalāni ||113|| ~ Khecarīvidyā 4.2cd
 P.56
muṇḍyāḥ pañcāṅgacūrṇaṃ kṛtvā prathamamaṇḍalaṃ takreṇa saha grāsamātram aśniyāt || dvitīye āranālena sahāśniyāt || āranālaṃ tu rājikāmiśraṃ takram || tṛtīye payasāśniyāt || caturthe madhunā sahāśniyāt || pañcame śarkarājalena sahāśniyāt || ṣaṣṭhe ājyena sahāśniyāt || evaṃ maṇḍalaṣaṭkakṛte sati valīpalitavarjitaḥ gadavarjitaḥ matipūrṇaś ca mahadbalaparākramo bhavati || tiktāmlakṣāravarjaṃ bhuñjīta || atra svecchāvihāraḥ ||114||

tṛtīyopadeśaḥ
atha yogopayogiśarīrakṣā -
vairāgyasṛṣṭyādijñānārthaṃ śārīramucyate।। taitraikaikasya puruṣasya śarīre trividham। sthūlasūkṣmakāraṇabhedāt। tatrāpi gandhāditanmātrānvitapṛthivyādipañcabhūtavikāraviśeṣo bhogāvacchedakaṃ sthūlaṃ śarīram। etadevānnamayakośa ityucyate।। 1।।
saptadaśāvayavakaṃ sūkṣmaśarīram।। te cāvayavāḥ prāṇāpānodānasamānavyānāḥ pañca vāyavaḥ।। kramāddigvātārkapraceto'śviniti yantritāni।। 2।।
śrotratvaṅnayanarasanāghrāṇātmakāni pañca jñānendriyāṇi। vahnīndropendrayamapraṃjāpatiniyantritāni।। 3।।
vākpāṇipādapāyapasthākhyāni pañca karmendriyāṇi। caturmukhacandrādhiṣṭhite ca buddhimanasī jñānakarmendriye।। evaṃ saptadaśa।। 4।।
vāsudevaśaṃkarādhiṣṭhitayościttāhaṃkārayorevāntarbhāvaḥ। anantarbhāve vā ūnaviṃśatyavayavakam।। 5।।
sūkṣmaśarīramālocyam।। etanmadhye'pi karmendriyasahitaṃ prāṇādipañcakaṃ prāṇamayakośaḥ। karmendriyasahitaṃ mano namomayakośaḥ। buddhindriyasahitā buddhirvijñānamamayakośaḥ।। 6।।
sūkṣmaśarīramevātivāhitaṃ śarīraṃ nirantarabhāvitaṃ sat parakāya praveśādau samarthaṃ bhavati।। karttatvabhoktṛtvādyabhimāni ihalokaparalokagāmi ca bhavati।। 7।।
taduktaṃ sāṃkhye-
pūrvotpannamasaktaṃ niyataṃ mahadādisūkṣmaparyantam।।
saṃsarati nirupabhogaṃ bhāvairadhivāsitaṃ liṃgam।। 8।।
pūruṣārthahetukamidaṃ nimittanaimittikaprasaṃgena।।
prakṛtervibhutvayogānnaṭavadavatiṣṭhate liṃgamiti।। 9।।
pūrvotpannam।। pradhānenādisarge pratipuruṣamekaikamutpāditam।। asaktam avyāhataṃ śilāsvapyanu viśati।। niyam।। ādisargādau mukte ścaikam।। mahadādisūkṣmaparyantam।। mahadahaṃkāraikādaśendriyapañcatanmātraparyanto'sya samudāyaḥ।। etanmate pañcatanmātrā api sūkṣmaśarīrāntargatā iti dhyeyam।। 10।।
bhāvaiḥ dharmādharmajñānājñānavairāgyāvairāgye aiśvaryānaiśvarye puṣpaistailamiva adhivāsitaṃ saṃsarati।। pūrvaṃ sthūlaṃ śarīraṃ jahāti navaṃ gṛhṇātītyarthaḥ।। pradhāne layaṃ gacchatīti liṃgam।। 11।।
prakṛteḥ sāmarthyādidaṃ naṭavadanekajātīyaṃ śarīreṣu dharmādharmādinimittavaśādahaṃkāraṃ karotīti dvitīyārthaḥ। triguṇā prakṛtiravidyādipadavācyā kāraṇaśarīram ānandamayakośaśca।। 12।।
eṣu kośeṣu nigūḍho bhagavānātmā śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyo sākṣātkarttavyaśca।। sa ca yasmin kośe yathā yathā prakāśate tathoktaṃ sureśvarācāryeṇa-
dehamannamayaṃ kośamāviśyātmā prakāśate।।
sthūlo bālaḥ kṛśaḥ kṛṣṇo varṇāśramavikalpavān।। 14।।
prāṇakośe'pi jīvāmi kṣudhito'smi pipāsitaḥ।
saṃśito niścito manye iti kośe manomaye।। 15।।

etadapi śarīratrayaṃ vyaṣṭisamaṣṭibhedāt dvividham।। atra vyaṣṭisamāṣṭiśarīrayorvṛkṣavanayoriva jalajalāśayoriva bhedābhedau।। atrāpi vyaṣṭisthūlaśarīropahitaṃ caitanyaṃ viśva ityucyate।। 19।।
samaṣṭisthūlaśarīropahitaṃ caitanyaṃ vaiśvānaro virāḍityucyate।। sarvanarābhimānitvāt vividhaṃ virājamānatvācca।। 20।।
vyaṣṭisūkṣmaśarīropahitaṃ caitanyaṃ tejasa ucyate sarvanarābhimānitvāt vividhaṃ virājamānatvācca।। vyaṣṭisūkṣmaśarīropahitaṃ caitanyaṃ taijasa ucyate tejomayāntaḥ karaṇopahitatvāt।। 21।।
etatsamaṣṭayupahitaṃ caitanyaṃ sūtrātmā hiraṇyagarbhaḥ prāṇa iti cocyate।। sarvatrānusyūtatvena sraksūtratulyatvāt।। jñānakriyāśaktimadapañcīkṛta pañcamahābhūtābhimānitvācca ।। 22।।
evaṃ vyaṣṭikāraṇaśariropahitaṃ caitanyaṃ prājña ucyate।। ekāvidyāvabhāsakatvenānatiprakāśakatayā prakarṣeṇājñatvāt।। 23।।
etatsamaṣṭayupahitaṃ caitanyaṃ sarvajñatva sarveśvaratvasarveśvaratvasarvaniyantritvādiguṇakamavyaktamantaryāmi jagatkāraṇamīśvara iti ca vyapadiśyate।। sakalāvidyāvabhāsakatvāt।।24।।

upādheyasvarūpābhedenābhedāt।। tatrādya upāsakaḥ dvitīya upāsyastadabhedabhāvanayā pūrva sannevābhedo vismṛtagṛhastharatnavadanupayukto vedānumānanididhyāsanasamādhibhiḥ sākṣātkṛto bhavati।। 26।।
svakāryakārīti yuñjānairavirataṃ vyaṣṭisamaṣṭiśarīrayostadupahitacaitanyayoścābheda eva bhāvyam iti।। evaṃ sthirībhūtāyāmasyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ sakalaśarīrasamūhasyāvāntaravana samudāyasya mahāvana tvamivaikyaṃ bhāvyam।। 27।।
tadupahitacaitanyasyāpi mahāvanāvacchinnākāśasyaiva ca bhāvyamaikyam -
ko mohaḥ kaḥ śoka ekatvamanupaśyata ityādi śruteḥ। tadanantaraṃ prathamata eva vā sarvasyāpi caitanyopādānakatvādibhyaścaitanyātmakatvaṃ paśyan kṛtakṛtyo bhūyāditi śivam ।। 28।।
atha pāñcabhautike śarīre pratyekaṃ bhūtaguṇāḥ proktā śrībhagavatā śrīvyāsadevena śrīśukadevāya mokṣadharme।। 29।।

gandho gurutvaṃ śaktiśca saṃghātaḥ sthāpanā dhṛtiḥ।। 30।।
tathā ca -
sthiratvādinā sādharmyeṇa śarīrasya mahīmahīruhamahīdharādyaiḥ samamaikyaṃ bhāvanīyamevamagre'pi।। ata eva vaidharmyasādharmyābhyāṃ bhedābhedayorjñānena mokṣahetubhūtastatvasākṣātkāraḥ samutpadyate।। 31।।

nirukte trayodaśadvitīyapāde -
tadidamannamantaḥśleṣmā retaḥ sambhavati।। śleṣmaṇo raso rasācchoṇitam।। śoṇitānmāṃsam।। māṃsānmedo।। medasaḥ snāyavaḥ।। snāvo'sthini।। asthibhyo majjā।। majjāto retaḥ।। 39।।
tadidaṃ yonau retaḥ siktaṃ puruṣaḥ sambhavati।। śukrātireke pumān bhavati।। śoṇitātireke strī bhavati।। dvābhyāṃ same napuṃsako bhavati।। śukreṇa bhinnena yamo bhavati।। 40।।
dampatī ghātakṛtyena tathopalakṣitāṃgo bhavati।। taccaikarātroṣitaṃ kalalaṃ bhavati।। pañcarātrād budbudaḥ।। saptarātrāt peśī।। dvisaptarātrādarbudaḥ।। pañcaviṃśatirātrāt susthitaṃ māsamātrāt kaṭhinaṃ bhavati।। dvimāsābhyantare śiraḥ sampadyate।। māsatrayeṇa grīvāvyādeśo।। māsacatuṣkeṇa tvagavyādeśaḥ। pañcame māse nakharomavyādeśaḥ। ṣaṣṭhe mukhanāsikākṣiśrotraṃ ca sambhavati।। saptame calanasamartho bhavatyaṣṭame budhyādyadhyavasyati ।। navame sarvāṃgasampūrṇo bhavati।। 41।।

yājñavalkyaśca -

tathā hi -
tīkṣṇāyāsabhīrutvapracaṇḍatāvihārācāracāpalyaṃ sārpasya।। atikāmitvājasrāhārāmarṣānasthityādi śākunasya ekāntagrāhiraudratvāsūyādharmabāhyatvādi rākṣasasya।। ucchiṣṭāhārataikṣṇyasāhasaratrīlolupatvanirlajjādi paiśācasya।। asaṃvibhāgālasyāsūyāduḥkhaśīlatvalolupatvakārpaṇyādi pretasya।। 56।।
atha tāmasānāṃ durmedhatvamandatāmaithunanityatā kariṣṇutādi pāśavasya।। anavasthitatāmaurkhyabhīrutvaṃ salilarthitā parasparābhimarṣādi mātsyasya।। ekasthānaratinityāhāraratidharmakāmārthavarjitatvādi vānaspatyasya।। evamanyadapi yathāśāstralokaprasidhyā jñeyam।। 57।।
evaṃ puruṣāṇāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ ca prakṛtiṃ jñātvā tadanurūpāṃ ca kriyāṃ caret।। svrakṛtyanusāriṇī hi kriyā।। kṣipraṃ prasidhyati।। svaprakṛtau sthitaḥ sukhī bhavati।। svaprakṛtiśca bhayaṃ na bhavatītyādi jñeyamālocyam।। 58।।
tacca śarīraṃ ṣaḍaṃgam।। śākhāścatasraḥ।। madhyamaṃ pañcamam।। ṣaṣṭhaṃ ca śira iti ।। 59।।
atha pratyaṃgāni।। mastakodarapṛṣṭhanābhilalāṭanāsācibukabastigrīvā ekaikāḥ।। karṇanetrabhruvoṣṭhagaṇḍa kakṣāstanavṛṣaṇapārśvasphikjānubāhūrūprabhṛtayo dve dve।। evaṃ tvacaḥ kalādhātavo malāḥ doṣāḥ।। yakṛtplīhānau phuphusodakau hṛdayāmāśayāḥ। antrāṇi vṛkkau srotāṃsi kaṇḍarājālāni kūrccā rajjavaḥ sīvanyāḥ saṃghātāḥ sīmantāḥ asthīni sandhayaḥ snāyavaḥ peśyaḥ śirā dhamanyo marmmāṇi ceti।। 60।।
tatra tvacaḥ sapta।। kalāḥ sapta।। dhātavaḥ sapta।। trayo malāḥ।। trayo doṣāḥ।। yakṛdodyekaikamuktam।। āśayāḥ sapta।। vātāśayapittāśayaśleṣmāśayaraktāśaya- āmāśayapakvāśayamūtrāśayabhedāt।। strīṇāṃ garbhāśayo'pyaṣṭamaḥ।। 61।।
śravaṇanayanaghrāṇodarameḍhrāṇi nava srotāṃsi।। nārīṇāṃ trīṇyaparāṇyapi।। dve stanayorekaṃ rasavaham।। ṣoḍaśa kaṇḍarāḥ।। hastapādagrīvāpṛṣṭheṣu pratyekaṃ catasraḥ।। 62।।
tatra hastapādakaṇḍarāṇāṃ nakhā agraprarohāḥ।। grīvākaṇḍarāṇāṃ meḍhram।। pṛṣṭhakaṇḍarāṇāṃ nitambaḥ।। jālāni ṣoḍaśa।। māṃsaśirāsnāyvasthiṣu pratyekaṃ catvāri।। tāni maṇibandhagulphasaṃśritāni।। 63।।
ṣaṭ kūrcāḥ।। hastayordvau pādayordvau।। grīvāmeḍhrayorekaikaḥ।। mahatyo māṃsarajjavaścatasraḥ pṛṣṭhavaṃśamubhayataḥ peśinibandhanārthe dve bāhye ābhyantare ca dve।। 64।।

caturddaśāsthisaṃghātāḥ।। trayo gulphajānuvaṃkṣaṇeṣu।। pratyekaṃ pādayorbā-hvośca।। trikaśairasi ekaikaḥ।। ekaikasaṃghāte'ṣṭādaśāsthīni।। caturddaśa sīmantāḥ।। 66।।
asthīni ṣaṣṭayadhikaśatatrayāṇi।। vaidikamate śākalyamate triśatāni।। teṣāṃ viṃśatyadhikaśataṃ śākhāsu।।saptadaśottaraṃ śroṇipṛṣṭhodarorassu ।। grīvāṃ pratyūrdhvordhva triṣaṣṭiḥ।। evaṃ śatatrayam।। 67।।
tatraikāṃgulyāṃ trīṇi trīṇi।। iti pañcadaśa।। talagulpha kūrcasthāne daśa।। jaṃghāyāṃ dve।। pārṣṇijānūruṣu ekaikam।। evamekaikaśākhāyāṃ triṃśat।। 68।।
bhagagudanitambeṣu catvāri।। trike ekam।। ekaikasmin pārśve ṣaṭtriṃśat।। aṣṭāvurasi।। dve kṣasaṃjñe 117।। grīvāyāṃ nava।। kaṇṭhanāḍyāṃ catvāri।। dve hanvoḥ ।। 69।।
dantā dvātriṃśat।। nāsāyāṃ trīṇi।। ekaṃ tāluni।। gaṇḍakarṇe śaṃkheṣvekaikam।। ṣaṭ śirasi ।। 63।। evaṃ 300।। etāni pañcavidhāni।। kapālarucakataruṇavalayanalakabhedāt।। 70।।

tāvantya eva pārśvayoḥ।। urasyaṣṭau।। tāvantya eva grīvāyām।। trayaḥ kaṇṭhe।। nāḍīṣu hṛdayaklomanibaddhāsvaṣṭādaśa।। dantamūleṣu dvātriṃśat।। ekaḥ nāsāyāṃ dvau varttamaṇḍajau।। netrāśayau gaṇḍakarṇaśaṃkheṣvekaikaḥ।। dvau hanusandhī।। dvau bhrūpari śaṃkhayośca dvau।। pañca śiraḥkapāleṣu eko mūrdhni।। 75।।
nava snāyuśatāni।। tāsāṃ śākhāsu ṣaṭśatāni।। dve triṃśacca koṣṭhe।। grīvordhvaṃ saptatiḥ।। ekaikāṃgulyāṃ ṣaṭnicitāstāḥ।। triṃśat।। tāvatya eva kūrcagulpheṣu।। tāvatya eva jaṃghāyām।। daśa jānuni।। catvāriṃśadūrau।। daśa vaṃkṣaṇe।। evamekaikasyāṃ sārddhaśatam।। ṣaṣṭiḥ kaṭyām।। aśītiḥ pṛṣṭhe।। pārśvayoḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ।। urasi triṃśat।। ṣaṭtriṃśad grīvāyām।। mūrdhni catustriṃśat।। evaṃ navaśatī।। 76।।

tasyāstṛtīye tvāvartte garbhaśayyā pratiṣṭhitā।।
yathā rohitamatsyasya mukhaṃ bhavati rūpataḥ।। 80।।
saptaśirāśatāni।। yābhiridaṃ śarīraṃ kedāra iva kulyābhirārāma iva jalahāriṇibhirupatiṣṭhate।। anugṛhyate cākuñcanaprasāraṇādibhiḥ।। tāsāṃ nābhimūlaṃ tata ūrdhvamadhastiryak prasaranti।। tāsāṃ mūlaśirāścatvāriṃśat।। daśa bātavahāḥ।। daśa pittavahāḥ।। daśa śleṣmavahāḥ।। daśa raktavahāḥ।। tāsāṃ vātavahā vātasthānagatāḥ pañcasaptatyadhikaśatasaṃkhyakā bhavanti।। 81।।
evaṃ pittavahādyā api pittādisthāna gatāstāvantya eva bhavanti।। tatra vātavahā ekaikasyāṃ śākhāyāṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ।। koṣṭhe catustriṃśat।। tāsāṃ gudameḍhrāśritāḥ।। śroṇyāmaṣṭau ।। 82।।
dve dve pārśvayoḥ।। ṣaṭ pṛṣṭhe tāvantya evodare।। daśa vakṣasi।। ekacatvāriṃśat jatruṇa ūrdhvam।। tāsāṃ caturddaśa grīvāyām।। karṇayoścatasraḥ।। nava jihvāyām।। ṣaṭ nāsikāyām।। 83।।
aṣṭau netrayoḥ।। evaṃ pañcasaptatyadhikaṃ śataṃ vātavahānām।। eṣa eva vibhāga pittavahādīnāmapi।। viśeṣastu।। etā netrayorddaśa karṇayordve iti।। 84।।
atra svasvavahāsu śirāsu vāyvādayo'vikṛtā ssañcarato bahūn guṇān kurvante ।। vikṛtāstu doṣāniti। caturviśati dhamanyo nābhiprabhavāḥ 85।।
tāstu śirāprāyā api karmavailakṣaṇyādāgameṣu tadanyatvena pratipāditāḥ।। tāsāṃ daśordhvagāḥ।। śabdarūparasagandhapraśvāsocchvāsajṛmbhatikṣutiḥ hasitakathitaruditādīn viśeṣānabhivahantyaḥ śarīraṃ dhārayanti।। 86।।
tāstu hṛdayagatāstridhā jāyanta iti triṃśat।। tāsu dve dve vātapittakaphaśoṇitarasān vahata iti daśa।। śabdarūparasagandhānaṣṭābhirgṛhṇīte।। 87।।
dvābhyāṃ bhāṣate dvābhyāṃ ghoṣaṃ karoti।। dvābhyāṃ svapiti।। dvābhyāṃ pratibudhyate।। dve cāsravāhinyau।। dve stanyaṃ striyaḥ vahataḥ śukraṃ narasya vahataḥ।। 88।।
etānābhyūrdhvaṃ śarīraṃ dhārayanti vyāpayanti ca nābhyūrdhva gamatvaṃ karma kurvanti ।। adhogamāstu daśa।। mūtrapurīṣaśukrārttavādīnadho vahanti tarpayanti ca śarīraṃ pakvāśayasyānnapānarasādinā।। 89।।
āmapakvāśayāṃ te tridhā jāyante।। atastriṃśat।। tāsāṃ tu vātapittakaphaśoṇitarasān dve dve vahataḥ।। tā daśa।। dve'nnavāhinyāvantrāśrite।। dve toyavahe।। dve meḍhrabastisthe mūtravahe।। dve śakravahe।। dve pakvāśayagudasthe visargāya।। te eva nārīṇāmārttavavahe।। dve sthūlāntrabaddhe।। dve vaconirasinyau।। 90।।
aṣṭāvanyāstiryaggāminīnāṃ svedamarpayanti।। tiryaggatāṃścatasraḥ।। tāsāmekaikā śatasahasradhā ceti na saṃkhyātāḥ।। tābhiridaṃ śarīraṃ gavākṣitaṃ vibaddhamātataṃ ca।। 91।।
tāsāṃ mukhāni romakūpapratibaddhāni।। yaiḥ svedamabhivahanti।। antarbahiśca annādirasaṃ abhyaṃgālepanādivīryāṇi cābhisarpayanti।। taireva ca sparśa sukhādi gṛhyate।। 92।।
atha marmasthānāni -
tāni saptottaraśatāni bhavanti।। teṣāmekaikasyāṃ śākhāyāṃ ekādaśaiva catuścatvāriṃśat।। udarorasordvādaśa।। caturddaśa pṛṣṭhe।। grīvāyāṃ pratyūrdhvaṃ saptatriṃśat।। tatra sakthiśākhāmarmāṇi।। chidratalahṛdayakūrcaśirogulphameḍhrabastijānvāṇyūrvīlohitākṣāṇi viṭ pañceti।। 93।।
evameveratarasakthimarma।। bāhūkṣipratalahṛdayakūrccaśiromaṇibandhendrabastikūrparāṇyūrvīlohitākhyāni kakṣandharaṃ ceti।। evamevetarabāhau ।। 94।।
udarorasostu gudabastinābhihṛdayastanamūlarohitāpalāpānyastaṃvau ca।। pṛṣṭhakaṭī taruṇakukundaranitambapārśvasandhibṛhatyaṃsaphalakānyaṃsau ceti।। 95।।
jatruṇa ūrdhva catasro dhamanyaḥ।। aṣṭau mātṛkāḥ।। dve kṛkāṭike ।। dve vidhure।। dvau phaṇau। dvāvapāṃgau।। vātāvahārttau।। dvāvutkṣepau।। dvau śaṃkhau।। ekā sthapaṇī।। pañca sīmantāḥ।। catvāri śṛṃgāṭakāni।। eko'dhipatiriti marmāṇi।। 96।।
māṃsaśirāsnāyvasthisannipātaisteṣu svabhāvata eva prāṇā viśeṣeṇa tiṣṭhanti।। tasmādyāgibhisteṣu te niyamyāḥ।। 97।।
teṣu cābhihatā vakṣyamāṇān bhāvan prāpadyate।। tatra sadyaḥ prāṇaharāṇyāgneyāni ।। 98।।
agniguṇeṣvāśukṣīṇeṣu kṣapayanti।। tāni ca।। kaṇṭhadhamanimātṛkāśṛṃgāṭakāpāṃgasthaphaṇikakṣāstanamūlastanarohita adhipatiśaṃkhagudahṛyabastinābhimarmmāṇi।। kālāntaraprāṇaharāṇi।। saumyāgneyāni।। 99।।
agniguṇeṣu āśukṣīṇeṣu krameṇa somaguṇeṣu kālāntare kṣapayanti।। tāni ca vakṣomarmmāṇi sīmantāstalākṣimeḍhrabastayaḥ।।
kaṭika taruṇe sandhipārśva bṛhatīdvayam।।
nitambāviti caitāni kālāntaraharāṇi tu।।100।।
viśalyaprāṇaharāṇi vāyavyāni।। śalyamukhāvaruddho yāvadante vāyustiṣṭhati tāvajjīvati।। 101।।
śalye tūddhṛtamātre marmasthalāśritaḥ prāṇo niṣkrāmati।। tāni ca utkṣepau sthapaṇī ca vaikalyakarāṇi saumyāni।। somo hi sthiratvācchaityācca prāṇāvalambanaṃ kurute।। 102।।
lohitākṣāṇi jānūrvī kūrccā viṭapakūrparāḥ।।
kakundhare kakṣandhare vidhure sakṛkāṭike।। 103।।
aṃsāṃsaphalakāpāṃgā nīle manye phaṇau tathā।।
vaikalyajananānyāhurāvarttau dvandvau tathaiva ca।। 104।।
rujākarāṇyāgneyavāyavyāni viśeṣatastau rujākarau।।
gulphau dvau maṇibandhau dvau dve dve kūrcaśirāṃsi ca।।
rujākarāṇi jānīyādaṣṭāvetāni buddhimān।। 105।।
atha pratyekaṃ marmasthānānyucyante -
tatra pādāṃguṣṭhāṃgulyormadhye kṣipramiti marma।। tatra viddhasya kṣepakena maraṇam।। madhyamāṃgulīmānupūrvyeṇa madhye pādasya talahṛdayaṃ tatra rujābhirmaraṇam।। 106।।
jaṃghāpādayoḥ sandhāne gulphaḥ tatra rujāstabdhapādatā khañjanatā vā।। gulphasandherubhayato'dhaḥ kūrccaśiraḥ।। tatra rujāśophau।। kṣiprasyopariṣṭādubhayataḥ kūrcaḥ।। tatra pādasya bhramaṇavepane bhavataḥ।। 107।।
pārṣṇipratijaṃghāmadhye indrabastiḥ।। tatra śoṇitakṣayena maraṇam।। jaṃghorvoḥ sandhāne jānuḥ।। tatra khañjanatā ।। jānūrdhvamu bhayataśca tryaṃgulā aṇoḥ।। tatra śoṣābhivṛddhistabdhasakthitā ca ।। 108।।
urumadhye ūrvī tatra rohite tatra lohitapūrṇakoṣṭhatayā kāsaśvāsābhyāṃ mriyate।। ante śoṇitakṣayāt sakthiśoṣaḥ।। ūrvyā ūrdhvamadhovaṃkṣaṇasandherūrumūle lohitākṣam।। tatra śoṇitakṣayena pakṣāghātaḥ।। 109।।
vaṃkṣaṇavṛṣaṇayorantare viṭapam।। tatra khāñjyamalpaśukratā vā iti sakthimarmmāṇi।। bāhvorapyevam।। ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ।। nānyatra gulphajānuviṭapāni tāni vāhau maṇibandhakūrparakakṣandharāṇi।। ‍110।।
tatra maṇibandhe kaṇṭhatā kūrpare kuṇiḥ।। vakṣaḥkakṣayormadhye kakṣandharaṃ tatra pakṣāghātaḥ।। evaṃ catvāriṃśat śākhāmarmmāṇi।। 111।।
athodaroraso marmmāṇi।। bāhvorapyevam ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ।। tatra vātavarcco nirasanaṃ sthūlāntrapratibaddhaṃ gudanāmakaṃ marmma।। tatra viddhe sadyo maraṇam।। alpamāṃsaśoṇite'bhyantarataḥ kaṭyāṃ mūtrāśayo bastiḥ tatrāpi sadyo maraṇamaśmarīvraṇādṛte।। 112।।
tatrāpyubhayato bhinne na jīvati।। ekato vā bhinne jīvati।। ekato vā bhinne mūtrasrāvivraṇo bhavati।। pakvāśayayormadhye śirāprabhavā nābhiḥ tatra sadyo maraṇam।। 113।।
stanayormadhye hṛdayaṃ tatrāpi sadyaḥ।। stanayoradhastād dvayaṃgulamubhayatastanamūle tatra kaphapūrṇakoṣṭhatayā mriyate।। stanacūcukayorūrdhvaṃ stanarohite tatra lohitapūrṇakoṣṭhatayā kāsaśvāsābhyāṃ mriyate।। 114।।
aṃsakūṭayoradhastāt pārśvoparibhāgayorapalāpau tato raktena pūyabhāgagatena maraṇam ।। 115।।
atha pṛṣṭhamarmmāṇi -
tatra pṛṣṭhavaṃśamubhayataḥ pratiśroṇikāṇḍamasthinī kaṭīkataruṇe।। tatra śoṇitakṣayāt pāṇḍuvivarṇo hīnarūpaśca mriyate।। pārśvajaghanabahirbhāge pṛṣṭhavaṃśamubhayataḥ kakundare tatra sparśājñānamadhaḥkāye ceṣṭopaghātaśca।। 116।।
śroṇikāṇḍayoruparyācchādano pārśvetarapratidvandvau nitambau tatrādhaḥ kāyaśoṣau daurbalyācca maraṇam।। adhaḥ pārśvāntara pratibaddhau jaghana pārśvamadhyayostiryagūrdhvaṃ ca jaghanāt pārśvasandhi tatra lohitapūrṇakoṣṭhatayā mriyante।। 117।।
stanamūlādanūbhayataḥ pṛṣṭhavaṃśasya bṛhatī tatra śoṇitātipravṛttijopadravairmriyate।। pṛṣṭhopari pṛṣṭhavaṃśamubhayatastrikasambaddhe aṃsaphalake tatra bāhvoḥ svāpaśoṣau।। bāhumūrdhvagrīvāmadhye aṃsapīṭhaskandhe bandhane aṃse tatra stabdhabāhutā।। 118।।
atha ūrdhvajatrūgatāni marmmāṇi-
kaṇṭhanāḍīmubhayataścatasro dhamanyaḥ dve nīle dve ca manye vyatyāsena tatra mūkatā svaravaikṛtaṃ maraṇam।। śirogrīvayoḥ sandhāne kṛkāṭike ।। tatra calamūrdhvatā ca karṇapṛṣṭhato'dhaḥ - saṃśrite vidhure tatra bādhiryam।। 119।।
ghrāṇamārgamubhayataḥ srotomārgapratibaddhe abhyantarataḥ phaṇe tatra gandhājñānam।। bhrūpucchāntarayoradho'kṣṇorbāhyato'pāṃgau tatrāndhyaṃ dṛṣṭyupaghāto vā।। 120।।
bhruvorantaropari karṇalalāṭayormadhye śaṃkhau tatra sadyo maraṇam।। śaṃkhayorupari keśānte utkṣepau।। tatra saśalyo jīvatyu ddhṛtaśalyo mriyate।। 121।।
bhruvormadhye sthaphaṇī tatrotkṣepavat।। pañca sandhayaḥ śirasi vibhaktāḥ sīmantāḥ।। tatronmādabhayacittanāśairmaraṇam।। 122।।
ghrāṇaśrotrākṣijihvāsantarpiṇīnāṃ śirāṇāṃ madhye śirasaḥ tannipātaḥ śṛṃgāṭakāni tāni catvāri marmmāṇi tatrāpi sadyaḥ mastakābhyantaropariṣṭācchirāsandhisannipāto romāvatto'dhipatistatrāpi sadya eva।। evametāni saptottaramarmmaśatāni।। 123।।
athaitat parimāṇam।। ūrvyaḥ śirāṃsi----- ca sakakṣapārśve ekaikamaṃgulimitāḥ stanapūrvamūlam।। viddhyaṃguladvayamitaṃ maṇibandhagulphaṃ trīṇyeva jānusaparaṃ sahakūrparābhyām ।। 124।।
hṛdbastikūrcagudanābhi vadanti mūrdhni catvāri pañcakagale daśa tāni ca dve।। tāni svapāṇitalakuñcitasammitāni śeṣāṇyavaihi parivistaratoṃ'gulārdham।। 125।।
jānusaparamiti dvitīyajānusahitaṃ trīṇyaṃgulāni viddhītyarthaḥ।। catvāri śṛṃgāṭakāni।। pañca sīmantāḥ।। gale daśa tāni ca dve।। dvādaśa।। aṣṭau mātṛkāḥ।। dve nīle dve ca manye।। 126।।
tāni svapāṇītyādi।। caturaṃgulamitānītyarthaḥ।। śeṣāṇi ṣaṭpañcāśanmarmmāṇyaṃgulārdhamitāni viddhīti।। kecittu।। imāni ṣaṭpañcāśanmarmmāṇi svapāṇitalakuñcitasammitāni caturaṃgulānyāhuḥ।। 127।।
apare tu stanamūle dve।। gulphe dve।। indrabastayaścatvāraḥ।। dvau pāṇibandhāviti daśadvayaṃgulamitāni।। jānunī dve।। anyāścatasraḥ।। kūrpare dve।। ityaṣṭau ।। 128।।
suśrute -
tataḥ prabhṛti sā vyāyāma vyavasāyavyavāyātitarpaṇadivāsvapnarātrijāgaraṇaśokabhayayānārohaṇavegadhāraṇakukkuṭāsanaśoṇitamokṣaṇāni pariharediti।। 129।।
sadyo gṛhītagarbhāyāḥ śramo glāniḥ pipāsā ca sakthispandanaṃ śukraśoṇitayoranubandhaḥ।। sphuraṇaṃ ca yonyā ityādi garbhalakṣaṇamuktaṃ tatraiva।। 130।।
punaryājñavalkyaḥ-
tasya ṣoḍhā śarīrāṇi ṣaṭ tvaco dhārayanti ca।।
ṣaḍaṃgāni tathāsthyāṃ ca saha ṣaṣṭyā śatatrayam।। 131।।
tasyātmanaḥ jarāyujāni।। prāyaśo'ṇḍajānyapi śarīrāṇi।। svasvakośasthāgni pacyamānānnarasapariṇāmabhūtaḥ saktamāṃsamedo'sthimajjāśukrabhedāt।। ṣaṭprakārāṇi ।। 133।।
etadevāha-
sthānaiḥ saha catuḥṣaṣṭirddantā vai viṃśatirnakhāḥ।।
pāṇipādaśalākāśca tāsāṃ sthānaṃ catuṣṭayam।। 134।।
sthānāni।। dantamūlapradeśasthānyasthīni dvātriṃśat।। taiḥ saha dvātriṃśad dantāḥ।। evaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ।। nakhāḥ viṃśatiḥ।। tanmūlasthāni śalākākārāṇi ca viṃśatiḥ।। evaṃ catvāriṃśat।। tāsāṃ nakhādyasthnāṃ karau vakṣastasya saptadaśa।। evaṃ trayoviṃśatiḥ।। 23।। evaṃ ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayam।। 360।। 135।।
tathā-
gandharūparasasparśaśabdāśca viṣayāḥ smṛtāḥ।।
nāsikā locane jihvā tvak śrotraṃ cendriyāṇi tu।।
ṣiñ sambandhane dhāturato viṣayabandhakān।। ityuktam।। 136।।
hastau pāyurupasthaṃ ca jihvā pādau ca pañca vai।।
karmendriyāṇi jānīyānmanaścaivobhayātmakam।। 137।।
nābhirojo gudaṃ śakraṃ śoṇitaṃ śaṃkhakau tathā।।
mūrdhāṃsakaṃ ca hṛdayaṃ prāṇasyāyatanāni tu।। 138।।
prāṇāyatanāni vistārayati-
vapāvasāvahananaṃ nābhiklomayakṛtplīhā।।
kṣudrāntraṃ vṛvakakau bastipurīṣādhānameva ca।। 139।।
āmāśayo'tha hṛdayaṃ sthūlātraṃ guda eva ca।।
udaraṃ ca gudau koṣṭhau vistāro'yamudāhṛtaḥ।। 140।।
vapā prasiddhā।। vasā māṃsasnehaḥ।। avahananaṃ puppusaḥ।। plīhā āyurvede prasiddhaḥ।। tau ca māṃsapiṇḍākārau savyakukṣigau।। yakṛt।। kālikāklomamāṃsapiṇḍastau ca dakṣiṇakukṣigau। 141।।
vṛkkakau hṛdayasamīpasthau māṃsapiṇḍau bastiḥ mūtrāśayaḥ purīṣādhānaṃ purīṣāśayaḥ।। bāhyād gudavalayādantargudavalaye dve।। tau gudau koṣṭhau।। 142।।
kānīnike cākṣikūṭe।। śaṣkulīkarṇapatrakau karṇapālyau।। dantā dve'ṣṭau।। dantapālyau।। kakundare।। jaghanakūpakau।। vaṃkṣaṇa jaghanorusandhi vṛkkau pūrvoktau ।। tau ca śleṣmasandhajau।। 143।।
upajihvikā ghaṇṭikā।। sphijau kaṭiprothau jaṃghayorūrvośca piṇḍikā।। māṃsalacaraṇau ceti sthānacatuṣṭayam।। evaṃ caturuttaraṃ śatam।। 144।।
kiṃ ca ṣaṣṭayaṃgulīnāṃ dve pārśve gulpheṣu ca catuṣṭayam 4।। catvāryaralikāsthīni jaṃghayostāvadeva tu।। viṃśatyaṃgulīnāṃ ekaikasyāstrīṇi trīṇityevam।। 145।।
aṃgulīsambandhānyasthīni ṣaṣṭirbhavanti।। pādayoḥ paścimau bhāgau pārṣṇī tayoḥ asthīni dve।। gulpheṣu catuṣṭayam।। evaṃ ṣaṭ bāhvoraralipramāṇāni catvāri।। jaṃghayorapi catvāryeva evaṃ catuḥ saptatiḥ।। 74।। 146।।
