dve dve jānukapoloruphalakāṃsasamudbhave।। akṣatālūṣake śroṇiphalake ca vinirddiśet।। jaṃghorusandhirjānuḥ kapolau galla ūruḥ sakthi।। tatphalakam aṃso bhujamūlam ।। 147।।
akṣakarṇanetrayormadhye śaṃkhādayo'dhobhāge।। tālūṣakam।। kākudam।। śroṇīkakudminī।। tatphalakam।। teṣāmekaikatrāsthinī dve dve evaṃ caturddaśa।। 148।।
bhagākhyekaṃ tathā pṛṣṭhe catvāriṃśacca pañca grīvāyāṃ pañcadaśāsthiḥ syājjatvekaṃ ca tathā hanuḥ।। ekaṃ guhye pañcacatvāriṃśat pṛṣṭhe grīvāyāṃ pañcadaśa vakṣīṃsayoḥ sandhirjatruḥ pratijatruḥ।। 149।।
ekaikaṃ hanuścibukaṃ tatrāpyekaṃ evaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ।। tanmūle dve lalāṭākṣigaṇḍe nāsācca nāsikā।। pārśvakāḥ sthālakaiḥ sārddhamarbudeśca dvisaptatiḥ।। 150।।
tasyā hanormūle'sthīni dve।। lalāṭe akṣikapolākhyamadhyama pradeśo gaṇḍaḥ।। tatra ca pratyekamasthiyugalaṃ nāsāvajñakāsthinī pārśvakāḥ kakṣādhaḥ pradeśasthānyasthīni।। 151।।
tadādhārabhūtāni sthālakāni taiḥ sthālakairarbudeścāsthiviśeṣaiḥ saha pārśvakā dvisaptatiḥ।। pūrvoktaiśca navabhiḥ saha ekaśītiḥ।। 81।। 152।।
dvau śaṃkhakau kapālāni catvāri śirasastathā।।
uraḥ saptadaśāsthīni puruṣasyāsthi saṃgrahaḥ।। 153।।
bhrūkarṇayormadhyadeśāsthiviśeṣau śaṃkhakau।। śirasaścatvāri uropradeśaḥ।। galaśuṇḍike।। hanumūlagalayoḥ sandhiḥ ।। śīrṣaśiraḥ।। 154।।
pravaṭaḥ yaḥ kaścana śarīre kaṇṭhamūlakakṣādinimnaḥ deśaḥ।। tathā akṣṇoḥ kanīnikāyāḥ pratyekaṃ pārśvadvayaṃ śvetamiti vartmacatuṣṭayaṃ tathā।। 155।।
akṣiyugalaṃ karṇayugalaṃ nāsāvivaradvayam।। āsyaṃ pāyūpasthamityetāni nava chidrāṇi prāṇāyatanāni ceti।।156।।
śirāśatāni saptaiva nava snāyuśatāni ca।।
dhamanīnāṃ śate dve tu pañca peśīśatāni vai।। 157।।
śirānābhisambaddhāścatvāriṃśatsaṃkhyā vātapittaśleṣmavāhinyaḥ sakalakalevakhyāpinyo nānāśākhāḥ satyaḥ saptaśatasaṃkhyā bhavanti।। 158।।
tathā।। aṃgapratyaṃgasandhibandhanāḥ snāyavo navaśatāni।। dhamanyo nābherudbhūtāścaturviśatisaṃkhyāḥ prāṇādivāyuvāhinyaḥ।। śākhābhedena dviśataṃ bhavanti ।। 159।।
peśyaḥ māṃsalākārā ūrupiṇḍikādyaṃgapratyaṃgasambandhinyaḥ pañcaśatāni।। āsāmeva śirādīnāṃ śākhāprācuryeṇa saṃkhyāntaramāhaḥ।। 160।।
ekonatriṃśallakṣāṇi tathā navaśatāni ca।।
ṣaṭpañcāśacca jānīte śiradhamanisantatiḥ।। 161।।
kiñca-
trayo lakṣāstu vijñeyā śmaśrukeśāḥ śarīriṇām।।
saptottaraṃ marmaśataṃ dve ca sandhaiśate tvatha।। 162।।
śmaśrukeśayormiliteyaṃ saṃkhyā।। asthnāṃ dve sandhiśate।। snāyuśirādisandhayastvanantā romṇāṃ koṭyastu pañcāśat catasra koṭya eva ca।। 163।।
saptaṣaṣṭistathā lakṣāḥ sārddhā svedāyanai) vāyavīvairvigaṇyanta ityarthaḥ।। etacca cakṣurādyagocaratvād durjñeyamapi śāstradṛṣṭyā proktaṃ yaḥ kaścidanuvetti sa dhanya iti bhāvaḥ ।। 164।।
rasādiparimāṇamāha-
rasasya nava vijñeyā jalasyāñjalayo daśa।।
saptaiva tu purīṣasya raktasyāṣṭau prakīrttitāḥ।। 165।।
ṣaṭ śleṣmā paṃca pittaṃ tu catvāro mūtrameva ca।।
vasā trayo dvau medo majjaikorddhaṃ tu mastake।। 166।।
śleṣmaṇo rajasastāva dretasastāvadeva tu।।
ityevamasthiraṃ brahma yasya mokṣāya kṛtyasau।। 167।।
pārthivaparamāṇusaṃśleṣanimittasya jalasyāñjalayo daśa।। pariṇatāhārarasaparimāṇam evaṃ mastake'rddhāñjalimajjāśleṣmarasasya retasaścārddho'ñjalireva parimāṇam।। 168।।
etacca samadhātuśarīraviṣayam।। viṣamadhātostu na niyamaḥ।।
taduktamāyurvede-
vailakṣaṇyaṃ śarīrāṇāmasthāpitatvāttathaiva ca।।
doṣadhātumalānāṃ tu parimāṇaṃ na vidyate।। 169।।
atropāsyasvarūpamāha-
dvā saptatisahasrāṇi hṛdayādabhiniḥ sṛtāḥ।।
hitā nāma hi tā nāḍyaḥ tāsāṃ madhye śaśiprabhām।। 170।।
maṇḍalaṃ tasya madhyastha ātmā dīpa ivācalaḥ।।
sa jñeyastadviditveha punarājāyate na hi।। 171।।
suṣumnāsthamātmajyotirvijñeyamityarthaḥ।।
ananyaviṣayaṃ kṛtvā mano buddhiṃ smṛtīndriyam।।
dhyeya ātmā sthito yo'sau hṛdi dīpa iva prabhuḥ।। 172।।
tadetadevātmano dhyeyatvaṃ yaccittavṛtterbahirviṣayatā sati tiraskāreṇātmapravaṇatā nāma।। śaravasampuṭaniruddhaprabhātasya pradīpasyaikaniṣṭhatvamiva।। 173।।
dhyānasamaye vicāraṇīyamāha।। atītārthasmṛtiḥ।। kasya ko vā svapnādikārakaḥ।। jātirūpavayovṛttavidyādibhiralaṃkṛtaḥ।। śabdādiviṣayo'pyeṣa manasā karmaṇā girā।। iti ।। 174।।
atha viśeṣataḥ prāṇāyāmopayogī yājñavalkgītoktaṃ śārīramabhidhīyate- tatra prāyaśaḥ sarveṣāṃ svāṃgulibhiḥ ṣaṇṇavatyaṃgulaṃ śarīram।। tato dvādaśāṃgulaḥ prāṇaḥ ।। 175।।
sa ca svadehamadhyasthaśikhinā ekīkṛtya dehemadhye'maratvādivāñchatā dharttavyaḥ। śikhisthānaṃ tu gudād dvayaṃgulopari।। meḍhrād dvayaṃgulādadhaḥ।। 176
taptajāmbūnadaprabhaṃ trikoṇaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ hṛnmadhyasthaṃ caturasraṃ tiraścāṃ tundamadhyasthaṃ maṇḍalaṃ pakṣiṇām।। tata ārabhyopari navāṃgule kandasthānam।। 177।।
kandaścaturaṃgulavyāyāmo tsedhaśca aṇḍākṛtistvagādi bhūṣitaḥ tanmadhye dehapratiṣṭhāhetunābhicakram।। tatra cakre puṇyapāpapraṇoditaḥ adhasthaprāṇārūḍhaḥ tantupañjaramadhyastho lūtika iva jīvo bhramate।। 178।।
tasyordhvaṃ kuṇḍalinīśaktirnabhestiryagadhordhvataḥ।। aṣṭaprakṛtirūpā sā cāṣṭadhā kuṇḍalākṛtiḥ।। 179।।
yathāvadvāyusañcāraṃ jalānnādīni nityaśaḥ।।
paritaḥ kandapārśve tu nirudhyaiva sadā sthitā।। 180।।
mukhenaiva samāveṣṭya brahmarandhramukhaṃ tathā।।
yogakāle tvapānena prabodhaṃ yāti sāgninā।। 181।।
sphurantī hṛdayākāśe nāgarūpā mahojvalā।।
vāyurvāyusakhenaiva tato yāti suṣumnayā।। 182।।
kandamadhye sthitā nāḍī suṣumneti prakīrttitā।।
tiṣṭhanti paritastasyāścakre'sminnāḍīsaṃjñakāḥ।। 183।।
tatra kande dvāsaptati sahasrasaṃkhyakā nāḍya utpannāstāsāṃ madhye iḍā 1 piṃgalā 2 suṣumnā 3 sarasvatī 4 vāruṇā 5 pūṣā 6 hastijihvā 7 yaśasvinī 8 viśvodarā 9 kuhū 10 śaṃkhinī 11 payasvinī 12 alambuṣā 13 gāndhārī 14 nāḍikāścaturddaśa mukhyāḥ ।। tāsāmapyādyāstisraḥ।। tāsāmapi suṣumnā muktikāriṇī viśvadhāriṇi mahottamā।। 184।।
sā ca kandamadhye pṛṣṭhamadhyasthenāsthnā saha vīṇātanturiva vīṇādaṇḍena saha mūrdhni brahmarandhre sthitā।। vāme iḍā yasyā sudhāmayaḥ sudhāṃśurāste।। 185।।
dakṣiṇe piṃgalā yasyāṃ viṣamayo ravirāste।। etāveva tamorajorūpau rātrau dine kalpayataḥ।। suṣumnā tu kālabhoktrī satvamayī vahnidevatākā।। 186।।
sarasvatī kuhūścaiva suṣumnā pārśvayoḥ sthite।।
gāndhārī hastijihvā ca iḍāyāḥ pṛṣṭhapūrvayoḥ।। ‍187।।
kuhośca hastijihvāyā madhye viśvodarā sthitā।।
yathāsvinī kuhūrmadhye vāruṇā ca pratiṣṭhitā।। 188।।
pūrvabhāge suṣumnāyā meḍhrāntaṃ ca kuhūḥ sthitā।।
adhaścordhvaṃ ca vijñeyā vāruṇā sarvagāminī।। 189।।
yaśasvanī ca yāmyasya pādāṃguṣṭhāntamiṣyate।।
piṃgalā cordhvagā yāmye nāsāntaṃ viddhi me priye।। 190।।
yāmye pūṣā ca netrāntā piṃgalāyāśca pṛṣṭhataḥ।।
payasvinī tathā gārgi yāmye karṇāntamiṣyate।। 191।।
sarasvatī tathā cordhvamājihvāyāḥ pratiṣṭhitā।।
āsavyakarṇā dviprendre śaṃkhinī cordhvagāminī ।।192।।
gāndhārī savyanetrāntamīḍāyāḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ sthitā।।
iḍā ca savyanāsāntā savyabhāge vyavasthitā।। 193।।
hastijihvā tathā savyapādāṃguṣṭhāntamiṣyate।।
viśvodarā tu yā nāḍī tundamadhye vyavasthitā।। 194।।
alambuṣā mahābhāge tundamadhyādadhomukhī।।
etāsvanyāḥ samutpannāḥ śirāsvanyāśca tāsvapi।। 195।।
yathāśvatthadale tadvadabjapatreṣu vā śirāḥ।।
nāḍīṣvetāsu sarvāsu vijñātavyāstapodhane।। 196।।
prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaścodānaśca vyāna eva ca।।
nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkaro devadatto dhanañjayaḥ।।
ete nāḍīṣu sarvāsu caranti dasa vāyavaḥ।। 197।।
eṣu prāṇādayaḥ pañca mukhyāsteṣvapi prāṇāpānau tayorapi ca prāṇa eva mukhyaḥ।।
āsyanāsikayormadhye hṛnmadhye nābhimaṇḍale।।
prāṇālaya iti proktaṃ pādāṃguṣṭhe'pi kecana।। 198।।
ādhaścordhvaṃ ca kuṇḍalyāḥ paritaḥ prāṇasaṃjñakaḥ।।
tiṣṭhannetāsu tisṛṣu prakāśaṃ yāti dīpavat।। 199।।
apānanilayaṃ kecid gudameḍhrorujānuṣu।।
udare vṛṣaṇe kaṭyāṃ jaṃghe nābhau vadanti hi।। 200।।
gudāgyrādhārayostiṣṭhanmadhye'pānaḥ prabhañjanaḥ।।
sthāneṣveteṣu satataṃ prakāśayati dīpavat।। 201।।
vyānaḥ śrotrākṣimadhye ca kṛkaṭyāṃ gulphayorapi।।
ghrāṇe gale sphijau deśe tiṣṭhatyatra na saṃśayaḥ।। 202।।
udānaḥ sarvasandhisthaḥ pādayorhastayorapi।।
samānaḥ sarvagātreṣu sarvaṃ vyāpya vyavasthitaḥ।। 203।।
bhuktaṃ sarvarasaṃ gātre vyāpayannagninā saha।।
dvisaptatisahasreṣu nāḍīmārgeṣu sañcaran।। 204।।
samānavāyurevaikaḥ sāgnirvyāpya vyavasthitaḥ।।
agnibhiḥ saha sarvatra sāṃgopāṃge kalevare।। 205।।
nāgādivāyavaḥ pañca tvagasthyādiṣu saṃsthitāḥ।।
tundasthaṃ jalamannaṃ ca rasānapi samīkṛtaḥ।। 206।।
tundamadhyagataḥ prāṇastāni kuryāt pṛthak pṛthak।।
uparyagnau jalaṃ sthāpya tvannādīni jalopari।। 207।।
svayaṃ tvapānaṃ samprāpya tenaiva saha mārutaḥ।।
prayāti jvalanaṃ tatra tena madhyagataḥ punaḥ।। 208।।
vāyunā prerito vahnirapānena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ।।
tato jvalati viprendra svakule dehamadhyage।। 209।।
jvālābhirjvalanastatra prāṇena preritastadā।।
karoti jalamatyuṣṇaṃ koṣṭamadhyagataṃ punaḥ।। 210।।
annaṃ vyañjanasaṃyuktaṃ jalopari niveśitam।।
tatastu pakvamakarod vahniḥ santaptavāriṇā।। 211।।
svedamūtre jale syātāṃ vīryarūpaṃ raso bhavet।।
purīṣamannaṃ syād gārgi prāṇaḥ kuryāt pṛthak pṛthak।। 212।।
samānavāyunā sārddhaṃ rasaṃ sarvāṣu nāḍīṣu।।
vyāpayan śvāsarūpeṇa dehe carati mārutaḥ।। 213।।
deharadhaiśca navabhirviṇmūtrādivisarjanam।।
kurvanti vāyavaḥ sarve śarīreṣu nirantaram।। 214।।
niḥśvāso cchvāsakāsāstu prāṇakarmeti kīrttyate।।
apānavāyoḥ karmeti viṇmūtrādivisarjanam।। 215।।
dānopādānaceṣṭādi vyānakarmeti cocyate।।
udānakarma tatproktaṃ dehasyonnayanādi yat।। 216।।
devadattāttu tandrā syājjṛmbhādistu dhanañjayāt।।
jñātvaivaṃ nāḍīsaṃsthānaṃ vāyusaṃsthānakarmaṇi।।
vidhinoktena mārgeṇa nāḍīsaṃśodhanaṃ kuru।। 217।।
atha bhāvanāviśeṣopayogi śārīraṃ nānāvidhopaniṣatsu gītaṃ svīkṛtaṃ taddarśibhirmahāyogibhirlikhitaṃ ca śrīśaṃkarācāryaprabhṛtibhiḥ svasvagranthe'bhidhīyate।। 218।।
tatra yadyapi brahmamayatvādinā sarvameva tīrthaṃ pratilomakūpaṃ ca tīrthāni pratipāditāni tadvedibhistathāpi prādhānyena yuñjānasya prathamabhāvanāyāṃ tīrthayātrārthaṃ kiñcidviśiṣyocyate ।। 219।।
mardhni ayodhyā।। hṛdaye mathurā।। mūlādhāre māyā।। bhrūmadhye kāśī।। svādhiṣṭhāne kāñcī।। maṇipūre avantī।। kaṇṭhe dvārāvatī।। nāsāgre prayāgaḥ।। iḍāyāṃ gaṃgā।। piṃgalāyāṃ yamunā।। suṣumnāyāṃ sarasvatī।। anyāsu nāḍīṣu narmadādyā sarvā api saritaḥ santīti tatra tatrādhārādheyabhāvenābhedena vā tattarattīrthaṃ bhāvyam।। 22¬0।।
evaṃ ca śirasī।। kaṇṭhādadho nābhiparyantam।। nābheradhaścaraṇaparyantaṃ grāmatrayam।। śiraḥśmaśruhṛdayakakṣādiṣu salomakeṣu deśeṣu caturddaśāraṇyāni।। lalāṭādiṣu lomaśūnyeṣu navo svarāśca santi।। puryādisavidhe vopapuryādīni santīti sarvatārthamayaṃ śarīram।। 221।।
atha devayātrārthaṃ śarīre devā abhidhīyante।। caitanyānandayoḥ puruṣaḥ śivarūpī।। satvādiguṇeṣu prakṛtiḥ śaktirūpiṇī।। tadubhayopādānakatvāt tanmayaṃ sarvam।। 222।।
evaṃ ca citte vāsudevaḥ।। buddhau caturmukhaḥ।। ahaṃ kāre śaṃkaraḥ।। manasi candraḥ।। śrotre dik।। tvaci vāyuḥ।। cakṣuṣī sūryaḥ।। tatra dakṣiṇe'kṣiṇi yaḥ puruṣa rūpaḥ sa indrasvarūpaḥ īśvaraḥ।। vāme yaḥ sa indrāṇī svarūpā īśvarī।। bhrūmadhye ca tayoḥ sammilanā ttatraiva nayanatejorūpaṃ bhāvanīyau।। tau dampatī।। 223।।
evaṃ ca rasanāyāṃ varuṇaḥ।। ghrāṇe'śvinīkumārau।। vāci agniḥ।। pāṇayoḥ parākramādau cendraḥ।। pādayorupendraḥ ।। pāyau yamaḥ।। upasthe prajāpatirastīti।। 224।।
tatra tatrādhārādheyabhāvenānugrāhyānugrāhaka bhāvenābhedena vā sā sā devatā bhāvyā।। evaṃ ca tattadvyāpāra phalayoḥ pūrṇatā bhavati।। 225।।
atha pūrṇāhambhāvasiddhihetuśarīracaturddaśabhuvanabhāvanārthaṃ tānyucyante।। 226।।
pādamūle pātālam।। pārṣṇiprapadayoḥ rasātalam।। gulphadvaye mahātalam।। jaṃghādvaye talātalam।। jānudvaye sutalam।। ūrudvaye vitalam atalaṃ ca।। jaghane mahītalam।। nābhau bhūrlokam।। udare bhuvarlokam।। urasi svarlokam।। grīvāyāṃ maharlokam।। vadane janalokam।। lalāṭe tapolokam।। mūrdhni satyalokam।। bāhvādiṣu śakrādidevatāścaivaṃ sarvamayaṃ svavapurīśvarī sattayā bhāvayet।। 227।।
atha varṇayatvaṃ taduktaṃ bhuvaneśvarīstutau-
ādyo maulirathāparo mukhami ī netre ca karṇāmbu ū।।
nāsāvaṃśapuṭe ṛ ṛ tadanujau varṇau kapoladvayam।। 228।।
dantāścordhvamadhastatho'ṣṭayugalaṃ sandhyakṣarāṇi kramāt।।
jihvāmūlamudagrabinduraparo grīvāvisargīśvaraḥ।। 229।।
kādirddakṣiṇato bhujastadaparo vargaśca vāmo bhujaḥ।।
ṣṭādistādiranukrameṇa caraṇau kukṣidvayaṃ te paphau।। 230।।
vaṃśaḥ pṛṣṭhabhavo'tha nābhihṛdaye vāditrayaṃ dhātavo।।
yādyāḥ saptasamīraṇāśca saparaḥ kṣaḥ krodha ityambike।। 231।।
mātṛkānyāsoktasthalena tadvarṇadhyānamiti phalitārthaḥ।।
evaṃ varṇamayaṃ vapustava śive lokatraya vyāpakam।।
yo'haṃ bhāvanayā japatyavayaveṣvāropitairakṣaraiḥ।। 232।।
mūrttibhūya dināvasānakamalākāraiḥ śiraḥ pāpibhistaṃ vidyāḥ samupāsate karatalairdṛṣṭipradānotsukāḥ।। 233।।
atha ṣaṭcakrāṇi।। tatra gudād dvayaṃgulopari meḍhrād dvayaṃgulādadhaḥ kandasthānam।। tadupari taptacāmīkaraprabhaṃ trikoṇaṃ kāmarūpam।। 234।।
tattrikoṇasya vāme koṇe iḍā।। dakṣiṇe piṃgalā।। madhye suṣumnā।। madhye citriṇī varṇamayī tadvarṇavinyāsād kamalākāracakraniṣpattistathā hi।। 235।।
prathamataḥ kāmarūpopari mūlādhāranāmakaṃ cakram।। tacca suvarṇābham।। laṃ bijānvitena sāvitrīsahitabrahmasarasvatīsahitagaṇeśādyadhisthitena।। ṣaṭpañcāśat śivaśaktimithunavatā catuḥkoṇena pṛthvīsvarūpeṇa karṇikākāreṇa madhye bhūṣitam।। vaśasātmakacaturddalam।। tanmadhye bhāsvarākāram adhomukhaṃ citsvarūpiṇyā sārddhatribalayākāreṇa sthitayā vidyutprabhayā kuṇḍalinyā svamukhena tanmukhaṃ mudrayitvā veṣṭitaṃ svayambhūnāmakaṃ śivaliṃgam।। 236।।
etacca bhāvitaṃ sakalayogabhogasiddhiḥ pṛthvījayādikaṃ brahmagaṇeśādiprītiṃ ca viracayati।। 237।।
yacca yājñavalkyena kuṇḍalinyā nābhau sthalamuktaṃ tadetasyā evordhvagatyā sthūlībhūtāyāḥ kiñcit prākaṭyena yoginaḥ sujñeyatārthaṃ tatrāpyetadrūpāyāstasyāḥ satvāt ।। 238।।
evaṃ ca pṛthvyādīnāṃ hṛdayādiṣu yadgorakṣanāthādinā sthalamuktaṃ tadapi matāntaram।। yogināṃ mate sarvatra sarmasti bhāvanādipratyakṣasāmagrītaḥ sthānaviśeṣeṣu pratyakṣamiti sarvamapi satyam।। bhāvanātaḥ sarvasiddheḥ।। 239।।
evaṃ citkalāyā hṛdaye'dhiṣṭhānaṃ śrutyādiprasiddhaṃ na viruddhyate।। mūlādhārādatthitāyāstasyā hṛdaye prakaṭībhāvāditi।। 240।।
evamevānyatrāpi yogaśāstreṣu viruddhatvena pratibhātamavirodhena bodhyaṃ guruprasādāditi dik ।। 241।।
tadupari liṃgamūle svādhiṣṭhānanāmakaṃ cakram।। tacca sanmāṇikyasamaprabham।। vaṃbījānvitena।। lakṣmīsahitaviṣṇvādyadhiṣṭhitena dvipañcāśanmithunavatā।। 242।।
ardhacandrākāreṇa śubhreṇa jalasvarūpeṇa karṇikottamena madhye bhūṣitam।। vabhamayaralātmakaṣaḍdalam।। bhāvukānāṃ viṣṇuprītijala jayādikārakam ।। 243।।
atha nābhau maṇipūrakaṃ nāmakaṃ cakram।। tacca vidyutprabhaṃ raṃ-bījānvitena pārvatīsahitaśaṃkarādyadhiṣṭhitena।। dvisamadhikaṣaṣṭhisaṃkhyakamithunavatā।। 244।।
trikoṇena udyatādityaprabhena tejaḥsvarūpeṇa karṇikottamena madhye bhūṣitaṃ ḍādiphāntavarṇātamakaṃ daśadalaṃ svabhāvukānāṃ bhavānīśaṃkaraprītitejojayādikārakam।। 245।।
atha hṛdaye anāhatanāmakaṃ cakram।। ete devā ekādaśendriyapañcatanmātrakaṣoḍaśavikārādhārādhiṣṭhitayā ṣoḍaśāraṣoḍaśādhārādināmakatvenāpyāgamādau prasiddham।। 246।।
tacca dhūmravarṇam।। maṃ-bījānviteva prakṛtisahiteśvarādyadhiṣṭhitena catuḥpañcāśanmithunavatā ṣaṭkoṇena bāṇanāmakaśivaliṃgalakṣitena।। vāyusvarūpeṇa karṇikottamena madhye bhūṣitam।। kādiṭhāntavarṇātmakadvādaśadalam।। svabhāvukānāṃ prakṛtīśvara prativāyujayādikārakam।। kaṇṭhe viśuddhanāmakaṃ cakram।। tacca śvetam aṃbījānvitena।। 247।।
arddhanārīśvarasvarūpasadāśivādyadhiṣṭhitena dvisaptatimithunavatāvṛttākāreṇa śuddhāṃśuprabheṇa nabhaḥsvarūpeṇa karṇikottamena madhye bhūṣitam।। svarīyavarṇātmaka ṣoḍaśadalam।। bhāvukānāṃ sadāśivaprītijayādikārakam।। 248।।
bhrūmadhye ājñānāmakaṃ cakram।। tacca muktākāraṃ haṃ-bījānvitena avidyāsahitajīvādyadhiṣṭhitena śṛṃgāṭakākāreṇa catuḥṣaṣṭistrīpuruṣavatā manorūpeṇa karṇikottamena madhye bhūṣitam।। hakāralakārātmaka varṇadvayadalam।। bhairavānandanāmnā itareṇa śivaliṃgenopalakṣitam।। svabhāvukānām ātmaprītimanojayādikārakam।। 249।।
pādādijānuparyantaṃ pūrvoktaṃ laṃ-bījānvitatvabrahmādhiṣṭhitatvādi viśeṣeṇa viśiṣṭaṃ pṛthvīsthānam।। evamājānvoḥ pāyuparyantam apāṃ sthānam।। āpāyoḥ hṛdayāntaṃ tejaḥ sthānam।। āhṛnmadhyāt bhrūmadhyaparyantaṃ vāyusthānam।। ābhrūmadhyānmūrddhaparya-ntamākāśasthānamiti।। yājñavalkyagītāyāmuktaṃ sarvatra sarvasvāṃgīkārāt।। 250।।
tattadvasthadoṣopapatteraviruddhaṃ veditavyam।। mūrdhni sahasradalakamalam।। tacca śaśiprabhasya guroḥ paramātmanaḥ sthanām।। tadupari ahaṃkārasya।। lalāṭopari buddhitatvasya।। tadupari prakṛteḥ।। 251।।
sahasradale puruṣasya sthānamiti prapañcitam।। ..... athānāhatasavidhakandamadhyāt samucchritaṃ dvādaśaāṃgulanālaṃ caturaṃgulam।। adhomukhamapyūrdhvamukhatayā bhāvyaṃ daharaṃ nāmakamaṣṭadalaṃ kamalam।। 252।।
tatra prāṇāpānapreritaḥ pūrvakarmavāsanābaddho vijñānāsamartho jāvo divāniśaṃ bhramate ।। 253।।
tatra bhramaṇakramaḥ।। prathamaṃ pūrvadalasya vāmārddhe mūlamārabhya patrāgraparyantaṃ triṃśacchvāsaparyantam।। ākāśatatvamāśritya bhramate।। āṣaṣṭiśvāsaparyantaṃ vāyutatvam।। navatiśvāsān tejastatvam।। viṃśatyadhikaśataśvāsān vāritatvam।। pañcāśadadhikaśataśvāsān pṛthvītatvam।। 254।।
evaṃ sa ekasmin patrārddhe sārddhacatuḥśataśvāsā bhavanti।। evamaparārddhe patrāgrādārabhya mūlaparyantaṃ prathamaṃ sārddhaśataśvāsān pṛthvītatvam।। tataḥ viṃśatyadhikaśataśvāsān vāritatvam।। 255।।
navatiśvāsān tejastatvam।। ṣaṣṭiśvāsādvāyutatvam।। triṃśatśvāsān ākāśattvamāśritya bhramate।। evaṃ sampūrṇapatre navaśatāni śvāsānāṃ bhavanti।। 256।।
evameva dalāṣṭakabhramaṇe śatadvayādhikasaptasahasrasaṃkhkā śvāsā bhavanti।। evaṃvidhabhramaṇena vāratrayamahorātreṇa bhramate।। 257।।
taduktam - aharniśoddhibhramo'treti tena ṣaṭśatādhika ekaviṃśatisahasrasaṃkhyakāḥ śvāsāḥ bhavanti।। gorakṣanātho yadāha--।। 258।।
ṣaṭśatāni divārātrau sahasrāṇyekaviṃśatiḥ।।
haṃsa haṃseti yanmantraṃ jīvo japati sarvadā।।
hakāreṇa bahiryāti sakāreṇa viśet punaḥ।। ityādi।। 259।।
etāvadbhiḥ śvāsaiḥ ṣaṣṭighaṭikā bhavanti।। tathāhi-yāvatā kālena daśagurvakṣarāṇi ucyante tāvatā kālena sādhāraṇanasya prāyaśaḥ ekaḥ śvāso bhavati।। 260।।
ṣaḍbhiḥ śvāsairekaḥ palo bhavati।। ṣaṣṭayadhikaśatatrayasaṃkhyakaiḥ śvāsairekā ghaṭikā।। ṣaṣṭighaṭikābhirahorātram।। ṣaṣṭayadhikaśatatrayāhorātrairvatsaraḥ।। 261।।
evaṃ pratipuruṣaṃ śvāsanirgamasamayavaicitryādvatsarādipṛthagiti vācyam।। ata eva prāṇāyāmairāryuvṛddhiḥ।। ata eva kalpāyuṣo'pi yoginaḥ śatavarṣāyuṣaḥ procyante।। 262।।
asmatkalpasya tadīyaśvāsaghaṭitaṃ saṃvatsaraśatakatvāditi।। jyotissiddhāyurvido'pi etatkrameṇaiva।। ata eva kadācit sādhāraṇavatsarakrameṇa sammilanīyamiti।। 263।।
yathā yathā prāṇo jīvaḥ pañcatatvāni samāśritya hṛdayakamale bhramate tathā jñānāya hṛdayacakramidaṃ likhyate।। atha pūrvādidigdalacare śvasena yādṛśī manovṛttirbhavati tādṛśī ।। 264।।
tatra tatra śvasanasañcāra jñānāyocyante।। 265।।
tathā ca haṃsopaniṣadi-
yo'sau so'haṃ paramahaṃso bhānukoṭipratīkāśastasyāṣṭadhāvṛttirbhavati।। pūrvadale puṇye matiḥ।। āgneye nidrālasyādayo bhavanti।। yāmye kraurye matiḥ।। naiṛtye pāpe manīṣā।। vāruṇyāṃ krīḍā।। vāyavye gamanāgamanādau buddhiḥ saumye ratiprītiḥ।। aiśānye dravyadānam।। madhye vairāgyam।। kesare jāgradavasthā।। karṇikāyāṃ svapnaḥ।। liṃge suṣuptiḥ।। padmatyāge turīyamityādi।। 266।।
samarasāre'pi-
indrādidigdalacare śvasane raṇāya bhoktuṃ ruṣetha viṣayāya mude gamāya।।
ceto bhavetkṛpayituṃ ca nṛpāsyadāya patradvāyāntaraṃcaretu caretu mude parasyai।। 267।।
parasyai brahmānandasandohabhavāyai।। ata eva kṛtābhyāsā yoginaśca ākāśatatvavahanasamanantarameva patrāntara saṃgatāvāntarakālīne patradvayāntaraśca śvasanasañcāre jāyamāne kṣaṇamātraṃ brahmānandodbodhe prādurbhūte tathā manaḥ sthirīkurvanti।। yathā patradvayāntara eva śvasanaviśiṣṭo jīvaḥ sthiro bhavati।। tataścānavarataṃ brahmānandodbodho bhavati ।। 268।।
ata eva ca aiśavaryādilakṣaṇendrādiguṇārthino yogino indrādidala eva śvasanaviśiṣṭaṃ sthirīkurvanti।। tatstharīkaraṇopāyasta।। tattaddale śvasanasañcārasamakālīnāyāḥ pūrvoktāyāstaddikpālānurūpāyā indro'hamityādidikpālāśca manovṛtteranavaratamāśrayaṇaṃ yāvaccendrādyanurūpā manovṛttirāśriyate tāvattattaddevadigdala eva śvasanaviśiṣṭo jīvastiṣṭhati।। 269।।
evameva vakṣyamāṇākāśādiguṇaviśiṣṭā yāvatkālaparyantaṃ manovṛttirbhavāti tāvatkālaparyantaṃ tattatva eva tathābhūto jīvastiṣṭhatīti।। gurupadeśasāram ।। 270।।
athākāśādi tatvavahanaparīkṣā yathāgurūpadeśasāram। ākāśāditatvavahanaparīkṣā yathāgurūpadeśamucyate।। 271
yadākāśatatvamāśritya jīvaḥ śvasanena preritaḥ prācīnakarmaviśeṣavāsanāviśeṣādibaddho rajjanaśyenādiriva bhramate।। tathā nāsāvivaranirgataḥ śvasanaḥ anuṣṇāśītasparśo varttulākāro dvādaśāṃgulādapi dīrghaḥ।। nāsāgrasthitakaratalādivyāpakāyāmo raktaśvetaśyāmādisaṃgitalo'dbhūta karburavarṇo jāyate।। nāsāgrasthite ca darpaṇe śvāsasambandhenotpannāyāḥ malinacchāyāyāḥ kramāddarpaṇamadhye saṃkocamāgacchantyā varttalākāratayā pariṇāmo varṇaśca karburaḥ sphuṭamabhivyajyata iti ।। 272।।
evaṃ vāyutatvavahane śvasano'sau anuṣṇāśītasparśo laghuḥ।। ṣaṭkoṇākāro navāṃgulo nāsāgrasthitakaratalādau tiryagvahannadhigato dhūmravarṇaśca jāyate।। pūrvavad darpaṇamalinacchāyāyāḥ ākuñcantyāḥ ṣaṭkoṇākāratā dhūmravarṇaśca prakaṭamupalakṣyate।। 273।।
evaṃ tejastatvavahane śvasan uṣṇo raktastrikoṇākāro ṣaḍaṃgulo nāsāgrasthitakaratalāderūrdhvabhāgasparśo jāyate।। darpaṇacchāyāyāśca raktatā trikoṇatā copalabhyate।। 274।।
evaṃ jalatatvavahane śvasanaḥ śītalo'rddhacandrākāraśvetavarṇaḥ।। ṣaḍaṃgulādapi nyūnagatiḥ।। nāsāgrasthitakaratalāderadhobhāgasparśo jāyate।। darpaṇamalinacchāyāyāścāddha-candrākāratā śvetavarṇaścopalakṣyate।। 275।।
pṛthvītatvavahane śvasanaḥ anuṣṇaśītaḥ pītavarṇaścatuṣkoṇo nāsāgrasthitakaratalādau madhye'bhivyaktagatiśca jāyate।। darpaṇamalinacchāyāyāstu catuṣkoṇatā pīto varṇa ścopalakṣyata ityādi gurūpadeśato jñeyam।। 276।।
atha prasaṃgāt kiñcidanyadapyucyate -
tataḥ pañcaghaṭikāḥ sūryādisaṃjñakā dakṣiṇanāḍī vahati।। tataścaturthīmārabhya ṣaṣṭiparyantaṃ prathamato dakṣiṇānāḍī tato vāmanāḍī।। 277।।
evaṃ ghaṭikāpañcakenāṣṭādaśatasaṃkhyā 1800 saṃkhyakaśvāsaghaṭitena patradvayasañcārādekanāḍīsañcāraḥ proktakrameṇa tithyārambhasamaye samucitaḥ syāt sa eva cejjāyate ।। 278।।
tadā tasyāṃ tithau kalyāṇaṃ jāyate।। viparīte tu viparītamiti।। evaṃ ca cāndrevahe nṛpavilokanādiśubhaṃ karma saphalaṃ bhavati।। sūryanāḍīvahe tu yuddhamāraṇoccāṭanādyaśubhaṃ siddhyati।। 279।।
taduktaṃ samarasāre -
candrevahe nṛpavilokanagehaveśapaṭṭabhiṣekamukhakarma bhavecchubhaṃ yat।।
saure tu majjanavadhūratibhuktiyuddhamukhyaṃ bhavedaśubhakarmaphalāya satyam।। 280।।
idaṃ tu dhyeyam।। yadi vāmanāḍīvahanasamaye bhojana maithunādidakṣiṇanāḍīvahanasamayaṃ karttavyaṃ bhavati śvāso vāmāyāṃ tu ruddho dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ vahati।। 281।।
evameva dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ vahantaṃ śvāsaṃ dakṣiṇapārśvaśayanādinā dakṣiṇanāḍīyantraṇaṃ vidhāya vāmanāḍyāmānīya vāmanāḍīvahanasamayaḥ karttavyaḥ।। 282।।
yātrāvaśīkaraṇagrīṣmadāhanivṛtyādisampādanīyam।। rātrau dakṣiṇānāḍyā divā vāmanāḍyā yadi śvāsaṃ sarvadā vāhayati yogī tadā kālaṃ jayati।। 283।।
rātrau śaityādinā divācarau hyādinā nābhibhūto bhavati।। anyacca-
arkke'gnitatvavahane harihelayāṃ pade ko'pi hanti subahūniti kiṃ vicitram।। śūnye ripūn svapṛtanāmapi vāhapakṣe niḥkṣipya vikṣipati lakṣamarīn kṣaṇena।। ‌284।।
yadā dakṣiṇanāḍyā tejastatvaṃ vahati tadā yodhaḥ svayameko'pi san śrīkṛṣṇa iva bahūn vairiṇo hanti।। kiñca vahata śūnyanāḍī diśi vairisenāṃ vahannāḍī diśi svasenāṃ ca sthāpayitvā yuddhaṃ kurvanneko'pi lakṣamarīn hantītyarthaḥ।। 285।।
atha praśnaḥ-
praśne candra vahe tu vāmaganareṇa prokto jayo niścitam।।
sūrye dakṣagate ca kṛcchravijayī śūnyasya dvate kṣatim।। 286।।
sūrye cedviṣamākṣarāṇi śaśini brūte samāni dhruvaṃ tadā jayaḥ।। 287।।
atha tasya bhogijanapriyaratyādyupayogo'pyucyate।। yadā aṅganāyāḥ vāmanāḍyāṃ vāritatvaṃ vahati puruṣasya dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ tejastatvaṃ tadā surate kriyamāṇe sā vahnisambandhanavanītavat dravate।। 288।।
taduktam-
vahati śaśini vā cedaṅganāyā narasya dyumaṇi manukṛśānustatra kāle rateṣu।।
sravati madanavāri nirjharaṃ sātha puṃsāṃ yadi śikhinavanītā śaktivadbhāvitā syāt ।। 289।।
vaśīkaraṇamapyucyate-
suptāyā nijavahaduṣṇaraśmināḍyā candraṃ cedvahanagataṃ pibettadānīm।।
āmṛtyorvaśayati tāmiyaṃ ca kāntaṃ candreṇa dyumaṇivahaṃ muhuḥ pibanti।। 290।।
śayanīye sthitāyā aṃganāyā vāmanāḍyāṃ vahantaṃ śvāsaṃ yadi puruṣaḥ svakīyadakṣiṇanāḍyā pibati tadā sā yāvajjīvaṃ tasya vaśyā bhavati।। 291।।
evaṃ ca aṃganā cet svavāmanāḍyā puruṣasya dakṣiṇanāḍīsthaṃ śvāsaṃ pibati tadā kāntaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ vaśayatītyarthaḥ।। 292।।
tasmādyoginā bhoginā cāpi mokṣasvargaihikasuratādibhogasarvajñatvadīrghāyuṣyabalapuṣṭisuṣṭyādisāmarthyārogyādikāmena।। cittaprāṇādisthairyakāmena niṣkāmena brahmajñānakāmena vā।। nāsā vivarābhyāṃ nirgacchati śarīramadhyaṃ ca praviśati sthirībhūte cāpānayukte prāṇe sāvadhānatayā maheśvarabhāvanāpūrvakaṃ mano deyam।। pūrvoktaparīkṣākrameṇa ca pañcatatvāni parīkṣyāṇīti śivam।। 293।।
iti śrīmaithilasanmiśraśrīkṛṣṇadevatanayasanmiśraśrībhavadevaviracite yuktabhavadevanāmni granthe śārīropadeśo nāma tṛtīyopadeśaḥ।। 3।।
ūm̐ gurucaraṇasarasiruhebhyo namaḥ।।

caturthopadeśaḥ
atha nirvikalpakasamādhisvarūpayogarājasyāṣṭāṃgānyucyante।। tāni ca yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasavikalpaka samādhisvarūpāṇi।। 1।।
taduktaṃ pātañjale-
yogāṃgānuṣṭhānādaśuddhikṣaye jñānadīptirāvivekakhyāteḥ।। yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāma pratyāhāradhāraṇādhyānasamādhayoṣṭāvaṃgāni।। 2।।
tatrāhiṃsā sarvathā sarvadā sarvabhūtānāma drohaḥ।। tatra hiṃsā tāvat।। kṛtakāritānumoditā ceti trividhā।। sāpi punarlobhakrodhamohahetukatvāt।। pratyekaṃ trividhā.।। lobhādayo'pi mṛdumadhyādhimātratvena trividhā।। ityevaṃ saptaviṃśatibhedāḥ।। 3।।
mṛdumadhyādhimātrāḥ punastrividhāḥ।। mṛdumṛduḥ madhyamṛduḥ tīvramṛdu iti।। tathā mṛdumadhyaḥ madhyamadhyaḥ tīvramadhyaḥ mṛdutīvro madhyatīvro'dhimātratīvraḥ।। ityekāśīti bhedāḥ।। 4।।
ahiṃsāpratiṣṭhāyāṃ tatsannidhau bhavati vairatyāgaḥ sarvaprāṇinām।। sarvaprāṇināṃ satyaṃ yathārthe vāṅmanasī paropakārike।। etatpratiṣṭhāyām amoghāsya vāgbhavati।। 5।।
steyamaśāstrapūrvakaṃ dravyāṇāṃ parataḥ svīkaraṇapratiṣedhaḥ asteyamiti śeṣaḥ।। asteyapratiṣṭhāyāṃ ratnopasthānam।। 6।।
brahmacaryaṃ guhyendriyopasthasaṃyamaḥ।। brahmacaryapratiṣṭhāyāṃ vīryalābhaḥ।। 7।।
ityete yamāstu te jātideśasamayānavacchinnāḥ sārvabhaumamahāvratam।। brāhmaṇādihiṃsā na mayā karttavyeti jātyavacchinnā sādhāraṇajanānām।। 8।।
mahāyogisārvabhaumānāṃ tu na kasyāpi kutrāpi kadāpi hiṃsā karttavyeti।। jātideśasamayānavacchinnā hiṃsāvratamityarthaḥ।। evaṃ satyādāvapyūhyam।। 9।।
yājñavalkyagītāyām-
ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dayārjavam।।
kṣamādhṛtirmitāhāraḥ śaucaṃ ceti yamā daśa।। 10।।
tatra manovacanakarmabhiḥ sarvatra sarvadā sarvabhūtakleśānupārjanam acchedyatvādilakṣaṇaḥ।। sarvagato hyātmā sa eva cāhamiti matirvā ahiṃsā।।11।।
satyaṃ prāyaśo yathārthaṃ brāhmaṇaprāṇarakṣādyartham ayathārthamapi vacanaṃ sarvasatyaparaṃ brahma na cānyaditi matirvā।। manovākkarmabhiḥ paradravyeṣu aspṛhā asteyam।। 112।।
avihitamaithunatyāgo brahmacaryam।। agnipurāṇe।। maithunasya parityāgo brahmacaryaṃ tadaṣṭadhā।।
smaraṇaṃ kīrttanaṃ keliḥ prekṣaṇaṃ guhyabhāṣaṇam।।
saṃkalpo'dhyavasāyaśca kriyāniṣpattireva ca।। 13।।
etanmaithunamaṣṭāṃgaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ।।
brahmacaryakriyāmūlamanyathā viphalā kriyeti।
guruśuśruṣā vā brahmacaryam।। 14।।
sarvabhūtānugraho dayā।। pravṛttau vā nivṛttau vā ekarūpatvamārjavam।। priyāpriyayoḥ samatvaṃ kṣamā।। arthahānau bandhuviyogādau vā cittasya sthāpanaṃ dhṛtiḥ।। caturthāśaparityāgena pathyabhojanaṃ mitāhāraḥ।। 15।।
taduktaṃ haṭhapradīpikāyām-
susnigdhamadhurāhārāścaturthāṃśavivarjitaḥ।।
bhujyate śivasamprītyai mitāhāraḥ sa ucyate।। 16।।
kaṭvamlatiktalavaṇoṣṇaharitaśākasauvīratailatilasarṣapamadyamatsyāḥ
ajā vimāṃsadadhitakrakulattha kolapiṇyākahiṃgulaśunādyamapathyamāhuḥ।।
punaruṣṇīkṛtaṃ rukṣaṃ cāpathyaṃ varjayet pumān।।
varjayeddurjanaprītiṃ vahnistrīpathi sevanam।।
prātaḥ snānopavāsādikāyakleśavidhiṃ tyajet।। 18।।
gorakṣanāthaḥ-
tela āmalī ora rāī e ti nu kālake bhāī।।
yattu bhārate -
piṇyākarukṣādīnāṃ balārthiyogi bhakṣyatvamuktam।। tadrājayogavirodhikāmaprauḍhijanakasnigdhādibhojanapratiṣedhārtham।। 19।।
nānāvidhavakṣyamāṇaprāṇāyāmamūlabandhanādi mudrāsamūhātmakaṃ haṭhayoge tu śarīrabala sādhye svata evordhvaretatvādi hetutvena kāmādijayopāye abhyasyamāne susnigdhājīrṇājanakabalyakṣīraghṛtādhalpabhojanameva śastamiti bodhyam।। 20।।
tathā ca śivayoge-
godhūmaśāliyavaṣāṣṭikaśobhanānnaṃ kṣīrājyakhaṇḍanavanīta sitāmadhūni।।
śuṇṭhīpaṭolakaphalādi ca pañcaśākamudgādi divyamudakaṃ ca munīndrapathyam।।21।।
sarvaśākamacākṣuṣyaṃ cākṣuṣyaṃ śākapañcakam।।
jayantīvāstu matsyākṣī meghanādaḥ punarnavā।। 22।।
śreṣṭhaṃ sumadhuraṃ snigdhaṃ gavyaṃ dhātuprapoṣaṇam।।
mano'bhilaṣitaṃ yogyaṃ yogī bhojanamācaret।। 23।।
yuvā vṛddho'tivṛddho vā vyādhito durbalo'pi vā।।
abhyāsāt siddhimāpnoti sarvayogeṣvatandritaḥ।। 24।।
atyāhāraḥ prayāsaśca prajalpo niyamāgrahaḥ।
janasaṃgaśca laulyaṃ ca ṣaḍbhiryogo vinaśyati।। 25।।
utsāhāt sāhasauddhairyāttatvajñānācca niścayāt।।
janasaṃgaparityāgāt ṣaḍbhiryogaḥ prasidhyati। 26।।
abhyāsakāle prathame śastaṃ kṣīrājya bhojanam।।
tato'bhyāse dṛḍhībhūte na tādṛṅ niyamāgrahaḥ।। ityādi।। 27।।
śaucaṃ tu mṛñjalābhyāṃ bāhyam।। dharmādhyātmavidyāmāntaram।। harismaraṇādinā bāhyamāntaraṃ ca pavitram।। 28।।
tathā ca smṛtiḥ -
apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṃ gato'pi vā।।
yaḥ smaret puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraḥ śuciḥ।। ityādi।। 29।।
dayādāvapi sarvabhūtopajīvyatvādistatra yamānurūpā siddhairūhyā।। 30।।
iti śrīyūktabhavadeve yamopadeśaścaturthaḥ।।4।।

yuktabhavadevaḥ
pañcamopadeśaḥ
atha niyamaḥ
pātañjale-
śaucasantoṣatapaḥ svādhyāyeśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāśca।।
śaucaṃ mṛjjalādinā janitam amedhyaharaṇādinā bāhyam।। ābhyantaraṃ cittamalānāṃ kṣālanam।।1।।
śocāt svāṃgajugupsā parairasaṃsargaśca।।
tasvaśuddhasaumanasyaikāgyrendriyajayātmadarśanayogyatādīni ca bhavanti।। 2।।
santoṣaḥ sannihitasādhanādadhikasyānupāditsā।।
santoṣādanuttamaḥ sukhalābhaḥ।। tapaḥ kṛcchrādi।। kāyendriyaśuddhiraśuddhikṣayāttapasaḥ ।।3।।
svādhyāyo mokṣaśāstrāṇāmadhyayanam। īśvarapraṇidhānam।। sarvagurau parameśvare sarvakarmārpaṇam।। 4।।
taduktam -
śayyāsanastho'pi pathi vrajan vā susthaḥ parikṣīṇavitarkajālaḥ।।
saṃsārabījakṣayamīkṣamāṇaḥ syānnityayukto'mṛtabhogabhogī।। 5।।
tataḥ pratyakcetanādhigamo'pyantarāyābhāvaśca।।
samādhisiddhirīśvarapraṇidhānāt।। iti ।। 
yadā tu hiṃsādau manaḥ pravarttate tadā hiṃsādau doṣabhāvanamahiṃsādau guṇabhāvanam।।
yathāśāstralokaprasiddhigurūpadeśaṃ vidheyam।। tena mano hiṃsādernivarttate।। taduktaṃ tatraiva।। vitarkabādhane pratipakṣabhāvanamiti।।
vitarkā hiṃsādayaḥ kṛtakāritānumoditā lobhakrodhamohapūrvakāḥ mṛdumadhyādhimātrāḥ duḥkhājñānānāntaphalā iti pratipakṣabhāvanamityarthaḥ।। 6।।
yājñavalkyagītāyām -
tapaḥsvādhyāya āstikyaṃ dānamīśvarapūjanam।।
siddhāntaśravaṇaṃ caiva hīrmatiśca japo vratam।। 7।।
ete ca niyamāḥ praktāstāṃśca sarvān pṛthak।। tatra kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādibhiḥ śarīraśoṣaṇaṃ tapaḥ।। yadṛcchāprāptyā tuṣṭiḥ santoṣaḥ।। dharmaśāstreṣu viśvāsaḥ āstikyam।। nyāyārjitasya dhanānnāderarthibhyaḥ pratipādanaṃ dānam।। viṣṇurudrādīnāṃ prasannasvabhāvena pūjanam īśvarapūjanam।। 8।।
tatraiva coktam -
rāgādyapetaṃ hṛdayaṃ vāgaduṣṭānṛtādibhiḥ।।
hiṃsādirahitaḥ kāryaḥ etadīśvarapūjanam।। iti vā ।। 9।।
dvijānāṃ vedāntaśravaṇaṃ strīśūdrayośca purāṇaśravaṇaṃ siddhāntaśravaṇam।। lokavedaniṣiddhe karmaṇi hrīḥ।। gurūktavedādivihiteṣu śraddhā matiḥ।। vāciko mānaso vā mantrābhyāsaḥ japaḥ ।। 10।।
dharmārthamātmasiddhayarthaṃ vā upāyagrahaṇaṃ vratam।। atrāpi pūrvavadevānurūpā siddhirūhyā ।। 11।।
taduktaṃ bhāgavate bhagavatā-
ahiṃsā satyamāstikyamasaṃgo hrīrasañcayaḥ।।
āstikyaṃ brahmacaryaṃ ca maunaṃ sthairyaṃ kṣamā'bhayam।। 12।।
śaucaṃ japastapo homaḥ śraddhātithyaṃ madarcanam।।
tīrthāṭanaṃ parārthehā tuṣṭirācāryasevanam।।13।।
ete yamāḥ saniyamā ubhaye dvādaśaḥ smṛtāḥ।।
puṃsāmupāśritāstāta yathākāmaṃ duhanti hi ।। iti ।। 14।।
yamānābhīkṣṇaṃ seveta nimayān matparaḥkvacit।। tathā ca tapohomādayo yadā yogānukūlatvenābhyāsadaśāyāṃ jñāyante tadā vidheyāḥ।। yadā yogavikṣepakatvena jñāyante tadā na vidheyāḥ।। 15।।
taduktaṃ gītāsu -
ārurukṣormuneryogaṃ karma kāraṇamucyate।।
yogārūḍhasya tasyaiva śamaḥ kāraṇamucyate।। iti ।। 16।।
ahiṃsādikaṃ tu sarvadaiva vidheyamiti saṃkṣepaḥ।। yamaniyamayostu bahuvidhaśāstroktayoḥ kvacit virodhe'pi ubhayorapi karttavyatābodhatātparyakatvenāvirodha eva paryavasānamiti śivam ।। 17।।
iti śrīmaithilasanmiśraśrīkṛṣṇadevavaṃśaniyamite niyamopadeśo nāma pañcamopadeśaḥ।। 5।।
yuktabhavadevaḥ
ṣaṣṭhopadeśaḥ
athāsanāni-
tāni ca pṛthagāsanānāṃ jīvajātīnāṃ caturāśītilakṣasaṃkhyakatvāt tāvatsaṃkhyakāni।। tebhyaśca jagadguruṇā śrīmaheśvareṇa sāratayā samuddhṛtāni kānicillikhyante।। 1।।
tāni ca svastikaṃ 1 gomukhaṃ 2 vīrāsanaṃ 3 padamaṃ 4 siṃhāsanaṃ 5 bhadraṃ 6muktāsanaṃ 7 mayūrāsanaṃ 8 kūrmāsanaṃ 9 kukkuṭāsanaṃ 10 uttānakūrmāsanaṃ 11 dhanurāsanaṃ 12 matsyendrapīṭhaṃ 13 śavāsanaṃ 14 paścimatānāsanādi 15 bhedātt pañcadaśa ।। 2।।
tatra pratyekalakṣaṇamuktaṃ yājñavalkyagītāyām -
jānūrvorantare samyak kṛtvā pādatale ubhe।।
ṛjukāyaḥ sukhāsīnaḥ svastikaṃ tatpracakṣate।। 3।।
sīvanyā pādapārśve tu gulphau niḥkṣipya pādayoḥ।।
savye dakṣiṇagulphaṃ tu dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇetaram।।
etacca svastikaṃ proktaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam।। iti svastikāsanam।। 4।।
savye dakṣiṇagulphaṃ tu pṛṣṭhapārśve niveśayet.।
dakṣiṇe'pi tathā savyaṃ gomukhaṃ gomukhaṃ yathā।। iti gomukhāsanam।। 5।।
aṃguṣṭhau ca nibadhnīyāddhastābhyāṃ vyutkrameṇa tu।।
ūrvorūpari viprendra kṛtvā pādatale ubhe।।
padmāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarveṣāmapi pūjitam।। iti padmāsanam।। 6।।
ekapādamathaikasmin vinyasyoruṇi saṃsthitam।।
itarasmiṃstathā coruṃ vīrāsanamudāhṛtam।। iti vīrāsanam।। 7।।
gulphau ca vṛṣaṇasyādhaḥ sīvanyāḥ pārśvayoḥ kṣipet.।।
dakṣiṇe savyagulphena dakṣiṇena tathā param।। 8।।
hastau ca jānvoḥ saṃsthāpya cāṃguliḥ samprasārya ca।।
vyāttavaktro nirīkṣyeta nāsāgraṃ susamāhitaḥ।।
siṃhāsanaṃ bhavedetat pūjitaṃ yogibhiḥ sadā।। iti siṃhāsanam।। 9।।
gulphau ca vṛṣaṇasyādhaḥ sīvanyāḥ pārśvayoḥ kṣipet।।
pārśvapādau ca pāṇibhyāṃ dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā suniścalam।।
bhadrāsanaṃ bhavedetat sarvavyādhiviṣāpaham।। iti bhadrāsanam।। 10।।
sampīḍya sīvanīṃ sūkṣmāṃ gulphenaiva tu savyataḥ।।
savyadakṣiṇagulphena muktāsanamidaṃ smṛtam।।
etadeva granthāntare siddhāsanamudāhṛtam।। 11।।
meḍhrādupari niḥkṣipya savyagulphaṃ tathopari।।
gulphāntaraṃ tu niḥkṣipya muktāsanamidaṃ tu vā ।। iti muktāsanam।। 12।।
avaṣṭabhya dharāṃ samyak talābhyāṃ hastayordvayoḥ।।
hastayoḥ kūrparau cāpi sthāpayennābhipārśvayoḥ।।13।।
samunnataśiraḥpādau daṇḍavad vyomni saṃsthitaḥ।।
mayūrāsanametacca sarvapāpapraṇāśanam।। iti mayūrāsanam।।
sarve cābhyantarā rogā vinaśyanti viṣāṇi ca।। 14।।
haṭhapradīpikāyām-
gudaṃ niyamya gulphābhyāṃ vyutkrameṇa samāhitaḥ।। 
kūrmāsanaṃ bhavedetaditi yogavido viduḥ।। iti kūrmāsanam।। 15।।
padmāsanaṃ tu saṃyojya jānūrvorantare karau।।
niveśya bhūmau saṃsthāpya vyomasthaṃ kukkuṭāsanam।। iti kukkuṭāsanam ।। 16।।
kukkuṭāsanabandhastho dorbhyāṃ sambadhya kandharām।।
śete kūrmavaduttānametaduttāna kūrmakam।। iti uttānakūrmāsanam।। 17।।
pādāṃguṣṭhau tu pāṇibhyāṃ gṛhītvā śravaṇāvadhi।।
dhanurākarṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā dhanurāsanamīritam।। iti dhanurāsanam।। 18।।।।
vāmorumūlārpitadakṣapādaṃ jānvorbahi rveṣṭitadakṣadoṣṇā।।
pragṛhya tiṣṭhan parivarttitāṃgaḥ śrīmatsyanāthoditamāsanaṃ syāt।। 19।।
matsyendrapīṭhaṃ jaṭharaprabuddhaṃ pracaṇḍaruṅmaṇḍalakhaṇḍanāstram।।
abhyasataṃ kuṇḍalinīprabodhaṃ daṇḍasthiratvaṃ ca dadāti puṃsām।। matsyendrapīṭhāsanam ।।20।।
uttānaṃ śavavad bhūmau śayanaṃ tu śavāsanam।।
śavāsanaṃ śrāntiharaṃ cittaviśrāntikārakam।। iti śavāsanam।। 21।।
prasārya pādau bhuvi daṇḍarūpau dorbhyāṃ ca pādadvitayaṃ gṛhītvā।।
jānūpari nyastalalāṭapaṭṭo nyasedidaṃ paścimatānamāhuḥ।।
anyatra -
iti paścimatānamāsanāgyraṃ pavanaṃ paścimavāhinaṃ karoti ।। iti paścimatānāsanam।। 22।।
pātañjale-
athāsanam।। tatra sthirasukhabhāsanam।। prayatnaśaithilyānanta samāpattibhyām।। tato dvandvānabhighātaḥ ।। iti sūtrāṇi।। 23।।
etadarthastu-
yena śarīrasaṃsthāna viśeṣeṇa sthiratayā cirakālaṃ sukhena sthātuṃ śakyate tadevāsanam।। tacca prayatnoparamāt ।। anantasukhadam।। manodhāraṇācca sidhyati।। tasmin siddhe dvandvāt śītoṣṇāderabhibhavo na bhavatīti।। 24।।
iti maithilasammiśraśrīkṛṣṇadevatanayasamiśrabhavadevaviracite yuktabhavadeve āsanopadeśo nāma paṣṭhopadeśaḥ।। 6।।
yuktabhavadevaḥ
saptamopadeśaḥ
atha prāṇāyāmaḥ -
sa coktaḥ pātañjale-
tasmin sati śvāsapraśvāsayorgativicchedaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ।। sa tu bāhyābhyantara stambhavṛttirddeśakālasaṃkhyābhiḥ paridṛṣṭo dīrghasūkṣmaḥ।। bāhyābhyantaraviṣayākṣepī caturthaḥ।। tata kṣīyate prakāśāvaraṇam।। dhāraṇāsu ca yogyatā manasaḥ।।iti ।। 1।।
ayamarthaḥ -
satyāsane bāhyasya vāyorācamanaṃ śvāsaḥ।। antaḥsthasya vāyorniḥsāraṇaṃ praśvāsastayorgativicchedaḥ ubhayābhāvaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ।। sa tvityādi।। 2।।
yatra praśvāsapūrvako gatyabhāvaḥ sa bāhyaḥ।। yatra śvāsapūrvako gatyabhāvaḥ sa ābhyantaraḥ।। tṛtīyastambhavṛttiryatrobhayābhāvaḥ sakṛtprayatnād bhavati।। 3।।
trayo'pyete deśādividbhirdṛṣṭāḥ।। iyānasya vāyorgamyadeśaḥ।। iyān kālaḥ।। iyatī saṃkhyā।। prathamadvitīyādyudghāte।। 4।।
evaṃ mṛdumadhyamottamasaṃkhyādibhirddṛṣṭāḥ।। sa evamabhyasto dīrghasūkṣmaḥ।।
śvāsapraśvāsayorye viṣayā deśādayastaddarśanapūrvako gatyabhāvastṛtīyastaddarśanapūrvakaścaturthaḥ।। iti viśeṣaḥ।। 5।।
evaṃ prāṇāyāmamabhyasataḥ prakāśasyāvaraṇaṃ mohendrajāladuṣkarmādi kṣīyate।। taduktaṃ tapo na paraṃ prāṇāyāmāt ।। tato viśuddhirmalādīnāmiti।। 6।।
ata eva nirmalaṃ mano dhāraṇāyogyaṃ ca bhavati।। śrīgorakṣanāthastu-
cale vāte caled bindurniścale niścalo bhavet।।
yogī sthāṇutvamāpnoti tato vāyuṃ nirundhayet।। 7।।
yāvad baddho maruddehe yāvaccittaṃ nirāmayam।।
yāvaddṛṣṭarbhruvormadhye tāvat kālabhayaṃ kutaḥ।।8।।
alpāyāsaprayāsaśca pāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ।।
yogino munayaścaiva kuryuḥ prāṇanirodhanam।।9।।
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṃgulo haṃsaḥ prayāṇaṃ kurute bahiḥ।।
vāmadakṣiṇamārgeṇa tataḥ prāṇo'bhidhīyate।। 10।।
śuddhimeti yadā sarvaṃ nāḍīcakraṃ malākulam।।
tadaiva jāyate yogī prāṇasaṃgrahaṇe kṣamaḥ।। 11।।
baddhapadmāsano yogī prāṇaṃ candreṇa pūrayet।।
dhārayitvā yathāśakti bhūyaḥ sūryeṇa recayet।।12।।
amṛtadravasaṃkāśaṃ gokṣīradhavalopamam।।
dhyātvā candramaso bimbaṃ prāṇāyāmī sukhī bhavet।। 13।।
prāṇaṃ sūryeṇa cākṛṣya pūrayedudaraṃ śanaiḥ।।
kumbhayitvā vidhānena bhūyaścandreṇa recayet।। 14।।
prajvala jjvalanajvālāpuñjamādityamaṇḍalam।।
dhyātvā nābhisthitaṃ yogī prāṇāyāmī sukhī bhavet।। 15।।
prāṇaṃ cediḍayā pibet parimitaṃ bhūyo'nyayā recayet
pītvā piṃgalayā samīraṇamalaṃ baddhvā tyajed vāmayā।।
sūryācandramasoranena vidhinā bimbadvayaṃ dhyāyatāṃ
śuddhā nāḍīgaṇā bhavanti yamināṃ māsadvayādūrdhvataḥ।। 16।।
yatheṣṭaṃ dhāraṇaṃ vāyoranalasya pradīpanam।।
nādābhivyaktirārogyaṃ jāyate nāḍīśodhanāt।। ityādi।। 17।।
sa cāyaṃ prāṇāyāmaḥ pūrakakumbhakarecakasvarūpa tritayātmakaḥ।। tatra samantrakaprāṇāyāmapakṣe dvijātibhiroṃkārādinā।। strīśūdrābhyāṃ tu paurāṇikaviṣṇuśivādimantreṇa karttavyaḥ।। 18।।
taduktaṃ yājñavalkyagītāyām -
dvijavat kṣatriyasyoktaḥ prāṇasaṃyamane japaḥ।।
vaiśyānāṃ dharmayuktānāṃ strīśūdrāṇāṃ tapasvinām।। 19।।
prāṇasaṃyamane gārgi mantraḥ praṇavavarjitaḥ।।
namo'ntaḥ śivamantro vā vaiṇṇavo vocyate budhaiḥ।। iti।। 20।।
tatra yāvatā pūrakaṃ taccaturguṇaiḥ kumbhakam।। taddviguṇena recakamiti।। yathā ṣoḍaśabhiroṃkāraiḥ pūrakaḥ catuḥṣaṣṭibhiḥ kumbhakaḥ dvātriṃśadbhiḥ recakamityādinā krameṇa yathābhyāsaṃ kuryāt।। 21।।
gorakṣanāthastu-
pūrake daśa oṃkārāḥ kumbhake ṣoḍaśo bhavet।।
recake daśa oṃkārāḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate।। 22।।
vastutastu śanaiḥ pūrake recake yathāśakti kumbhakaṃ kuryāt ।। daśagurvakṣaroccārasamakāla ekaśvāsaḥ iti sādhāraṇakramaḥ।। 23।।
yadapekṣayā yāvantameva kālamadhikamatikrāmati-
śvāsastāvat kālamevāyuṣo vṛddhiṃ smapādayati।। ata eva pratipāṇāyāmamāyuṣo vṛddhiḥ।। evaṃ ca surataśramādau daśarvakṣaroccārasamakāla eva śvāsāttarodgame pratiśvāsamāyuṣo hāniḥ।। 24।।
daśagurvakṣaroccārasamakālīne tu śvāse na vṛddhirna vā hānirāyuṣaḥ।।। śvāsaghaṭitavatsarairāyuṣo niyamanādītyuktaṃ prāk।। yathāśakti yathābhyāsameva vāyuṃ sthirīkuryāt ।। na tu śaktyabhyāsamatikramya।। 25।।
tathā ca gorakṣanāthaḥ -
prāṇāyāmena yuktena sarvarogasya saṃkṣayaḥ।।
ayuktā bhyāsayogena sarvarāgasya sambhavaḥ।।26।।
hikkāśvāsastathā kāsaḥ śiraḥkarṇākṣivedanā।।
bhavanti vividhā rogāḥ pavanasya vyatikramāt।। 27।।
yathā siṃho gajo vyāghro bhaved vaśyaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ।।
anyathā hanti yoktāraṃ tathā vāyurasevitaḥ।। 28।।
yuktaṃ yuktaṃ tyajedvāyuṃ yuktaṃ yuktaṃ tu pūrayet।।
yuktaṃ yuktaṃ tu badhnīyādevaṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt।। 29।।
athaitat karaṇakramamāha sa eva-
baddhapadmāsano yogī namaskṛtya guruṃ śivam।।
nāsāgre dṛṣṭirekākī prāṇāyāmaṃ samabhyaset।। 30।।
ūrdhvamākṛṣya cāpānaṃ vāyuṃ prāṇe niyojayet।।
ūrdhvamānīyate śaktyā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate।। 31।।
dvārāṇāṃ navakaṃ nirudhya marutaṃ pītvā dṛḍhaṃ dhāritaṃ
nītvākāśamapānavahnisahitaṃ śaktyā samuccālitam।।
ātmadhyānayutastvanena vidhinā vinyasya mūrdhni dhruvam
yāvattiṣṭhati tāvadeva mahatāṃ saṃghena saṃstūyate।। 32।।
prāṇāyāmo bhavatyevaṃ pātakendhanapāvakaḥ।।
bhavodadhimahāsetuḥ procyate yogibhiḥ sadā ।। iti।। 33।।
śivayoge-
suśobhane maṭhe yogī padmāsanasaganvitaḥ।।
āsanopari saṃviśya pavanābhyāsamācaret।। 34।।
prātaḥkāle ca madhyāhne sūryāste cārdharātrake।।
kuryādevaṃ caturvāraṃ jṛmbhakānekaviṃśatiḥ।। 35।।
jṛmbhakān prāṇāyāmān -
sadyo bhukte'tikṣudhite nābhyāsaḥ kriyate budhaiḥ।।
abhyāsinā ca bhoktavyaṃ stokaṃ stokamanekadhā।। 36।।
pūrvoktakāle kurvīta jṛmbhakān prativāsaram।।
tato yatheṣṭaśaktiḥ syādyogino vāyudhāraṇe।। 37।।
yatheṣṭadhāraṇe vāyorjṛmbhakaḥ sidhyati dhruvam।।
kevale jṛmbhake siddhe kiṃ na syādiha yoginaḥ।। 38।।
svadeḥ sañjāyate dehe prathame jṛmbhake'dham।।
yadā sañjāyate svedo marddanaṃ tena kārayet।। 39।।
anyathā vigrahe dhāturnaṣṭo bhavati yoginaḥ।।
madhyame jṛmbhake kampo dārdduri ca plutirbhavet।। 40।।
tato'dhikatarābhyāsād gagane sādhakottamaḥ।।
yogī padmāsanastho'pi bhuvamutsṛjya gacchati।। 41।।
vāyusiddhistadā jñeyā saṃsārabhrāntināśinī।।
tāvatkālaṃ prakurvīta yogoktaniyamagrahaḥ।। 42।।
alpā nidrā purīṣaṃ ca stokaṃ mūtraṃ ca jāyate।।
adīnatvamarogitvaṃ yoginastatvadarśinaḥ।। 43।।
svedalālākṛmiścaiva sarvathaiva na jāyate।।
kaphapittānilāścaiva sādhakasya kalevare।। 44।।
athābhyāsabalodyagī bhūcarīṃ siddhimāpnuyāt।।
yena durdharṣajantūnāṃ mṛtiḥ syāt pāṇitāḍanāt।। 45।।
santyatra bahavo vighnāḥ dāruṇāḥ durnivāraṇāḥ।।
tathāpi sādhayedyogī prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatairapi।।
praṇavaṃ prajapeddīrghaṃ vighnapraśamahetave ।। ityādi।। 46।।
tadatra sakalasammatyā prayogaḥ।। svasvavarṇāśramācāra niṣkāmakāmyakarmagurūktakriyākaraṇaparaḥ।। atyāsaṃgavarjito yuñjānaḥ।। phalamūlajalacchāyātapasviśobhite manorame sakaladurantopadravadurjanādivarjite tapovanadevālayanadītīragirikandarādau।। 47।।
parakṛte svakṛte vā suśobhane maṭhe।। mṛdvāsane cailājinakuśottare।। nātyucce nātinīce।। padmāsanādi samupaviśya।। guru gaṇeśabaṭukakṣetrapālabrahmaviṣṇuśiveṣṭadevatādīn namaskṛtya pūrakādikrameṇa।। 48।।
sanadhyātraye madhyarātre ca nātikṣudhito nātipūrṇodaro jīvātmaparamātmanorabhedaṃ bhāvayanneva।। sāvadhānatayā prāṇāpānādigatirvivecayan prāṇāyāmamabhyaset।। 49।।
tatra praṇavena prāṇāyāme kriyamāṇe pūrakakumbhakarecakeṣu krameṇa akāraukāramakārasvarūpāḥ raktaśuklakṛṣṇarūpāḥ haṃsagaruḍavṛṣabhavāhanāḥ bālayauvanajarāvasthā daṇḍacakratriśūlādidhāriṇyaḥ brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikāḥ gāyatrīsāvitrīsarasvatīnāmnyaḥ pūrvādisandhyāḥ akārādyarthāśca।। 50।।
devatrayalokatrayatrayāvasthā trayavedatrayavahnitrayādyaśca।। sarve'pi trividhāḥ prapañcabhedāḥ dhyeyāḥ।। sakaladikkālavartticidātmakaṃ tu brahmasakalapraṇavavācyaṃ sadaiva dhyeyam ।। 51।।
evamiḍāyāṃ pavane nīyamāne tu candramāḥ।। piṃgalāyāṃ sūryaḥ suṣumnāyāṃ brahmarūpaśca vahniḥ dhyeyaḥ।। śramodbhūtasvedena gomayabhasmasahitena kevalena vā aṃgamarddanaṃ karttavyam ।। 52।।
pathyādibhojanam।। strīpathaśramādya pathyavarjanam।। stokaṃ stokamanekadhā kṣīrādipānaṃ ca yāvadvāyujayaṃ kāryam।। vāyuśca yadā svasthaṃ yatra tatra sthāpayituṃ śakyate tadā jito jñātavyaḥ।। 53।।
tatra prāṇādau jetavye tattatsthāneṣu ākuñcyānena pūrakaṃ kumbhakaṃ prasāraṇena recakaṃ kuryāt।। tattadrūpadhyānam।। tattadbījajapaṃ ca kuryāt।। 54।।
tatra prāṇasya nāsāgrahṛdayanābhipādāṃguṣṭhādīni sthānāni।। harito varṇaḥ।। yaṃ bījam।।tajjaye jāṭharānalaprābalyaśvāsasthairyasarvavāyujayaśarīralāghavādīni bhavanti।। 55।।
apānasya manyāpṛṣṭhagudapārṣṇiprabhṛtīni sthānāni।। kṛṣṇaṃ rūpam।। aiṃ bījam।। tajjaye kṣatabhaṃgādirehaṇam udarāgnipradīpanaṃ medo'lpatvam ādhivighātaśca bhavati ।।56।।
samānasya hṛdayanābhisarvasandhyādīni sthānāni।। śuklaṃ rūpam।। vaṃ-bījam।। tajjaye'pānasya jayavat phalam।। 57।।
udānasya hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhyamūrdhādīni sthānāni।। raktaṃ rūpam।। raṃ - bījam। tajjaye arcirādimārgeṇa sveṣṭamutkrāntiḥ।। vāripaṃkakaṇṭakādāvapratihatagatitvaṃ ca bhavati ।। 58।।
vyānasya sarvāstvacaḥ sthānāni।। śukrakārmukavad rūpam।। laṃ-bījam।। tajjaye śītātapādyabhibhavaḥ kāntirarogitā ca bhavati।। 59।।
evaṃ yatra yatrāṃge pīḍā bhavati tatra tacchāntyai prāṇādivāyuṃ tattadaṃgākuñcanaviśeṣasthairyeṇa kumbhayet।। tacchāntirbhavati।। evaṃ pādāṃguṣṭhajaṃghājānūrūgudamehaneṣu dhārito vāyuḥ śīghragatyai balāya ca bhavati।। 60।।
tathā nābhau dhāritaḥ jvarādighātāya।। jaṭhare kāyaśuddhaye।। hṛdaye jñānāya।। kūrmanāḍyāṃ rogajarācchide।। kaṇṭhe kṣuttṛṣānāśāya ।। jihvānāsāgrādau rasagandhādijñānāya।। mūrdhni paramānandaprāptaye ।। ityādi।। 61।।
pūrvoktamārṣavākyayuktigurūpadeśebhyaḥ svayamūhyam।। sphuṭīkṛto'yamartho'dhyātmopaniṣanmūlake yogaśāstrasāre ।। 62।।
prāṇāpānasamānaśca udānavyānameva ca।।
prāṇāyāmairjayet sthānavarṇabījakriyārthavit।। 63।।
kriyāḥ prāṇādikarma।। pranayanāpanayanādi।। artho jayaphalam।। bījāni yaṃ aiṃ vaṃ roṃ loṃ।। prāṇo nāsāgrahṛnnābhipādāṃguṣṭhāntago harit।। gamāgamaprayogeṇa tajjayo dhāraṇena vā।। 64।।
gamāgamaprayogābhyāṃ hṛnnābhyaṃguṣṭhameva ca।।
nāsādisthānayogena pūraṇād recanān muhuḥ।।
gamāgama prayogaḥ syāt kumbhakāddhāraṇaṃ punaḥ।। 65।।
apānaḥ kṛṣṇaruk manyāpṛṣṭhapṛṣṭhāntapārṣṇigaḥ।।
jayaḥ svasthānayogena recanāt pūraṇān muhuḥ।। 66।।
manye grīvā paścānnāḍyau।। pṛṣṭhānto gudaḥ।। pārṣṇiḥ pādapaścādbhāgau ।। 67।।
śuklaḥ samāno hṛnnābhisarvasandhiṣvavasthitaḥ।।
jeyaḥ svasthānayogena recanān pūraṇān muhuḥ।। 68।।
rakto hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhyamūrdhvavyavasthitaḥ।।
udāno vaśyatāṃ neyo gatyāgatiniyogataḥ।। 69।।
gatyāgatiprayogamāha-
nāsākarṣaṇayogena sthāpayettaṃ hṛdādiṣu।।
balādutkṛṣya mārgaṃ tu ruddhvā ruddhvā vaśaṃ nayet। 70।।
sarvatvagvṛttiko vyānaḥ śakrakārmukasannibhaḥ।।
jetavyaḥ kumbhakābhyāsāt saṃkoca prasṛtikramāt।। 71।।
prāṇāpānasamānodānavyāneṣu ca vāyuṣu।।
yaṃ aiṃ vaṃ roṃ loṃ bījāni dhyātavyāni yathākramam।। 72।।
prābalyaṃ jāṭharāgneḥ syāddīrghaśvāsamarujjayau।।
lāghavaṃ ca śarīrasya prāṇasya vijaye sati।। 73।।
dīrgho avicchinnaḥ sthiraḥ śvāsa ityarthaḥ।। prāṇasya mukhyatvāt tajjayena tannibaddhānāmanyeṣāmapi marutāṃ jayaḥ।। 74।।
rohaṇaṃ kṣatabhaṃgāderudarāgneśca dīpanam।।
medolpatvaṃ vyādhighātaḥ samānāpānayorjaye।। 75।।
utkrāntirvāripaṃkādyaiścābādhodānanirjjaye।।
jaye vyānasya śītoṣṇāsaṃgaḥ kāntirarogitā।। 76।।
yatra yatra bhavet sthāne yato rogaḥ prapīḍakaḥ।।
tacchāntyai dhārayettatra prāṇādimarutaḥ sadā।। 77।।
evaṃ prāṇādivijaye kṛtābhyāsaḥ pratikṣaṇam।।
dhāraṇādikamabhyasenmanaḥsthairyakṛte sadā।। 78।।
dhāraṇākramamāha-
uktāsanasamāsīno recayitvānilaṃ śanaiḥ।।
āpādāṃguṣṭhaparyantaṃ vāmamārgeṇa pūrayet।। 79।
pādāṃguṣṭhe manaḥ pūrvaṃ ruddhvā pādatale tataḥ।।
pārṣṇau gulphe ca jaṃghāyāṃ jānunyūrvau gude'pi ca ।। 80।।
liṃge nābhau ca tunde ca cahṛtkaṇṭharasane'pi ca।।
tālunāsāgranetre ca bhruvorbhāle śirasyatha।। 81।।
evaṃ raśmikrameṇaiva dhārayanmanasā saha।।
sthānāt sthānāntaraṃ nītvā yāvadbrahmapuraṃ nayet।। 82।।
tataḥ krameṇa tenaiva pādāṃguṣṭhāntamānayet।।
nābhipadmāntaraṃ nītvā tato vāyuṃ virecayet।। 83।।
nābhītyādi।। tena vāyurnābhisthasarvanāḍīmukhaiḥ sarvāsu nāḍīṣu pūrṇo bhūtvā dehaṃ pūrayatīti।। 84।।
pādāṃguṣṭhādijaṃghāyāṃ jānūrugudamehane।।
dhāritaḥ kramaśo vāyuḥ śīghragatyai balāya ca।। 85।।
nābhau jvarādighātāya jaṭhare kāyaśuddhaye।।
jñānāya hṛdaye kūrmanāḍyāṃ rogajarācchide।। 86।।
kaṇṭhe kṣattarṣanāśāya jihvāgre rasasaṃvide।।
gandhajñānāya nāsāgre rūpajñānāya cakṣuṣoḥ।। ityādi।। 87।।
yājñavalkyagītāyām -
dhāraṇāṃ kurvatastvaṃghrau sarve naśyanti vātajāḥ।।
pārthivāṃśe jalāṃśe ca dhāraṇāṃ kurvataḥ sadā।। 88।।
naśyanti śleṣmajā rogāḥ vātajāścāniśaṃ tathā.।।
vyomāṃśe mārutāṃśe ca dhāraṇāṃ kurvataḥ sadā।। 89।।
tridoṣajanitā rogāḥ sarve naśyanti niścitam।।
prāṇasaṃyamanenaiva taddoṣa śamanaṃ nṛṇām।। iti।। 90।।
sa cāyaṃ prāṇāyāmo'ṣṭavidhaḥ।। taduktaṃ haṭhapradīpikāyām-
vāyusthairyānmanaḥsthairyānmanaḥsthairyalakṣaṇo unmanībhāva ityabhidhāya।। 91।।
tatsiddhaye vidhānajñāśicatrān kurvanti kumbhakān।।
vicitrakumbhakābhyāsādvicitrāṃ siddhimāpnuyāt।। 92।।
sūryabhedanamujjāyī sītkārī śītalī tathā।।
bhastrikā bhrāmarī mūrcchā kevalāścāṣṭakumbhakāḥ।। 93।।
pūrakānte ca karttavyo bandho jālandharābhidhaḥ।।
kumbhakānte recakādau karttavyastūḍḍiyānakaḥ।। 94।।
adhastāt kuñcanenāśu kaṇṭhasaṃkocane kṛte।।
madhye paścimatānena syāt prāṇo madhyanāḍiga।। 95।।
apānamūrdhvamutthāpya prāṇaṃ kaṇṭhādadho nayet।।
yogī jarāvinirmuktaḥṣoḍaśo vayasā bhavet।। 96।।
kumbhakasya prādhānyāt tadbheda eva uktastadbhedanaiva ca prāṇāyāmabheda iti dhyeyam ।। 97।।
atha sūryabhedanam -
āsane sukhade yogī baddhvā padmāsanaṃ tataḥ।।
dakṣanāḍyā samākṛṣya bahiḥsthaṃ pavanaṃ śanaiḥ।। 98।।
ākeśādānakhāgrācca nirodhāvadhi kumbhayet।।
tataḥ śanaiḥ savyanāḍyā recayet pavanaṃ sudhīḥ।।99।।
kapāla śodhanaṃ vātadoṣaghanaṃ kṛmidoṣaham।।
punaḥ punaridaṃ kāryaṃ sūryabhedamudāhṛtam।। ‍100।।
athojjāyī -
mukhaṃ niyamya nāḍībhyāmākṛṣya pavanaṃ śanaiḥ।।
yathā lagati kaṇṭhāttu hṛdayāvadhi pūraṇam।। 101।।
pūrvavat kumbhayet prāṇān recayediḍayā tataḥ।।
śleṣmoṣaharaṃ dehānaladīptipravardhanam।। 102।।
nāḍījalodarādhātugata doṣanivāraṇam।।
gacchatā tiṣṭhatā kāryamujjākhyaṃ kumbhakaṃ tvidam।। 103।।
atha sītkārī -
sītkāṃ dadyāt sadā vaktre ghrāṇe caiva vijṛmbhikām।।
evamabhyāsayogena kāmadevo dvitīyakaḥ।। 104।।
yoginīcakrasāmānyaḥ sṛṣṭisthityantakārakaḥ।।
na kṣudhā na tṛṣā nidrā nālasya ca prajāyate।। 105।।
bhavetsvacchandadehaśca sarvopadravavarjitaḥ।।
anena vidhinā satyaṃ yogīndro bhuvi maṇḍale।। 106।।
etadevoktaṃ gorakṣanāthena-
rasanātālumūlena yaḥ prāṇaṃ satataṃ pibete।।
abdārdhena bhavettasya sarvarogaparikṣayaḥ।। 107।।
atha śītalī -
jihvayā vāyumākṛṣya pūrvavat kumbhakādanu।।
śanaistu ghrāṇarandhrābhyāṃ recayedanilaṃ sudhīḥ।। 108।।
gulmaplīhādikān doṣān jvaraṃ pittaṃ kṣudhāṃ tṛṣām।।
anyāṃśca śītalī nāma kumbhako'yaṃ nihanti hi।। 109।।
atha bhastrikā -
ūrvorupari cādhatte ubhe pādatale tathā।।
padmāsanaṃ bhavet samyak sarvapāpapraṇaśanam।। 110।।
samyak padmāsanaṃ baddhvā samagrīvodaraḥ śanaiḥ।।
mukhaṃ saṃyamya yatnena prāṇaṃ ghrāṇena recayet।। 111।।
yathā lagati hṛtkaṇṭhakapāleṣu ca sasvanam।।
vegena pūrayet kiñcit hṛtpadmāvadhi mārutam।। 112।।
punarvirecayettadvat pūrayecca punaḥ punaḥ।।
yathaiva lohakārāṇāṃ bhastrā vegena cālyate।।।113।।
tathaiva svaśarīrasthaṃ cālayet pavanaṃ dhiyā।।
yadā śramo bhaveddehe tadā sūryeṇa recayet ।। 114।।
yathodaraṃ bhavet pūrṇaṃ pavanena tathā laghu।।
dhārayennāsikāmadhye tarjanībhyāṃ vinā dṛḍham।। 115।।
kumbhakaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā recayediḍayā śanaiḥ।।
vātapittaśleṣmaharaṃ śarīrāgnivivardhanam।। 116।।
kuṇḍalībodhanaṃ sarvadoṣaghnaṃ sukhadaṃ śubham।।
brahmanāḍīmukhāntasthakaphādyargalanāśanam।।117।।
samyaggātrasamudbhūtagranthitrayavibhedanam।।
viśeṣeṇaiva karttavyaṃ bhastrākhyaṃ kumbhakaṃ tvidam।। 118।।
atha bhrāmarī -
vegodghoṣaṃ pūrakaṃ bhṛṃganādaṃ recakaṃ mandamandam।।
yogīndrāṇāmevamabhyāsayogāccitte jātā kācidānandalīlā।। 119
atha mūrcchā-
pūrakānte gāḍhataraṃ baddhvā jālandharaṃ śanaiḥ।।
recayen mūrcchanākhyeyaṃ manomūrcchā sukhapradā।। 120।।
antaḥpravarttitādhāramārutā pūritodaraḥ।।
payasyagādhe'pi sukhāt plavate padmapatravat।।
ayameva plāvinī kumbhako'pi।। 121।।
atha kevalaḥ -
prāṇāyāmastridhā prokto recapūrakakumbhakaiḥ।।
sahitaḥ kevalaśceti kumbhako dvividho mataḥ।। 122।।
recya vā pūrakaḥ kāryaḥ śanaiḥ sahitakumbhakaḥ।।
yāvat kevalasiddhiḥ syāt sahitaṃ tāvadabhyaset।। 123।।
recakaṃ pūrakaṃ muktvā yadvāyudhāraṇam।।
prāṇāyāmo'yamityuktaḥ sa vai kevalakumbhakaḥ।। 124।।
kevale kumbhake siddhe recapūrakavarjite।।
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcit triṣu lokeṣuṃ vidyate।। 125।।
śaktaḥ kevalakumbhena yatheṣṭaṃ vāyudhāraṇam।।
rājayogapadaṃ samyak labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ।। 126।।
haṭhaṃ vinā rājayogo rājayogaṃ vinā haṭhaḥ।।
na sidhyati tato yugmamāniṣpatteḥ samācaret।। 127।।
kumbhitaḥ prāṇarecānte kuryyāccittaṃ nirāmayam।।
evamabhyāsayogena rājayogapadaṃ vrajet।। 128।।
kumbhakāt kuṇḍalībodhaḥ kuṇḍalībodhato bhavet।।
anargalā suṣumnā ca haṭhasiddhiśca jāyate।। 129।।
vapuḥ kṛśatvaṃ vadane prasannatā nādasphuṭatvaṃ nayane ca nirmale।।
arogatā bindujayo'gnidīpanaṃ nāḍīviśuddhirhaṭhasiddhilakṣaṇam।। 130।।
haṭhenāgraheṇa prāṇādisthirīkaraṇopāyo haṭhayogaḥ।। sa coktaṃ vakṣyamāṇāṣṭavidhaḥ prāṇāyāma ṣaṭkarmamudrādaśakāditi।। 131।।
ete ca sūryabhedanādayaḥ prāṇāyāmaviśeṣāḥ -
gurūpadeśataḥ samyak vijñāya śaktyanusāreṇānekaphalārthibhirabhyanīyāḥ।। 132।।
yadi tu viśiṣya naite vijñāyante tathāpi mukhena nāsāvivarābhyāṃ vā bāhyavāyusahitaṃ prāṇaṃ hṛdayādānīya adhomārgākuñcanādinā apānamūrdhvabhānīya yathāśaktirnikumbhayet।। jihvāṃ ca tālughaṇṭikādāvupari sthāpayet।। praṇavaṃ ca japet।। manasā ca sakaladikkālavartticaitanyānandasvarūpaṃ brahma paśyet।। atha śanaiḥ śanaiḥ recayet।। punaḥ pūrvavadūrdhvādhodarābhyāṃ pūrayitvā kumbhayet।। sandhyātraye cārdharātre ca viśeṣato'bhyasedityupadeśasāram ।। 133।।
taduktaṃ sureśvarācāryeṇa-
ākuñcanamapānasya prāṇasya ca nirodhanam।।
lambikopari jihvāyāḥ sthāpanaṃ yogasādhanam ।। iti ।।134।।
iti yuktabhavadeve prāṇāyāmopadeśe'ṣṭavidhaprāṇāyāmopadeśaḥ।।
atha yogasyāvasthācatuṣṭayamucyate-
taduktaṃ śivayoge-
ārambhaśca ghaṭaścaiva tathā paricayaḥ punaḥ।।
niṣpattiśceti yogasya syādavasthācatuṣṭayam।। 135।।
tatra pūrvoktacatuṣkālikaprāṇāyāmādinā nāḍīśuddhau ārambhāvasthā bhavati।। tasyāḥ lakṣaṇaṃ - kāyasya sugandhitvaṃ kāntimatvādi।। 136।।
atha ghaṭāvasthā-
prāṇāpānau nādabindū jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ।।
militvā ghaṭate yasmāttasmādvai ghaṭa ucyate।। 137।।
yāmamātraprāṇāyāmādikamekatra dharttuṃ samartho ghaṭāvasthāmāpnoti।। asyamavasthāyāṃ trailokyeśvaratvepsitasiddhivāksiddhikāmacāritva dūradarśana śravaṇa sūkṣmadṛṣṭiparakāyapravesaviṇmūtra lepanasvarṇasiddhi adṛśyakaraṇādīni bhavanti।। 138।।
yadā bhaved ghaṭāvasthā pavanābhyāsinaḥ parā।।
tadā saṃsāracakre'smin tannāsti yanna sādhayet।। 139।।
atha paricayāvasthā -
yatheṣṭakaṃ yadā vāyurniścalo yogināṃ bhavet।।
tataḥ paricayāvasthā yogino'bhyāsato bhavet।। 140।।
asyāmavasthāyāṃ vāyuḥ sūryacandramasau tyaktvā niścalatvena paricitaḥ kriyāśaktiṃ gṛhītvā ṣaṭcakrāṇi bhitvā suṣumnāvyomni sañcarati।। tataḥ karmaṇāṃ trikūṭaṃ yogī paśyati ।। 141।।
tacca praṇavajapakāyavyūhopabhogādinā nāśayet।। manaḥ pavanayoḥ mūlādhārādyekaikacakre pañca pañca ghaṭikāparyantaṃ sthiratvaṃ dhāraṇā।। tāṃ cābhyaset।। yadā tatra tatra krameṇa pṛthivyādibhāvanā tattadbhūtajayaṃ karoti।। 142।।
kiñca -
medhāvī pañcabhūtānāṃ dhāraṇāṃ yaḥ samabhyaset।।
śatabrahmamṛtenāpi mṛtyustasya na vidyate।। 143।।
asyāmavasthāyāṃ svasāmarthyaṃ saṃgopayan vātulavattiṣṭhet।। svakāryaṃ nibhṛtakrameṇa sādhayediti।। 144।।
atha niṣpatyavasthā -
tato'bhyāsakrameṇaiva niṣpattiryogināṃ bhavet।।
anādikarmabījāni yena chitvāmṛtaṃ pibet।। 145।।
asyāmavasthāyāṃ svecchayā samādhi jīvanmuktiḥ śāntivīratvaṃ ca bhavati।। kiñcagṛhītvā cetanāṃ vāyuṃ kriyāśaktiṃ ca vegavān।।
sarvacakraṃ vijityāśu jñānaśaktau vilīyate।। 146।।
iti prāṇāyāmopadeśe'vasthācatuṣṭayopadeśaḥ।।
atha yadi yuñjānaḥ śleṣmamalādipūrṇo bhavati tadā ṣaṭkarmāṇi kuryāt।। tāni coktāni haṭhapradīpikāyām -
medaślemādisampūrṇaḥ ṣaṭkarmāṇi samācaret।।
anyastu nācarettāni doṣāṇāṃ samabhāgikaḥ।। 147।।
dhautirbastiśca netiśca trāṭakaṃ naulikaṃ tathā।।
kapālabhāticaitāni ṣaṭkarmāṇi pracakṣate।। 148।।
karmaṣaṭkamidaṃ gopyaṃ ghaṭaśodhanakāraṇam।।
vicitraguṇasandhāyī kriyate yogibhiḥ sadā।। 149।।
atha dhautiḥ -
caturaṃgulavistāraṃ siktaṃ vastraṃ śanairgraset।।
tataḥ pratyāhareccaitadākṣālaṃ dhautikarma tat।। 150।।
plīhā śvāsaśca kuṣṭhaṃ ca kapharogāśca viṃśatiḥ।।
dhautikarmaprabhāvena gacchantyeva na saṃśayaḥ।। 151।।
atha bastikarma-
nābhidaghne jale pāyau nyastanālotkaṭāsanaḥ।।
ādhārā kuñcanaṃ kuryāt kṣālanaṃ bastikarma tat।। 152।।
gulmaplīhodaraṃ cāpi vātapittakaphodbhavāḥ।।
bastikarmaprabhāvena naśyanti sakalāmayāḥ।। 153।।
dhātvīndrintaḥ karaṇaprabodhaṃ dadāti kāntiṃ dahanapradīptim।।
aśeṣadoṣopacayaṃ nihanyādabhyasyamānaṃ jalabastikarma।। 154।।
iyameva gajakaraṇī taduktam -
udaragatapadārthamudvamantī pavanamapānamudīryakaṇṭhanāle।।
kramaparicayavaśyavāyumārgā gajakaraṇīti nigadyate haṭhajñaiḥ।। 155।।
atha netikarma-
sūtraṃ vitastisusnigdhaṃ nāsānāle praveśayet।।
mukhānnirgamayedeṣā netiḥ siddhairnigadyate।।156।।
kapālaśodhinī caiva divyadṛṣṭipradāyinī।।
jatrūrdhvajātarogādyaiḥ jayatyeva suniścitam।। 157।।
atha trāṭakam -
vīkṣeta niścaladṛśā sulakṣyaṃ ca samāhitaḥ।।
aśrusampātaparyantamācāryaistrāṭakaṃ smṛtam।। 158।।
moṭakaṃ sarvarogāṇāṃ tandrādīnāṃ kapāṭanam।।
yatnatastrāṭakaṃ gopyaṃ yathā hāṭakapeṭakam।। 159।।
sā ca niścaladṛṣṭistrividhā bhūcarī khecarī agocarī ca।। tatra yā sarvato'nyato niruddhā nāsāgrasampūrṇadehapādāgrasthabhūparyantaṃ vilokayati sā bhūcarī।। 160।।
yā bhrūmadhyastha gaganaṃ sūryacandramaṇḍalādikaṃ vā vibhāvayati sā khecarī।। yā tu atyantaṃ nāsāgra nābhipradeśamātrameva vilokayati sā iṣadgo caratvādagocarī।। alavaṇāyavāgūritirvādeti।। 161।।
atha nauli -
amandāvarttavegena tundaṃ savyāpasavyataḥ।।
natāṃso bhrāmayedeṣā naulirgauḍaiḥ praśasyate।। 162।।
mandāgnisandīpanapācanādisandhāvanānandakarī sadaiva।।
aśeṣadoṣāmayaśoṣaṇīyaṃ haṭhakriyāmauliriyaṃ hi nauliḥ।। 163।।
atha kapālabhātiḥ-
bhastrāvallohakārāṇāṃ recapūrau sasambhramau।।
kapālabhātirvikhyātā kaphadoṣaviśoṣiṇī।। 164।।
ṣaṭkarmabhirgatasthaulyaṃ kaphamedomalātigaḥ।।
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kuryādanāyāsena sidhyati।। 165।।
prāṇāyāmaireva sarve praśuṣyanti malā iti।।
ācāryāṇāṃ tu keṣāñcidanyat karma na sammatam।। 166।।
trāṭakādikaṃ tu anantaphalavatvāt sarvaireva kāryam।। anyattu taddoṣavattaiva iti sampradāyaḥ।। nāsikāgre trāṭake kriyamāṇe niḥśvāsabhāsikā śaktirjāyate।। yayā tattatsvarūpopalakṣito vāyurnāsāgre candramaṇḍalavat dṛśyate।। tadrūpeṇa ca dhūmraharitaraktaśvetapītarūpāṇi।। ākāśatatvādīni paricīyante।। 167।।
kiñca-
prathamaṃ nāsāgre mayūrapucchākāraṃ chatravadīkṣyate।। tatastadeva lakṣmīnārāyaṇādirūpatayā prakaṭībhavatīti gurūpadeśato jñeyamiti dik।। 168।।
iti prāṇāyāmopadeśa ṣaṭkarmopadeśaḥ।।
atha mudrādaśakamucyate-
tatra śivayogagorakṣaśatakahaṭhapradīpikādāvuktam।। sarvāvirodhena likhyate।। 169।।
tatra haṭhapradīpikāyām -
saśailavanadhātrīṇāṃ yathādhāro hi nāyakaḥ।।
sarveṣāṃ yogatantrāṇāṃ tathādhāro hi kuṇḍalī ।। 170।।
suptā guruprasādena yadā jāgartti kuṇḍalī।।
tadā sarvāṇi padmāni bhidyante granthayo'pi ca ।। 171।।
prāṇasya śūnyapadavī mahārandhraṃ mahāpatham।।
śmaśānaṃ śāmbhavī madhyamārgaścetyekavācakam।। 172।।
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena prabodhayitumīśvarīm।।
brahmadvāramukhe suptāṃ mudrābhyāsaṃ samācaret।। 173।।
mahāmudrā mahābandho mahāvedhaśca khecarī।।
uḍyānaṃ mūlabandhaśca bandho jālandharastathā।। 174।।
viparītakṛtiścaiva vajrolī śakticālanam।।
idaṃ hi mudrādaśakaṃ mudrāṇāmuttamomam।। 175।।
ādināthoditaṃ samyagaṣṭaiśvaryapradāyakam।।
vallabhaṃ sarvasiddhendradurlabhaṃ marutāmapi।। 176।।
gopanīyaṃ prayatnena jarāmaraṇanāśanam।।
kasyacinnaiva vaktavyaṃ kulastrīsurataṃ yathā।। 177।।
tatra mahāmudrā-
pādamūlena vāmena yoniṃ sampīḍya dakṣiṇam।।
pādaṃ prasāritaṃ kṛtvā karābhyāṃ dhārayed dṛḍham।। 178।।
kaṇṭhe bandhaṃ samāropya dhārayedvāyumūrdhvataḥ।।
yathā daṇḍāhataḥ sarpo daṇḍākāraḥ prajāyate।। 179।।
ṛjvībhūtā tathā śaktiḥ kuṇḍalī sahasā bhavet।।
tadāsau maraṇāvasthā jāyate dvipaṭāśritā ।। 180।।
tataḥ śanaiḥ śanaireva recayenna tu vegataḥ।।
iyaṃ khalu mahāmudrā mahāsiddhaiḥ pradṛśyate।। 181।।
mahākleśāyato doṣā jīryante maraṇādayaḥ।।
mahāmadrā ca tāmeva vadanti vibudhāttamāḥ।। 182।।
candrāṃgena samabhyasya sūryāgenābhyasettataḥ।।
yāvattulyā bhavet saṃkhyā tato mudrāṃ visarjayet.।। 183।।
iha pathyamapathyaṃ vā sarasaṃ nirasaṃ ca vā।।
api bhuktaṃ viṣaṃ ghoraṃ pīyūṣamiva jīryati।। 184।।
kṣayakuṣṭhagudāvarttagulmājīrṇapurogamāḥ।।
tasya rogāḥ kṣayaṃ yānti mahāmudrāṃ tu yo'bhyaset।। 185।।
kathiteyaṃ mahāmudrā mahāsiddhikarī nṛṇām।।
gopanīyā prayatnena na deyā yasya kasyacit।। 186।।
ayamarthaḥ -
vāmapādamūlena gudameḍhrāntarālagaṃ yonisthānaṃ sampīḍya dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ prasāritaṃ kṛtvā pāṇibhyāṃ ca caraṇāgre dhṛtvā navadvārāṇi saṃyamya kaṇṭhe śirāsaṃkocalakṣaṇaṃ jālandharabandhaṃ vidhāya cittaṃ brahmaṇi vāyau vā nidhāya ūrdhvādhobhyāṃ vāyumākṛṣya hṛdayādūrdhvato dhārayet।। evaṃ vāyudhāraṇāyāṃ kriyamāṇāyāṃ vyākulā bhūtā kuṇḍalinī apānaprāṇābhyāṃ saha nāsāpuṭadvayāśritā bhavati।। 187।।
ataḥ tanniḥśeṣagamanarodhāya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ recayet।। evaṃ tataḥ pūrvavadeva yathāśakti prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt।। 188।।
atha dakṣiṇapādena yonisthānaṃ sampīḍya prasāritaṃ vāmapādaṃ pāṇibhyāṃ dhṛtvā pūrvavadeva tatsama kālaṃ tatsama kālaṃ tatsamasaṃkhyakaṃ ca prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt।। evaṃ mahāmudrā bhavatīti।। 189।।
atha mahābandhaḥ -
pārṣṇivāmasya pādasya yonisthāne niyojayet।।
vāmorupari saṃsthāpya dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ tathā।। 190।।
pūrayitvā tato vāyuṃ cibukaṃ dṛḍham।।
niḥkṣipya yonimākuñcya mano madhye niyojayet।। 191।।
dhārayitvā yathāśakti recayed anilaṃ sudhīḥ।।
rājadantadvayaṃ tatra jihvayonnamayoditi।। 192।।
ayaṃ khalu mahābandho mahāsiddhipradāyakaḥ।। 
kālapāśamahābandha vimocanavicakṣaṇaḥ।। 193।।
ayaṃ hi sarvanāḍīnām ūrdhvaṃ gativibodhakaḥ।।
triveṇīsaṃgamaṃ dhatte kedāraṃ prāpayen manaḥ।। 194।।
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -
mahāmudrānantaraṃ mahābandhaḥ kriyate iti।। mahāmudrāyāṃ yaḥ pādo yonisthāne kṛtaḥ taṃ tathaiva sthāpayitvā prasāritamaparaṃ pādaṃ tadūrupari vinyasya guda yonisthānaṃ samākuñcya apānaṃ ūrdhvagaṃ kṛtvā prāṇaṃ cādhomukhaṃ kṛtvā samānena saha saṃyojya hṛdayodarādau kumbhayitvā śanai recayet।। pūrvavadeva vāmadakṣaṇāṃśayoḥ samasaṃkhyākaṃ prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt।। 195।।
manastu suṣumnāyāṃ sthāpayet ।। kaṇṭha śirāsaṃkocanamapi kuryāt।। yadvā rājadantadvayaṃ mūlaṃ jihvayonnamayedevaṃ mahābandho bhavatīti।। 196।।
atha mahāvedhaḥ -
mahāmudrāmahābandhau niṣphalau vedavarjitau।।
rūpalāvaṇyasampannā yathā strī puruṣaṃ vinā ।। 197।।
mahābandhasthito yogī kṛtvā pūrakamekadhā।।
vāyunāṃ gatimāvṛtya nibhṛtaṃ kaṇṭhamudrayā।। 198।।
nyastahastayugo bhamau sphicau santāḍayecchanaiḥ।।
jaṃghādvayaṃ samākṛṣya vāyuḥ spharati satvaram।। ‍199।।
somasūryāgnisambandho jāyate cāmṛtāya ca।।
samutpannā mṛtāvasthā tato vāyuṃ virecayet।। 200।।
mahāvedho'yamabhyasto mahāsiddhipradāyakaḥ।।
valīpalitavepaghnaḥ sevyate sādhakottamaiḥ।। 201।।
vedhenānena saṃbhidya vāyunā yogipuṃgavaḥ।।
granthīnsuṣumnāmārgeṇa brahmarandhraṃ bhinattyasau।। 202।।
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ।। mahābandhānantaraṃ mahāvedhaḥ kriyate।। tena mahābandhe sthitaḥ prāṇāpānāvekīkṛtyodare pūrayitvā kaṇṭhasaṃkocādinā vāyugatirnirudhya bhūmau hastayugaṃ dhṛtvā 
nābhyadho māṃsalapradeśalakṣaṇaṃ sphicaṃ vāyunaiva śanaiḥ śanaiḥ santāḍayet।। evaṃ mahāvedho bhavati।। 203।।
bandhatrayaṃ mahāguhyaṃ jarāmṛtyuvināśanam।।
vahnivṛddhikaraṃ caiva aṇimādi guṇapradam।। 204।।
aṣṭadhā kriyate caiva yāme yāme dine dine।।
puṇyasambhārasandhāyi pāpaughabhiduraṃ sadā ।। 205।।
samyak śikṣāvatāmevaṃ svalpaṃ prathamasādhanam।।
vahnistrīpathisevānāmādau varjanamācaret।। 206।।
atha khecarī -
kapālakuhare jihvā praviṣṭā viparītagā।।
bhruvorantargatā dṛṣṭirmudrā bhavati khecarī।। 207।।
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -
sukhade āsane upaviśya bhruvormadhye sudṛḍhaṃ dṛṣṭiṃ vidhāya sudhākūpanāmni lambikordhvasthite kaṇṭhagartte viparītagāṃ jihvāṃ prayatnenāpi saṃyojya candramaṇḍalāccyuta mamṛtaṃ sasītkāraṃ tatraiva sthitayā jihvayā sarvadaivatasvarūpaṃ kuṇḍalinīdhyānapūrvakaṃ pibedevaṃ khecarīmudrā bhavati ।। 208।।
jihvāṃ parāṅmukhīṃ kṛtvā kṣaṇārddhaṃ yadi tiṣṭhati।।
kṣaṇena mucyate yogī vyādhimṛtyujarādibhiḥ।। 209।।
na rogo maraṇaṃ tasya na nidrā na tṛṣā kṣudhā।।
na ca mūrcchā bhavettasya yo mudrāṃ vetti khecarīm।। 210।।
pīḍyate na sa rogoṇa lipyate na sa karmaṇā।।
bādhyate na sa kālena yasya mudrāsti khecarī।। ‌211।
cittaṃ carati khe yasmājjihvā carati khe yataḥ।।
teneyaṃ khecarīmudrā sarvasiddhairnamaskṛtā।। 212।।
khecaryā mudritaṃ yena vivaraṃ lambikordhvataḥ।।
na tasya kṣarate binduḥ kāminyāliṃgitasya ca।। 213।।
kalāṃ parāṅmukhīṃ kṛtvā tripathe pathi yojayet।।
sā bhavet khecarīmudrā vyomacakraṃ taducyate।। 214।।
ūrdhvajihvaḥ sa medhāvī somapānaṃ karoti yaḥ।।
māsārddhena na sandeho mṛtyuṃ jayati yogavit।। 215।।
nityaṃ somakalāpūrṇaṃ śarīraṃ yasya yoginaḥ।।
takṣakenāpi daṣṭasya viṣaṃ tasya na sarpati।। 216।।
gorakṣanātho'pi prāha-
mūrdhnaḥ ṣoḍaśapatrapadmagalitaṃ prāṇādavāptaṃ haṭhāt
ūrdhvāsyo rasanāṃ niyamya vivare śaktiṃ parāṃ cintayet।।
tatkallolakalājalaṃ suvimalaṃ dhārāmṛtaṃ yaḥ pibet
nirddoṣaḥ samṛṇālakomalatanuryogī paraṃ jīvati।। 217।।
cumbantī yadi lambikāgramaniśaṃ jihvā rasasyandinī
sakṣārā kaṭukāmladugdhasadṛśī madhvājyatulyāthavā।।
vyādhīnāṃ haraṇaṃ jarāmbutaraṇaṃ śāstrāgamodgīraṇaṃ
tasya syādamaratvamaṣṭaguṇitaṃ siddhāṃganākarṣaṇam।। 218।।
ekaṃ sṛṣṭimayaṃ bījaṃ ekā mudrā ca khecarī।।
eko devo nirālamba ekāvasthā manonmanī।। 219।।
pātāle yadviśati suṣiraṃ merumūle yadasti
tadvaccaitat pravadati sudhīstanmukhaṃ nimnagānām।।
candrāt sāraḥ sravati vapuṣastena mṛtyurnarāṇām
badhnīyāttat sukaraṇamatho nānyathā kāyasiddhiḥ।। 220।।
khecarīsiddhimicchatā chedanacālanadohanairjihvā vṛddhiḥ sampādanīyā।। 221।।
atha uḍḍiyānabandhaḥ-śivayoge-
nābherūrdhvamadhaścāpi tānaṃ nirbhara mācaret।।
uḍḍiyāno hyayaṃ bandhaḥ sarvaduḥkhaughanāśanaḥ।। 222।।
nityaṃ yaḥ kurute yogī caturvāraṃ dine dine।।
tasya nābherviśuddhiḥ syādyena śuddho bhavenmarut।। 223।।
tasyāyaṃ bhāvaḥ-
ūrdhvādhobhyāṃ nābhiṃ dṛḍhaṃ saṃkocayitvā yathābhyāsaṃ vāyuṃ kumbhayedevamuḍyānam।। dṛḍhamāsanaṃ baddhvā gulphadeśasamīpe karābhyāṃ pādau datvā nābhisamīpasthaṃ kandaṃ pīḍayannudare paścimatāṇaṃ tathā kuryādyathā vāyuḥ kukṣisandhiṃ na gacchatyevamuḍḍiyānabandho jarāmṛtyuvināśanaḥ sampadyate।। 224।।
atha mūlabandhastatraiva -
pādamūlena sampīḍya gudamārgaṃ suyantritam।।
balādapānamākṛṣya kramādūrdhvaṃ samānayet।। 225।।
kathito'yaṃ mūlabandho jarāmaraṇanāśanaḥ।।
apānaprāṇayoraraikyaṃ kṣayaṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ।। 226।।
mūlabandhaḥ karotyeṣastathaikyaṃ bindunādayoḥ।।
bandhasyāsya prasādena gagane vijitānilaḥ।।
padmāsanasthito yogī bhuvamutsṛjya gacchati।। 227।।
pārṣṇyā sampīḍitaṃ gudamākuñcya vāramvāram apānamūrdhvamānayedevaṃ mūlabandho bhavati ।। 228।।
atha jālandharabandhaḥ -
baddhvā galaśirājālaṃ hṛdaye cibukaṃ nyaset।।
bandho jālandharaḥ prokto devānāmapi durlabhaḥ।। 229।।
badhnāti hi śirājālaṃ nādho yāti nabhojalam।।
tato jālandharo bandhaḥ kaṇṭhaduḥkhaughanāśanaḥ।। 230।।
jālandhare kṛte bandhe kaṇṭhasaṃkocalakṣaṇe।।
na pīyūṣaṃ patatyagnau na ca vāyuḥ prakupyati।। 231।।
bandhenānena pīyūṣaṃ svayaṃ pibati buddhimān।।
amaratvaṃ ca samprāpya modate bhuvanatraye।। 232।।
kaṇṭhasaṃkocanaṃ kṛtvā hṛdaye cibukaṃ nyaset।। evaṃ jālandharabandho bhavati।। tena candrānniḥsṛtamamṛtaṃ nābhisthe vahnimaṇḍale na patati kintu jihvādvārā dehe prasaratīti bhāvaḥ ।। 233।।
atha viparītakṛtiḥ -
bhūtale svaśiro datvā khe nayeccaraṇadvayam।।
viparītakṛtiścaiṣā sarvatantreṣu gopitā।।234।।
etāṃ yaḥ kurute nityamabhyāsādyāmamātrakam।।
amṛtaiḥ kurute pānaṃ pralaye'pi na sīdati।। 235।।
etadevoktaṃ gorakṣanāthena -
ūrdhvaṃ nābhiradhastālurūrdhvaṃ bhānuradhaḥ śaśī।।
karaṇī viparītākhyā guruvaktreṇa gamyate ।। iti ।। 236।।
ūrdhvaṃ caraṇadvayaṃ kṛtvā adhaḥ karadvayopari śiro nyasedevaṃ viparītākṛtirbhavati।। sa ca prathamadine kṣaṇamātraṃ vidheyā dvitīyadine kiñcidadhikaṃ kālamevaṃ yāmaparyantaṃ vidheyā ।। 237।।
asyāṃ kriyamāṇāyāṃ sādhakasya bhakṣyaṃ bahulaṃ sampādyamanyathā pravṛddho jāṭharānalo dhātuṃ dahatīti।। valitaṃ palitaṃ caiva ṣaṇmāsārddhānna dṛśyate।। yāmamātraṃ tu yo nityamabhyaset sa tu kālajit .।। 238।।
atha vajrolī -
sā ca prāptabrahmānandatayā maithunasukhānuraktānāṃ gṛhasthayujjānānāṃ kṛte proktā gorakṣanāthena ।। 239।।
vajrolīṃ kathayiṣyāmi gopitāṃ sarvayogibhiḥ।।
tyaktayogoktaniyamā yayā sidhyanti yoginaḥ।। 240।।
atra vastudvayaṃ manye durlabhaṃ yasya kasyacit।।
kṣīramekaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu nārī svavaśa varttinī।। 241।।
abhyāsaṃ śṛṇu vajrolyā vakṣyāmyatirahasyakam।।
sukhāni labhate nārī puruṣaḥ siddhimāpnuyāt।। 242।।
nārīṃ ramyāmavasthāpya rahasye tu digambarām।।
svayaṃ digambaro bhūtvā uttānāyāstathopari।। 243।।
śaitaṃ ca kumbhakaṃ kuryānnārī mandaṃ ca niḥśvaset।।
ayonau dṛḍhamāliṃgya yonau liṃgaṃ na cārpayet।। 244।।
atastvadharapānaṃ ca parasparamathācaret।।
parasparamathāliṃgya yāvat prasvedasambhavaḥ।।245।।
yadi skhaled bahirvīryaṃ tadā svedena marddayet।।
yadi bindurna skhalati puranāśliṣya kāminīm।। 246।।
yonau śiśnaṃ cārpayedvā yathā binduḥ pated bahiḥ।।
patite ca punaḥ svedajalena parimarddayet।। 247।।
evaṃ dinatrayaṃ kuryāt trivāraṃ pratyahaṃ tataḥ।।
rasanālena phūtkāraṃ vāyoḥ sañcārakāraṇāt।। 248।।
kuryāt śanaiḥ śanairyogī yāvacchaktiḥ prajāyate।।
tato maithunakāle tu patadbinduṃ samunnayet।। 249।।
vajrakandaṃ samāpīḍya kumbhayitvāpi mārutam।।
caladbinduṃ samākṛṣya manastatraiva dhārayet।। 250।।
evamabhyāsato bindurna yonau patati kvacit।।
itthaṃ maithunaśaktiḥ syāddarmadāmapi kāminīm।।251।।
marddayedyogayuktātmā śataśo nātra saṃśayaḥ।।
evaṃ bindau sthire jāte mṛtyuṃ jayati sarvathā।। 252।।
maraṇaṃ bindupātena jīvanaṃ bindudhāraṇāt।।
evaṃ caiva sthire jāte mṛtyuṃ jayati nānyathā।। 253।।
evamabhyāsato nārī yadi retaḥ samuddharet।।
deha sthiratvamāyāti vajrolyabhyāsa yogataḥ।। 254।।
abhyāsasya kramaṃ vakṣye nārīṇāṃ ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ।।
bahiḥśiśnagataṃ śukraṃ yadi skhalati kālataḥ।। 255।।
ṭiṇṭikāmukhamākuñcya maṇiṃ tatra praveśayet ।।
tamuddharet samākṛṣya vāyunā tena vartmanā।। 256।।
tado reto rajo nāśaṃ na gacchati kadācana।।
mūlādhāre ca nārīṇāṃ sa bindurnādatāṃ vrajet।। 257।।
ayaṃ yogaḥ puṇyavatāṃ siddhenmatsariṇāṃ na hi.।। ayaṃ bhāvaḥ।। nirvāte vilāsamandire nānāprakāreṇa priyayā saha vilāsaṃ kuryāt।। 258।।
patadbindumapānena huṃ huṃkārasahitena balādūrdhvamākṛṣya kiñcitkālaṃ vilambya ramet punaḥ।। yadā tu na dhārayituṃ śakyate tadā bahiḥskhalitena bindunā saha prasvedenāṃgaṃ marddayet।। 259।।
evaṃ trivāraṃ trivāraṃ pratyahaṃ dinatrayaṃ kṛtvā bindusiddhiṃ labhet।। nārī rajo'pyevamākarṣayet।। priyaṃ śukraṃ ca niḥśvāsaṃ cālpaṃ kuryāt।। śītalaṃ kumbhakaṃ ca kurvīta ।। 260।।
gorakṣaśatakenoktam।। śivasaṃhitāyām-
ādau rajaḥ striyo yonyā yatnena vidhivat sudhīḥ।।
ākuñcya liṃganālena svaśarīre praveśayet।। 261।।
svakaṃ binduṃ ca sambodhya liṃgacālanamārabhet।।
daivāccalati cedūrdhvaṃ nibaddho yonimudrayā।। 262।।
vāmamārge patadbinduṃ dhṛtvā liṃgaṃ nivārayet।।
gurūpadeśato jñeyo huṃ huṃkāreṇa yoginaḥ।। 263।।
apānavāyumākuñcya balādākarṣayed rajaḥ।।
anena vidhinā yogī kṣipraṃ yogasya siddhaye।। 264।।
gavyabhuk kurute yogī gurupādābjapūjakaḥ।।
bindurvidhumayo jñeyo rajaḥ sūryamayastathā।। 265।।
ubhayormelanaṃ kāryaṃ svaśarīre praveśanam।।
ahaṃ bindurajaḥśaktirubhayormelanaṃ yadā।। ‌266।।
yogīnāṃ sādhanavatāṃ bhaveddi vyavapustadā।।
maraṇaṃ bindupātena jīvanaṃ bindudhāraṇāt।। 267।।
tasmādetat prayatnena kurute bindudhāraṇam।।
jāyate mriyate loke bindunā nātra saṃśayaḥ।। 268।।
etajjñātvā sadā yogī bindudhāraṇamācaret।।
siddhe bindau mahāyatnaiḥ kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale।। 269।।
yasya prasādamahimā mama sādṛśyatā bhavet।।
binduḥ karoti sarveṣāṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca saṃsthitam।। 270।।
saṃsāriṇāṃ vimūḍhānāṃ jarāmaraṇanāśanam।।
ayaṃ sukhakaro yogo yogināmuttamottamaḥ।। 271।।
abhyāsāt siddhimāpnoti bhogayukto'pi mānavaḥ।।
sa kāle sādhitārthe'pi siddhirbhavati bhūtale।। 272।।
bhuktvā bhogānaśeṣān vai yogenānena niścitam।।
anena sakalā siddhiryogināṃ bhavati dhruvam।। 273।।
svamūtrotsargakāle yo balādākṛṣya vāyunā।।
stokaṃ stokaṃ tyajenmūtram ūrdhvamākṛṣya tatpunaḥ।। 274।।
gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa pratyahaṃ yaḥ samācaret।।
bindusiddhirbhavettasya sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ।। 275।।
ṣaṇmāsamabhyasedyo vai pratyahaṃ guruśikṣayā।।
śatāṃganopabhoge'pi na bindustasya naśyati।। 276।।
siddhe yasya binduratne kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale।।
iṣṭatvaṃ yatprasādena mamāpi durlabhaṃ bhavet।। 277।।
calito'pi mahābinduḥ samprāpte'pi hutāśanam।।
vrajatyūrdhvaṃ haṭhaḥ śaktyā nibaddho yonimudrayā।। 278।।
sravate dvividho binduḥ pāṇḍuro lohitastathā।।
pāṇḍuraṃ śukramityāhurlohitaṃ ca mahārajaḥ।। 279।।
sindūradravasaṃkāśaṃ ravisthāne sthitaṃ rajaḥ।।
śaśisthāne sthito bindustayoraikyaṃ sudurlabham।। 280।।
śivo bindurajaḥ śaktiḥ bindurindu rajo raviḥ।।
ubhayoḥ saṃgamādeva prāpyate paramaṃ padam।। 281।।
vāyunā śakticāreṇa preritaṃ ca mahārajaḥ।।
yāti bindusahaikatvaṃ bhaveddivyaṃ vapustadā।। 282।।
śukraṃ candreṇa saṃyuktaṃ rajaḥ sūryeṇa saṃgatam।।
tayoḥ samarasaikatvaṃ yo jānāti sa yogavit।। 283।।
khecaryā mudritaṃ yena vivaraṃ lambikordhvataḥ।।
tasya na kṣarate binduḥ kāminyāśaleṣitasya ca।। 284।।
etad guhyatamaṃ guhyaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati।।
ṭasmādetat prayatnena gopanīyaṃ sadā budhaiḥ।। 285।।
iti śivaśatoktaṃ śivasaṃhitāyām।।
punaḥ śivayoge-
stokaṃ stokaṃ tyajenmūtraṃ ūrdhvamākṛṣya tatpunaḥ।।
gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa pratyahaṃ yaḥ samācaret।। 286।।
bindusiddhirbhavettasya sarvasiddhipradāyinī।।
etasya ṣaṇmāsābhyāsena śatāṃganopabhoge'pi na bindupātaḥ।। 287।।
anyacca purīṣatyāgakāle aṃgulībhiryonisthānaṃ pīḍayedevaṃ mūtrarodho'bhyasanīyaḥ।। evaṃ mūtratyāgakāle gudākuñcanena purīṣarodhaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ vidheyaḥ।। 288।।
evaṃ tāvadabhyaset yāvat svayameva mūtrapurīṣakālabhedena bhavata iti।। etadabhyasato'pi bindusiddhirbhavati ।। etacca bindusidhyādikaṃ mūḍhānāmeva jñānināṃ tu yathoktaṃ gītāyām ।। 289।।
vihāya kāmān yaḥ sarvān pumāṃścarati niḥspṛhaḥ।।
nirmamo nirahaṃkāraḥ sa śānti madhigacchati।। 290।।
eṣā brāhmī sthitistāta naināṃ prāpya vimuhyati।।
sthitvā syāmantakāle'pi brahmanirvāṇamṛcchati।। 291।।
vajrolyeva kiñcidviśeṣavatī amarolī sahajolī ca।। tathāhi śivayoge-
sahajolyamarolī ca vajrolī ca na bhidyate।।
yena kena prakāreṇa binduṃ yatnena dhārayet।। 292।।
daivācced bhavati bhage melanaṃ candrasūryayoḥ।।
amarolī riyaṃ proktā enāṃ nālena śoṣayet।। 293।।
nītvā binduṃ svakaṃ yogī bandhayedyonimudrayā।।
sahajoliriyaṃ proktā sarvatantreṣu gopitā।। 294।।
saṃjñābhedād bhaved bhedaḥ kārye tulyagatitrayamiti।। tathā ca svabindorūrdhvanayanaṃ vajrolī।।
ūm̐ jrāṃ phaṃ kruṃ lluṃ jaṃ jaḥ svāhā-vajrolīmantraḥ।। 295।।
bhage raktena saha militasya bindorūrdhvanayanam amarolī।। svadehe saraktasya kevalasya vā bindorbandhanaṃ sahajolīti vivekaḥ।। ‌296।।
atha śakticālanam śivayoge-
ādhārakamale suptāṃ cālayet kuṇḍalīṃ dṛḍhām।।
apānavāyumākṛṣya balādākuñcya buddhimān।। 297।।
śakticālanametaddhi pratyahaṃ yaḥ samācaret।।
agnivṛddhirbhavettasya rogāṇāṃ ca vināśanam।। 298।।
vihāya nidrāṃ bhujagī tvarayordhvaṃ vrajet khalu।।
muhūrttadvayaparyantaṃ vidhinā śakticālanam।। 299।।
yaḥ karoti prayatnena tasya siddhiradūrataḥ।।
kuṭilāṃgī kuṇḍalinī bhujaṃgī śaktirīśvarī।।
kuṇḍalyarundhatī devī śabdāḥ paryāyavācakāḥ।। 300।।
apānākarṣaṇaṃ gudākuñcanaṃ mūlādhāracālanaiḥ śaktiṃ cālayediti śaktacālanam।। etanmudrādaśakamatīva gopyamanekasiddhijanakametanmadhye siddhaikaiko'pi siddho bhavatīti dhruvam ।। 301।।
iti mudrādaśakopadeśaḥ
iti maithilasammiśrakṛṣṇadevatanayaśrībhavadevaviracite yuktabhavadevanāmni yoganibandhe saparikaraprāṇāyāmopadeśo nāma saptama upadeśaḥ।। 7।।
aṣṭamopadeśaḥ
atha pratyāhāraḥ -
sa ca svasvaviṣayebhyaḥ indriyāṇāṃ pratyāharaṇarūpaḥ।।
taduktaṃ pātañjale-
svaviṣayāsamprayoge cittasya svarūpāndhakāra ivendriyāṇāṃ pratyāharaṇaṃ pratyāhāraḥ।। tataḥ paramāvaśyatendriyāṇāmiti।। 
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-
cakṣurādīnāṃ svasvaviṣayāsamprayoge iti cittasya svarūpāndhakāra ivātmani viśrāmaḥ pratyāhāraḥ।। 1।।
tena cittasya nirvyāpāratayā rūpādyapratipattilakṣaṇasyoparam indriyajayo bhavatīti।। atra rūpādiṣvavyasanamindriyajaya ityeke।। 2।।
svecchayā teṣvindriyasamprayoga ityanye।। rāgadveṣādyabhāve sukhaduḥkhaśūnyaṃ viṣayajñānamindriyajaya ityapare।। citaikāgryāt।। 3।।
bāhyaviṣayāpratipattirevendriyajaya iti jaigīṣavyaḥ।। seyaṃ paramendriyavaśyatā cittanirodhāt bhavati iti dhyeyam।। asya pratyāhārasya catvāraḥ prakārāḥ।। 4।।
tatraiko viṣayeṣu viṣayatva buddhyā caratāmindriyāṇāṃ brahmatvenāvadhārite yatra kutrāpi brahmatvabuddhyā sthirīkaraṇam।। 5।।
taduktaṃ gorakṣanāthena -
caratāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ viṣayeṣu yathākramam।।
yatpratyāharaṇaṃ teṣāṃ pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate।। 6।।
yathā tṛtīyakālastho raviḥ pratyāharet prabhām।।
tṛtīyāṃgasthito yogī vikāraṃ mānasaṃ tathā।।
āsanādiṣaḍaṃgo yoga iti pakṣe pratyāhārasya tṛtīyāṃgatvam।।7।।
aṃgamadhye yathāṃgāni kūrmaḥ saṃkocayed dhruvam।।
yogī pratyāharatyevamindriyāṇi tathātmani।। 8।।
yaṃ yaṃ śṛṇoti karṇābhyāmapriyaṃ priyameva vā।।
taṃ tamātmeti vijñāya pratyāharati yogavit।। 9।।
amedhyamathavā medhyaṃ yaṃ yaṃ paśyati cakṣuṣā।।
taṃ tamātmeti vijñāya pratyāharati yogavit।। 10।।
agandhamathavā gandhaṃ yaṃ yaṃ jighrati nāsayā।।
taṃ tamātmeti vijñāya pratyāharati yogavit।। 11।।
aspṛśyamathavā spṛśyaṃ yaṃ yaṃ spṛśati carmaṇā।।
taṃ tamātmeti vijñāya pratyāharati yogavit।। 12।।
alaulyamathavā laulyaṃ yaṃ yaṃ śrayati jihvayā।।
taṃ tamātmeti vijñāya pratyāharati yogavit ।। ityādi।। 13।।
dvitīyastu karttavyānāṃ karmaṇāṃ yogavikṣepatayā bahistyāge'pi manasā karaṇam।।
tathā ca yājñavalkyagītāyām -
karmāṇi yāni nityāni vihatāni śarīriṇām।।
teṣāmātmanyanuṣṭhān manasā yad bahirvinā।। 14।।
pratyāhāro bhavet so'pi yogasādhanamuttamam।।
pratyāhāre praśastaṃ yatsevite yogibhiḥ sadā।। 15।।
tṛtīyastu aṣṭāśasu marmasthāneṣu pādāṃguṣṭhādārabhya mūrdhaparyantam।। ekasmāt sthānādaparasmin sthāne vāyoḥ pratyāhṛtya dhāraṇaṃ ityevaṃ prakāraḥ।। 16।।
ayamapi tatraivoktaḥ -
aṣṭādaśasu yadvāyormarmasthāneṣu dhāraṇam।।
sthānāt sthānāt samākṛṣya pratyāhāro nigadyate।। 17।।
aśvinau tu sadā brūtāṃ gārgi deva bhiṣagvarau।।
marmasthānāni siddhārthaṃ śarīre yogamokṣayoḥ।। 18।।
tāni sarvāṇi vakṣyāmi yathāvat śṛṇu suvrate।।
pādāṃguṣṭhau ca gulphau ca jaṃghāmadhye tathaiva ca।।19।।
cityormūle ca jānvośca madhye corūdvayasya ca।।
pāyumūlaṃ tathā paścāddehamadhyaṃ ca meḍhrakam।। 20।।
nābhiśca hṛdayaṃ gārgi kaṇṭhakūpaṃ tathaiva ca।।
tālumūlaṃ ca nāsāsyamūlaṃ cākṣṇośca maṇḍale।। 21।।
bhravormadhyaṃ lalāṭaṃ ca mūrddhā ca munipuṃgava।।
marmasthānāni caitāni mānaṃ teṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak।। 22।।
pādāṃguṣṭhāttu gulphalya sārddhāgula catuṣṭayam।।
ghulphājjaṃghasya madhyaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ taddaśāṃgulam।। 23।।
jaṃghāmūlāccitermūlaṃ yattadekādaśāṃgulam।।
citimūlādvarārohe jānuḥ syādaṃguladvayam।। 24।।
jānornavāṃgulaṃ prāhururumadhyaṃ munīśvarāḥ।।
ūrumadhyāttathā gārgi pāyumūlaṃ navāṃgulam।। 25।।
dehamadhyaṃ tathā pāyormūlāt sārddhāṃgulīdvayam।।
dehamadhyāttathā meḍhraṃ tadvatsārddhāṅgulīdvayam।। 26।।
meḍhrānnābhiśca vijñeyā gārgi sārddhaṃ daśāṃgulam।।
caturddaśāṃgulaṃ nābherhṛdayaṃ ca varānane।। 27।।
ṣaḍaṃgulaṃ tu hṛnmadhyāt kaṇṭhakūpamudāhṛtam।।
kaṇṭhakūpācca jihvāyā mūlāttu caturaṃgulam।।
netrasthānaṃ tu tanmūlādardhāṃgulamitīṣyate।। 29।।
tasmādarddhāṃgulaṃ viddhi bhruvorantaramātmanaḥ।।
lalāṭād vyomasaṃjñaṃ tu aṃguli trayamiṣyate।। 30।।
sthāneṣveteṣu manasā vāyumāropya dhārayet।।
sthānāt sthānāt samākṛṣya pratyāhāraṃ prakurvataḥ।।
sarve rogāḥ praṇaśyanti bhogāḥ sidhyanti tasya vai।। 31।।
caturthastu - kumbhitasya vāyormūrddhataḥ pādāṃguṣṭhaparyantamadhaḥ krameṇa sthānāt sthānāt samākṛṣya sthānāntare dhāraṇamityevaṃ prakāraḥ।। 32।।
ayamapi tatraivoktaḥ -
sampūrṇakumbhavadvāyumaṃguṣṭhānmūrdhnimadhyataḥ।।
dhārayannanilaṃ buddhyā prāṇāyāme pracodite।। 33।।
vyomarandhrāt samākṛṣya lalāṭe dhārayet punaḥ।।
lalāṭādvāyumākṛṣya bhruvormadhye varānane।। 34।।
bhruvormadhyācca jihvāyā mūle prāṇaṃ tu rodhayet।।
jihvāmūlāntamākṛṣya kaṇṭha kūpe nirodhayet।। 35।।
kaṇṭhamūlāttu hṛnmadhye hṛdayānnābhimaṇḍale।।
nābhimadhyāt punarmeḍhre meḍhrādvahnyālaye tataḥ।। 36।।
dehamadhyād gude gārgi gudādevorumūlake।।
ūrumūlāttayormadhye tasmājjānvo nirodhayet।। 37।।
citimūle tu taṃ tasmājjaṃghayormadhyatastathā।।
jaṃghāto gulphamūle tu pādāṃguṣṭhe tu gulphataḥ।। 38।।
sthānāt sthānāt samākṛṣya yastvevaṃ dhārayet sudhīḥ।।
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā jīvedācandratārakam।। 39।।
nāḍībhyāṃ vāyumākṛṣya kuṇḍalyāḥ pārśvayoḥ kṣipet।।
dhārayedyugapat so'pi bhavarogādvimucyate।। 40।।
evaṃ vāyuṃ nāsābhyāmākṛṣya hṛdayavyomni udare bhrūmadhye vā dhārayet sarvarogapraśamanaṃ cirajīvitā brahmāvabodhaḥ pāpakṣayaḥ puṇyaṃ duḥkhānutpattiḥ paramānandaśca bhavati।। 41।।
ātmanaḥ prāṇamāropya bhruvormadhye suṣumnayā।।
yāvanmanolayastatra tāvatsaṃyamanaṃ kuru।। 42।।
viśuddhicakrastha candramaṇḍalāt plutasya nābhisthasūryamaṇḍale patato'mṛtasya jālandharabandhapūrvakaṃ tālumūle jihvāyāḥ sthāpanena viparītākṛtimudrayā yāvatpratyāhṛtya jihvāgradvārā svaśarīre dhāraṇaṃ tadapi pratyāhāra eveti।। 43।।
gorakṣanāthaḥ yadāha-
candrāmṛtamayīṃ dhārāṃ pratyāharati bhāskaraḥ।।
yatpratyāharaṇaṃ tasyāḥ pratyāhāraḥ sa ucyate।। 44।।
iti maithilasammiśraśrīkṛṣṇadevatanayaśrībhavadevaviracite yuktabhavadevanāmni granthe pratyāhāropadeśo nāmāṣṭama upadeśaḥ।। 8।।
yuktabhavadevaḥ
navamopadeśaḥ
atha dhāraṇā-
pātañjale -
deśabandhaścittasya dhāraṇeti।।
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -
yamādiguṇayuktasya manasaḥ cidānandasvarūpe brahmaṇi hṛdākāśamahākāśayoraikye jānvādau deśaviśeṣe yaccirataraṃ dhāraṇaṃ taddhāraṇetyucyate।। 1।।
sā ca śarīre pañcadhā।। tathāhi pādādijānuparyantaṃ pṛthivīsthānaṃ laṃ-bījānvitam।। tatra brahmāṇaṃ saśaktikaṃ caturmukhaṃ sṛṣṭikarttāramātmānaṃ dhyātvā ghaṭikāpañcakaṃ tatra prāṇaṃ manasā dhārayitvā pṛthivīṃ jayati।। 2।।
evamājānvoḥ pāyuparyantamapāṃ sthānaṃ vaṃ-bījānvitaṃ tatra nārāyaṇaṃ śuklaṃ pītāmbaraṃ caturbhujaṃ salakṣmīkaṃ śucismitamātmarūpaṃ dhyātvā pañcaghaṭikā prāṇaṃ manasi dhārayitvā jalaṃ jayati ।। 3।।
evamāpāyoḥ hṛdayāntaṃ vahnisthānaṃ raṃ-bījānvitam।। tatra trinetraṃ prasannaṃ bhasmāṃgarāgaṃ rudraṃ sapārvatīkaṃ dhyātvā pañcaghaṭikāstatra saprāṇaṃ mano dhṛtvā tejāṃsi jayati।। 4।।
evamāhṛnmadhyāt bhrūmadhyaparyanta yaṃ-bījānvitaṃ vāyusthānam।। tatreśvaraṃ sarvaśaktikaṃ māyāśarīramātmatvena dhyātvā pañcaghaṭikāḥ saprāṇaṃ mano dhṛtvā vāyuṃ jayet।। vāyuvadākāśaṃ jayati।। 5।।
ābhrūmadhyānmūrddhāntam ākāśasthānaṃ haṃ-bījānvitaṃ tatra vyomarūpaṃ mahādevaṃ pañcavaktraṃ trilocanaṃ daśabāhūdyatāstraṃ ca candramaulimumārddhakaṃ dhyātvā muhūrttamapi saprāṇaṃ mano dhṛtvā mukto bhavati ākāśaṃ jayati ca ।। 6।।
