evaṃ pañca।। atra dhāraṇāyāṃ saṃhāre karttavye yathā pūrvoktāṃ brahmādisahitāṃ pṛthvīm ।। yathokte jale jalaṃ tejasi tejo vāyau vāyumākāśe ākāśamātmani parabrahmaṇi cidānande saṃhṛtya tanmātre saprāṇaṃ mano dhārayet।। 7।।
sṛṣṭau punastadā bhūtādākāśādvāyuṃ vāyostejaḥ tejaso jalaṃ jalācca pṛthivīmuktarūpāmutpādya brahmajñānāmṛtairajasraṃ pūrayet।। 8।।
sthitau tu yathāsthitameva pṛthvyādikam।। tattadadhiṣṭhātṛbrahmātmavattayā yāvaditthaṃ bhāvayet।। bhāvitaṃ satsvādhīnaṃ bhavati।। atra ājānornābhiparyantaṃ śikhisthānamiti kecit।। 9।।
dehamadhye śikhisthānaṃ taptacāmīkaraprabhamityādinā gudameḍhrāntarāle dehamadhye śikhisthānasyoktatvānnaivamiti।। bhagavān yājñavalkyaḥ ।। 10।।
śrīgorakṣanāthastu-
hṛdayakaṇṭhatālumūlabhrūmadhyabrahmarandhrāṇi pṛthvyādidhāraṇāsthānāni iti prāha।। 11।।
bhāvanāyāḥ prādhānyāt sarve'pi pakṣāḥ saphalā iti guravaḥ ।। dhāraṇāṃ kurvatastvaṃghrau sarve naśyanti vātajā ityādi pūrvoktaphalamapyatrānusandheyam।। 12।।
etāḥ pṛthvyādidhāraṇāḥ -stambhinī 1 drāviṇī 2 dahanī 3 bhrāmiṇī 4 śoṣiṇī 5 nāmnyaḥ।। 13।।
tathā ca gorakṣanāthaḥ -
stambhinī drāviṇī caiva dahanī bhrāmiṇī tathā।।
śoṣiṇī ca bhavatyeṣā bhūtānāṃ pañcadhāraṇāḥ।। 14।।
karmaṇā manasā vācā dhāraṇāḥ pañca durlabhāḥ।।
vijñāya satataṃ yogī sarvaduḥkhaiḥ pramucyate।। 15।।
iti śrīyuktabhavadeve dhāraṇopadeśo nāma navama upadeśaḥ।। 9।।
yuktabhavadevaḥ
daśamopadeśaḥ
atha dhyānam -
taccoktaṃ pātañjale -
tatra pratyayaikatānatā dhyānamiti।। tasmin deśe dhyeyālambanasya pratyayasyaikatānatā sadṛśaḥ pravāhaḥ pratyayāntareṇāparāmṛṣṭo dhyānamityucyate।। 1।।
gorakṣanāthastu-
smṛtyaiva sarvacintāyāṃ dhātureko hi paṭhayate।।
yaccitte nirmale cintā taddhi dhyānaṃ pracakṣate।। 2।।
dvividhaṃ bhavati dhyānaṃ sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ tathā।।
sakalavarṇabhedena niṣkalaṃ nirguṇaṃ bhavet।। 3।।
antaśceto bahiścakṣuradhaḥ sthāpya sukhāsanam।।
samatvaṃ ca śarīrasya dhyānamudreti siddhadā ।। 4।।
aśvamedhasahasrāṇi vājapeyaśatāni ca।।
ekasya dhyānayogasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm।। 5।।
ādhāraṃ prathamaṃ cakraṃ svarṇābhaṃ ca caturddalam।।
kuṇḍalinyā samāyuktaṃ dhyātvā mucyeta kitviṣaiḥ।। 6।।
svādhiṣṭhāne ca ṣaṭpatre sanmāṇikyasamaprabhe।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā yogī sukhī bhavet।। 7।।
taruṇādityasaṃkāśe cakre'smin maṇipūrake।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā saṃkṣobhayejjagat।। 8।।
hṛdākāśe sthitaṃ śambhuṃ pracaṇḍaṃ ravitejasam।।
nāsāgre dṛṣṭimādhāya yogī brahmamayo bhavet।। 9।।
vidyutprabhe ca hṛtpadme prāṇāyāmivabhedataḥ।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā brahmamayo bhavet।। 10।।
satataṃ ghaṇṭikāmadhye viśuddhe dīpakaprabhe।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā duḥkhaṃ vimuñcati।। 11।।
sravatpīyūṣasampūrṇe lambikācandramaṇḍale।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā mṛtyuṃ vimuñcati।। 12।।
bhruvorantargataṃ devaṃ snigdhamauktikasannibham।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvānandamayo bhavet।। 13।।
dhyāyannīlanibhaṃ nityaṃ bhrūmadhye parameśvaram।।
ātmānaṃ vijitaprāṇo yogī yoga mavāpnuyāt।। 14।।
nirguṇaṃ ca śivaṃ śāntaṃ gagane viśvatomukham।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā brahmamayo bhavet।। 15।।
ākāśe yatra śabdaḥ syādājñācakraṃ taducyate।।
tatrātmānaṃ śivaṃ dhyātvā yogī jñānamavāpnuyāt।। 16।।
nirmalaṃ gaganākāraṃ marīcijalasannibham।।
ātmānaṃ sarvagaṃ dhyātvā yogī muktimanāpnuyāt।। 17।।
gudaṃ meḍhraṃ ca nāsā ca hṛtpadmaṃ candramṇḍalam।।
ghaṇṭikā lambikāsthānaṃ bhrūmadhyaṃ ca nabhobilam।। 18।।
kathitāni tavaitāni dhyānasthānāni yoginām।।
upādhitatvayuktāni kurvannaṣṭaguṇodayam।। 19।।
eṣu brahmātmakaṃ tejaḥ śivaṃ jyotiranuttamam।।
jñātvā dhyātvā vimuktaḥ syāditi gorakṣabhāṣitam।। 20।।
yājñavalkyagītāyāṃ nirguṇaṃ saguṇaṃ ca dhyānamuktam -
marmasthānāni nāḍīnāṃ saṃsthānaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak।।
vāyūnāṃ sthānakarmāṇi jñātvā kurvātmavedanam।। 21।।
ekaṃ jyotirmayaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvagaṃ vyomagaṃ dṛḍham।।
atyantamacalaṃ nityamādimadhyāntavarjitam।। 22।।
sthūlasūkṣmamanākārama savarṇamacakṣuṣam।।
na rasaṃ na ca ganadhāḍhyamaprameyamanāmayam।। 23।।
ānandamajaraṃ nityaṃ sadasatsarvakāraṇam।।
sarvādhāraṃ jagadrūpamamūrttamajamavyayam।। 24।।
adṛśyaṃ dṛśyamantaḥsthaṃ bahiḥsthaṃ sarvatomukham।।
sarvadṛk sarvataḥ pādaṃ sarvaspṛk sarvataḥ śiraḥ।। 25।।
brahma brahmamayo'haṃ syāmiti yadvedanaṃ bhavet।।
tadetannirguṇaṃ dhyānamiti brahmavido viduḥ।। 26।।
saguṇaṃ cānekadhā prāha-
athavā paramātmānaṃ paramātmānaṃ paramānandamavyayam।।
gurūpadeśādvijñāya puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṃgalam।। 27।।
brahmabhūte pure cāsmin daharākhye khamadhyame।।
abhyāsāt samprapaśyanti santaḥ saṃsārabheṣajam।। 28।।
hṛtpadme'ṣṭadalopete kandamadhyāt samutthite।।
dvādaśāṃgulanāle'smin caturaṃgulamunmukhe।। 29।।
prāṇāyāmairvikasite kesarānvitakarṇike।।
vāsudevaṃ jagadyoniṃ nārāyaṇamajaṃ vibhum।। 30।।
caturbhujamudārāṃgaṃ śaṃkhacakragadādharam।।
kirīṭakeyūradharaṃ padmapatranibhekṣaṇam।। 31।।
śrīvatsa vakṣasaṃ śrīśaṃ pūrṇacandranibhānanam।।
padmotpaladalābhāsaṃ suprasannaṃ śucismitam।। 32।।
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ pītavāsasamacyutam।।
padmacchavi padadvandvaṃ paramātmānamavyayam।। 33।।
prabhābhirbhāsayad rūpaṃ paritaḥ puruṣottamam।।
manasālokya deveśaṃ sarvabhūtahṛdisthitam।।
so'hamātmeti vijñānaṃ saguṇaṃ dhyānamucyate।। 34।।
athavā -
hṛtsaroruhamadhye'smin prakṛtyātmakakarṇike।।
aṣṭaiśvaryadalopete vidyākesara saṃyute।। 35।।
jñānanāle bṛhatkande prāṇāyāmaprabodhite।।
viśvārciṣaṃ mahāvahniṃ jvalantaṃ viśvatomukham।। 36।।
vaiśvānaraṃ jagadyoniṃ śikhastatvānamīśvaram।।
tāpayantaṃ svakaṃ deha māpādatalamastakam।। 37।।
nirvātadīpavat tasmin dīpikāhavyavāhanam।।
dṛṣṭvā tasya śikāmadhye paramātmanāmakṣaram।। 38।।
nīlatoyadamadhyasthaṃ vidyullekheva bhāsvaram।।
nīvāraśūkavadrūpaṃ pītābhaṃ sarvakāraṇam।। 39।।
jñātvā vaiśvānaraṃ devaṃ so'hamasmīti yā matiḥ।।
saguṇeṣūttamaṃ hyetad dhyānaṃ dhyānavido viduḥ।।
vaiśvānaratvaṃ samprāpya muktiṃ tenaiva gacchati।। 40।।
athavā maṇḍalaṃ paśyedādityasya mahātmanaḥ।।
ātmānaṃ sarvajagataḥ puruṣaṃ hemarūpiṇam।। 41।।
hiraṇyaśmaśrukeśaṃ ca hiraṇmayanakhaṃ harim।।
rathāsanaṃ caturvaktraṃ sṛṣṭisthityantakāraṇam।। 42।।
padmāsanasthitaṃ saumyaṃ prabuddhakamalānanam।।
padmodaradalābhāsaṃ sarvalokābhayapradam।। 43।।
bhāsayantaṃ jagatsarvaṃ dṛṣṭvā lokaikasākṣiṇam।।
so'hamātmeti yā buddhiḥ sā cā dhyāneṣu kathyate।। 44।।
dhyānenānena saureṇa muktaṃ yāsyanti sūriṇaḥ।।
eṣa eva tu mokṣasya mahāmārgastapodhane।। 45।।
athavā-
bhruvormadhyāntarātmānaṃ bhārūpaṃ sarvakāraṇam।।
sthāṇuvanmūrdhnyaparyantaṃ madhyadehāt samutthitam।। 46।।
jagatkāraṇamavyaktaṃ jvalantamamitaujasam।।
manasālokya so'haṃ syādityetad dhyānamuttamam।। 47।।
athavā baddhaparyakaḥ śithilīkṛtavigrahaḥ।।
śiva eva svayaṃ bhūtva nāsāgrā ropitekṣaṇaḥ।। 48।।
nirvikāraṃ paraṃ śāntaṃ paramātmānamavyayam।। 
bhārūpamamṛtaṃ dhyāyet bhruvormadhye varānane।। 49।।
so'hamātmeti yā buddhiḥ sā ca dhyānṣu kathyate।।
athavāṣṭadalopete karṇikākesarānvite।। 50।।
unnidrahṛdayāmbhoje somamaṇḍalamadhyage।।
svātmānamarbhakākāraṃ bhokturūpiṇamavyayam।। 51।।
sudhārasaṃ vimuñcadbhiḥ śaśiraśmibhirāvṛtam।।
ṣoḍaśacchadasaṃyuktaśiraḥ padmādadhomukham।। 52।।
nirgatāmṛtadhārābhiḥ sahasrābhiḥ samantataḥ।।
plāvitaṃ puruṣaṃ cintayitvā samāhitaḥ।। 53।।
tenāmṛtarasenaiva sāṃgopāṃge kalevare।।
ahameva paraṃ brahma paramātmāhamavyayaḥ।। 54।।
evaṃ tadvedanaṃ tacca saguṇaṃ dhyānamucyate।।
evaṃ dhyānāmṛtaṃ kurvan ṣaṇmāsānmṛtyujid bhavet।। 55।।
vatsarānmukta eva syājjīvanneva na saṃśayaḥ।।
jīvanmuktasya na kvāpi duḥkhāvāptiḥ kathañcana।। 56।।
kiṃ punarnityamuktasya muktireva hi durlabhā।।
tasmāttvaṃ ca varārohe phalaṃ tyaktvaiva nityaśaḥ।।
kurvāṇā karma vidhivad dhyānameva samācaret।। iti । 57।।
agnipurāṇe-
dhyai cintāyāṃ smṛto dhāturviṣṇucintā muhurmuhuḥ।।
anakṣiptena manasā dhyānamityabhidhīyate।। 58।।
dhyeyāvasthitacittasya pradeśe yatra kutracit।।
dhyānametat samuddiṣṭaṃ pratyayasyaikatānatā।। 59।।
evaṃ dhyānasamāyuktaḥ svadehaṃ yaḥ parityajet।।
kulasvajanamitrāṇi samuddhṛtya harirbhavet।।60।।
evaṃ muhūrttamarddhaṃ vā dhyāyedyaḥ śraddhayā harim।।
so'pi yāṃ gatimāpnoti na tāṃ sarvaiḥ mahāmakhaiḥ।। 61।।
jñānavairāgyasampannaḥ śraddadhānaḥ kṣamānvitaḥ।।
viṣṇubhaktaḥ sadotsāhī dhyānāya puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ।। 62।।
mūrttāmūrttaṃ paraṃ brahmaharidhyānaṃ hi cintanam।।
sakalo niḥkalo dhyeyaḥ sarvajñaḥ paramo hariḥ।। 63।।
aṇimādiguṇaiśvaryaṃ muktirdhyāna prayojanam।।
gacchan tiṣṭhan svapan jāgran unmiṣan nimiṣannapi।। 64।।
śucirvāpyaśucirvāpi dhyāyet satatamīśvaram।।
dhyānayajñaḥ paraṃ śuddhaḥ sarvadoṣavivarjitaḥ।। 65।।
hiṃsādoṣavimuktaśca viśuddhaḥ sthitasādhanaḥ।।
dhyānayajñaḥ paro'nyasmāda pavargaphalapradaḥ।। 66।।
hṛdi aṃguṣṭhamātramamalaṃ dhyāyedoṃkāramīśvaram।।
kadambagolakākāraṃ tārārūpamiva sthitam।। 67।।
caturbhujādi vā-
dhyānācchrānto japenmantraṃ japācchrāntaśca cintayet।। 
japadhyānābhiyuktasya viṣṇuḥ śīghraṃ prasīdati।। 68।।
japayajñasya vai yajñāḥ kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm।।
japinaṃ nopasarpanti vyādhayaścādhayo grahāḥ।।
bhuktimuktyadisakalaṃ japena prāpyate phalam।। 69।।
japyamoṃkārādi।। avayavaśaktisamudāyaśaktibhyāṃ sātvikarājasatāmasādibhedena triprakāraka viśvātmaka brahmaśarīrasakaladikkālavartticaitanyānandasvarūpabrahmādīṣṭa daivatamiti śivam।। 70।।
iti sammiśraśrīkṛṣṇadevatanayaśrībhavadevaviracite yuktabhavadevanāmni yoganibandhe dhyānopadeśo nāma daśama upadeśaḥ।। 10।।
yuktabhavadevaḥ
ekādaśopadeśaḥ
atha samādhiḥ -
tatrāpyekaṃ buddhibhirgrāhyam।। uditaghaṭādidharmaviśiṣṭabhūtasūkṣmamālambanī bhūtaṃ samādhiprajñāyāmupatiṣṭhate।। 1।।
yā punaḥ sarvathā sarvataḥ śāntoditā vyapadeśyadharmānavacchinneṣu sarvadharmānupātiṣu।। sarvadharmātmakeṣu samāpattiḥ sā nirvicāretyucyate।। 2।।
evaṃ svarūpaṃ hi tadbhūtaṃ etenaiva yaḥ svarūpenālambanībhūtaṃ samādhiprajñāparaṃ jayati ।। 3।।
prajñā ca svarūpaṃ hi tadbhūtasūkṣmaṃ prajñā ca svarūpaśūnyevārthamātranirbhāsā yadā bhavati tadā nirvicāretyucyate।। 4।।
tadayaṃ samudāyārthaḥ।। pṛthivyādau sthūlatayā śāstreṣu prasiddho'vayavini savitarkā nirvitarkā ceti samāpattidvayaṃ bhavati।। 5।।
avayavasamudāyairevāvayavisādhya siddhau।। avayavino'satva bhāvanayā'vayavisatvāsatvakoṭikasandehasambhavena vitarkavitarkāpagamayoḥ sambhavāt।। 6।।
avayavihetutayā siddheṣu paramāṇutanmātrādiṣu sūkṣmatayā śāstreṣu prasiddheṣu avayavihetutvalakṣaṇaviśeṣadarśanāt।। tatsatvakoṭikasandehāsambhavena vitarkāsambhavāt ।। 7।।
savitarkādyabhāve savicārā nirvicārā ceti samāpattidvayaṃ bhavati।।tatra vitarkābhāve'pi kasya kaḥ sūkṣmaḥ iti vicārasambhavāt।। 8।।
yogena yatkāryam upādānakāraṇe sūkṣmāvasthayā prāgapyasti।। sthūlā vasthayā madhye'bhivyaktaṃ bhavati ante'pi sūkṣmāvasthayā līyate iti।। yasya yadupādānakāraṇaṃ tasya tatsūkṣmamityabhidhīyate।।9।।
tadatra sthūlapārthivādeḥ paramāṇudvayaṇukānaṃgīkāre trasareṇustadaṃgīkāre tu pārthivaparamāṇvādiḥ sūkṣmaḥ।। pārthivasyāṇoḥ punaḥ prādhānyena gandhatanmātraṃ guṇībhāvena rasatanmātrādicatuṣṭayamapi sūkṣmaḥ।। 10।।
evamāpyasyāṇoḥ prādhanyena rasatanmātraṃ guṇībhāvena rūpatanmātrāditrayamapi tejaḥ paramāṇoḥ prādhānyena rūpatanmātraṃ guṇībhāvena svarśa tanmātrādidvayamapi vāyavīyasyāṇoḥ sparśatanmātraṃ prādhānyena śabdatanmātramapi ākāśasya śabdatanmātrameveti।। tanmātrāṇāmahaṃkāraḥ sūkṣmaḥ।। 11।।
ahaṃkārasyāpi mahattatvam।। mahattatvasyāpi prakṛteḥ।। prakṛtau tu sūkṣmātiśayamasāptiriti।। 
taduktaṃ pātañjale-
sūkṣmaviṣayatvaṃ cāliṃgaparyavasānam।। 12।।
kasyāpi na liṃgamityaliṃgaṃ prakṛtiriti।। nanu prakṛterapi sūkṣmaḥ puruṣo'stīti kathaṃ prakṛtāveva saukṣmyātiśayasamāptiriti cet।। satyam।। parantu na puruṣaḥ prakṛterupādānakāraṇamiti।। abhinnavastu।। 13।।
mahattatvāderviśvasyaiva sūkṣmaḥ prakṛtiviśiṣṭaḥ pumāniti।। tatrāpi savicārānirvicārā samāpattidvayamapīti।। atra yadyapi sthūle'pi savicārādidvayaṃ sambhavati।। 14।।
tatrāpi vicārasyāsambhavāt ।। evaṃ ca sūkṣme'pi savitarkādidvayaṃ sambhavati।। nānāvidhasya vitarkasya tatrāpi sambhavāditi।। 15।।
tathāpi pūrvoktayukteḥ paribhāṣāyāḥ svecchādhīnatayā yogibhistathā dvayaṃ bhāṣita tvācca sthūlālambanamādyasamāpattidvayam।। sūkṣmālambanaṃ cāntyadvayamiti।। sāmpradāyikamiti dik।। 16।।
atha samādhiprayogaḥ -
sarvavidhaśaucasantoṣeśvarapraṇidhānādilakṣaṇāvikṣepakaniyamayukto yogī।। sukhāsane cailādyāsane sukhaṃ samupaviśyānudvignaḥ।। prāṇāyāmena pātakendhanādi dagdhvā।। pratyāhāreṇa indriyajayakārakeṇa mano'dhikatarān kāmakrodhādīn parihṛtya dhāraṇayā hṛdayādau prāṇamanasī sadhairyamekīkṛtya pūrvoktābhīṣṭadaivata dhyānapūrvakaṃ samāsādya।। 17।।
prathamataḥ sthūlabhūtabhautikaviṣayāṃ sati vitarke savitarkām।। vitarkavigame nirvitarkāṃ samāpattiṃ samabhyasya।। paramāṇuṃ pañcatanmātrāhaṃkāramahattatvapradhānalakṣaṇabhūtasūkṣmaviṣayāṃ sati vicāre savicārā nāsati nirvicārāṃ samāpattiṃ samabhyaset।। evaṃ vitarkavicārānugataṃ samādhidvayamabhyasya।। buddhilakṣaṇāsinālambanatayā tadanugataṃ samādhimabhyaset।। 18।।
atha tattadghaṭādiviṣayākārākāritāntaḥkaraṇa vikārādyāstraiguṇyādacaitanyasvabhāvāyāḥ।। tattatsatvādiguṇaprābalyānusāriṇāmaśīlāyā dṛśyāyāḥ buddheravikāraṃ traiguṇyavirahāccetanasvabhāvapariṇāminaṃ sarvasākṣiṇaṃ sarvabhoktāraṃ dharmādyaṣṭaguṇavad buddhi bhoktāraṃ karmmādivattayā pratibhāsamānaṃ tadbuddhipratibimbādyavacchinnamahamityevamadhyavasīyaṃ svakīyamātmānaṃ --- vādyavacchinnānandasvarūpaṃ manasa ālambanīkṛtyavacchinnānandānugataṃ samādhimabhyaset।। 19।।
tataḥ punaḥ guruparamparayopadiṣṭaṃ tatvamasītyādimahāvākyārthamanusmṛtyatattadbuddhipratibimbādyavacchedakabhedena svaparabrahmaviṣṇuśiveśvarasadāśivaśakrādidikpālasūryādigrahaśarīreṣu।। ghaṭagraha grāmanagaradeśādyupādhibhedenākāśa ivaiko'pi mitho'bhibhāsate ।।20।।
bhagavānātmeti vibhāvyam।। tattadabuddhipratibimbalakṣaṇāvacchedakopādhiṃ parihṛtyākāśavadeva sarvagata saccidānandasvarūpamavabhāvayedityeṣo'navacchinnānandānugataḥ samādhiḥ।। 21।।
sa cātmā sarveṣu pṛthivījalavahnicandratārakāsūryavāyvākāśadigvidyutstanayitnusarvaloka sarvaveda sarvayajña sarvabhūtasarvaprāṇavākcakṣuḥśrotrādimanoretaḥ śarīrādiṣu tiṣṭhate'ntānantayamiti।। 22।।
sarvamapyete acetanatvāt।। enaṃ na viduretasya sarvāṇyetāni śarīrāṇi sarvāntaryāmyamṛtaścāsāviti।। tathā ca śrutiḥ।। yaḥ sarveṣu lokeṣu tiṣṭhat sarvebhyo lokebhyo'ntaro'yaṃ sarve lokāna viduryasya sarve lokāḥ śarīraṃ yaḥ sarvān lokānantaro yamayati sata ātmā antaryāmyamṛta ityādi।। sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma brahmaivedaṃ sarvam ātmaivedaṃ sarvamityādi।। 23।।
gorakṣanāthaśca-
upādhiśca tathā tatvaṃ dvayametadudāhṛtam।।
upādhiḥ procyate varṇastatvātmābhidhīyate।। 24।।
upādheranyathā jñānaṃ tatvasaṃsthitiranyathā।।
samastopādhividhvaṃsī sadābhyāsena jāyate।। 25।।
śabdādīnāṃ ca tanmātraṃ yāvat karṇādiṣu sthitam।।
tāvadeva sthitaṃ dhyānaṃ samādhiḥ syādataḥ param।। 26।।
dhāraṇaṃ pañcanāḍībhirdhyānaṃ syāt ṣaṭkanāḍikāḥ।।
ghaṭikādvādaśena syāt samādhiḥ prāṇasaṃyamāt।। 27।।
yatsarvadvandvayoraikyaṃ jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ।।
samastanaṣṭasaṃkalpaḥ samādhiḥ so'bhidhīyate।। 28।।
ambusaindhavayoraikyaṃ yathā bhavati yogataḥ।। 
tathātmamanasoraikyaṃ samādhirabhidhīyate।। 29।।
yadā saṃkṣīyate prāṇo mānasaṃ ca pralīyate।।
yadā samarasatvaṃ ca samādhiḥ procyate tadā।। 30।।
na gandhaṃ na rasaṃ sparśaṃ na rūpaṃ na ca niḥsvanam।।
nātmānaṃ ca paraṃ vetti yogī yuktaḥ samādhinā।। 31।।
khādyate na sa kālena bādhyate na ca karmaṇā।।
sādhyate na ca kenāpi yogī yuktaḥ samādhinā।। 32।।
abhedyaḥ sarvaśastrāṇāmavadhyaḥ sarvadehinām।।
agrāhyo mantrayantrāṇāṃ yogī yuktaḥ samādhinā।। 33।।
yuktāhāravihārasya yuktaceṣṭasya karmasu।
yuktasvapnāvabodhasya yogo bhavati duḥkhahā।। 34।।
nirādyantaṃ nirālambaṃ niḥprapañcaṃ nirāśrayam।।
nirāmayaṃ nirākāraṃ tatvaṃ tatvavido viduḥ।। 35।।
nirmalaṃ niścalaṃ nityaṃ nirguṇaṃ niḥkṛtaṃ mahat।।
vyomavijñānamānandamevaṃ brahmavido viduḥ।। 36।।
nirātaṃkaṃ nirālambaṃ nirādhāraṃ nirāmayam।।
yogī yogavidhānena pare brahmaṇi līyate।। 37।।
hetaduṣṭāntamirmuktaṃ manobuddheragocaram।।
vyomavijñānamānandaṃ tatvaṃ tatvavido viduḥ।। 38।।
yathā ghṛte ghṛtaṃ kṣiptaṃ ghṛtameva hi jāyate।।
kṣīre kṣīraṃ tathā yogī tatvameva hi jāyate।। 39।।
kṣīre kṣīraṃ ghṛte sarpirvahnau vahnirivārpitaḥ।।
tanmayatvaṃ vrajatyeva yogī līnaḥ pare pade।। 40।।
agnipurāṇe -
dehendriyamanobuddhi prāṇā haṃkāravarjitam।।
jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyā ca muktaṃ brahmaturīyakam।। 41।।
nitya tatvaṃ buddhiyuktaṃ nityamānandamavyayam।।
brahmāhamasmyahaṃ brahma sa vijñānavimuktaye ।। 42।।
brahmāhamātmetyevaṃ syāt samādhirmokṣadaḥ paraḥ।। iti ।। 43।।
tathā -
evamekamidaṃ viddhi na bhedo sakalaṃ jagat।।
vāsudevābhidheyasya svarūpaṃ paramātmanaḥ।।
iti niṣadaṃ prati ṛbhuvākyam।। 44।।
tathā -
ekaḥ samastastvaṃ cāhaṃ viṣṇuḥ sarvādirīśvaraḥ।।
pītanīlādi bhedena yathaikaṃ dṛśyate nabhaḥ।।
bhrānti dṛṣṭibhirātmāpi tathaikaḥ sa pṛthak pṛthak।। 45।।
tathā bhagavadvākyam -
brahmādistambaparyantāḥ sarvā mama vibhūtayaḥ।।
śrīmantaścorjitāḥ sarve mamāṃśāḥ prāṇinaḥ smṛtāḥ।।
ahameko viśvarūpo iti jñātvā vimucyate।। iti।। 46।।
karmaṇā manasā vācā sarvāvasthāsu sarvadā।।
brahmādistambaparyantaṃ jagadviṣṇuṃ careddhiyā।।
siddhimāpnoti bhagavadbhakto bhāgavato dhruvam।। 47।।
bhagavadgītāyām -
jñeyaṃ yattatpravakṣyāmi yajjñātvāmṛtamaśnute।।
anādiparamaṃ brahma na sa tannāsaducyate।। 48।।
sarvataḥ pāṇipādaṃ tat sarvato'kṣiśiromukham।।
sarvataḥ śrutimalloke sarvamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati।। 49।।
sarvendriyaguṇābhāsaṃ sarvendriyavivarjitam।।
asaktaṃ sarvabhṛccaiva nirguṇaṃ guṇabhoktṛ ca।। 50।।
bahirantaśca bhūtānāmacalaṃ calameva ca।।
sūkṣmatvāttadavijñeyaṃ dūrasthaṃ cāntike'pi tat।। 51।।
avibhaktaṃ ca bhūteṣu vibhaktamiva ca sthitam।।
bhūtabharttṛ ca vijñeyaṃ grasiṣṇuprabhaviṣṇu ca।। 52।।
jyotiṣāmapi tajjyotistamasaḥ paramucyate।।
jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ jñānagamyaṃ hṛdi sarvasyādhiṣṭhitam।। 53।।
dhyānenātmani paśyanti kecidātmānamātmanā।।
anye sāṃkhyena yogena karmayogena cāpare।। 54।।
anye tvevamajānanta śrutvā tebhya upāsate।।
te'pi cāpi tarantyeva mṛtyuṃ śrutiparāyaṇāḥ।। iti bhāgavate।। 55।।
viśeṣastasya deho'yaṃ sthaviṣṭhaśca sthavīyasām।।
yatredaṃ dṛśyate viśvaṃ bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhavacca yat ।। 56।।
āṇḍakośe śarīre'smin saptāvaraṇasaṃyute।।
vairājaḥ puruṣo yo'sau bhagavān dhāraṇāśrayaḥ।। 57।।
pātālametasya hi pādamūlaṃ paṭhanti pārṣṇiprapade rasātalam।।
mahātalaṃ viśvasṛjo'sya gulphau talātalaṃ vai puruṣasya jaṃghe।। 58।।
dve jānunī sutalaṃ viśvamūrtte rūrudvayaṃ vitalaṃ cātalaṃ ca।।
mahītalaṃ tajjaghanaṃ mahīpate nabhastalaṃ nābisaro gṛṇanti।। 59।।
uraḥsthalaṃ jyotiranīkamasya grīvāmaharvadanaṃ vai jano'sya।।
tapo lalāṭaṃ vidurādipuṃsaḥ satyaṃ tu śīrṣāṇi sahasraśīrṣaḥ।। 60।।
indrādayo bāhava āhurusrāḥ karṇau diśaḥ śrotramamuṣyaśabdaḥ।।
nāsatyadasrau paramasya nāse ghrāṇaśca gandho mukhamagniriddhaḥ।। 61।।
dyaurakṣimī cakṣurabhūtpataṃgaḥ pakṣmāṇi viṣṇorahanī ubhe ca।।
tadbhrūvijṛmbhaḥ parameṣṭhidhiṣṇyamāpo'sya tālū rasa eva jihvā।। 62।।
chandāṃsyanantasya śiro gṛṇanti daṃṣṭrā yamaḥ snehakalā dvijāni।।
hāso janonmādakarī ca mayā durantasargo yadapāṃpamokṣaḥ।। 63।।
vrīḍottaroṣṭho'dhara eva lobho dharmastanau'dharmapathaśca pṛṣṭhaḥ।।
kastasya meḍhraṃ vṛṣaṇau ca mitrau kukṣi) samudrā girayo'sthisaṃghāḥ।। 64।।
nadyo'sya nāḍyo'tha tanūruhāṇi mahīruhā viśvatanornarendraḥ।।
anantavīryaṃ śvasitaṃ mātariśvā gatirvayo karmaguṇapravāhaḥ।। 65।।
īśasya keśān vidurambuvāhā vāsastu sandhyā kuruvaryabhūmnaḥ।।
avyaktamāhurhṛdayaṃ manaśca sa candramāḥ sarvavikārakoṣaḥ।। 66।।
vijñānaśaktiṃ mahimāmananti sarvātmano'ntaḥkaraṇaṃ giritram।।
aśvāśvataryuṣṭragajānakhāni sarve mṛgāḥ paśavaḥ śroṇideśe।। 67।।
vayāṃsi tadvyākaraṇaṃ vicitraṃ manurmanīṣā manujo nivāsaḥ।।
gandharvavidyādhara cāraṇāpsaraḥ svarasmṛtīrasurānī kavaryaḥ।। 68।।
brahmānanaṃ kṣatrabhujo mahātmā viḍūruherandhraśrita kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ।।
nānāvidhābhījya guṇopapanno dravyātmakaḥ karmavitānayogaḥ।। 69।।
iyānasāvīśvaravigrahasya yaḥ sanniveśaḥ kathito mayā te।।
sandhāryate'sminvapuṣi sthaviṣṭhe manaḥ svabuddhyā nayato'sti kiñcit।। 70।।
sa sarvadhīvṛtyanubhūtasarva ātmā yathā svapnajanekṣitaikaḥ।।
taṃ satyamānandanidhiṃ bhajeta nānyatra sajjedyata ātmapātaḥ।। ityādi।। 71।।
śrībhagavānuvāca -
jitendriyasya yuktasya jitaśvāsasya yoginaḥ।।
mayi dhārayataścet upatiṣṭhanti siddhayaḥ।। 72।।
siddhayo'ṣṭādaśa proktā dhāraṇāyogapāragaiḥ।।
tāsāmaṣṭau matpradhānāḥ daśa vai guṇahetavaḥ।। 73।।
aṇimā mahimā mūrttirlaghimā prāptirindriyaiḥ।।
prākāmyaṃ śrutadṛṣṭeṣu śaktiḥ preraṇamīśitāḥ।। 74।।
guṇeṣvasaṃgo vaśitā yatkāmastadavasyati।।
etā me siddhayaḥ saumya aṣṭāvautpattikāmatāḥ।। 75।।
anūrmimatvaṃ dehe'smin dūraśravaṇadarśanam।।
manojavaḥ kāmarūpaṃ parakāyapraveśanam।। 76।।
trikālajñatvamadvandvaṃ paracittādabhijñatā।।
agnyarkāmbuviṣādīnāṃ pratiṣṭambho'parājayaḥ।। 77।।
etāścoddeśataḥ proktā yogadhāraṇasiddhayaḥ।।
yayā dhāraṇayā yatsyādyathā tattannibodha me।। 78।।
bhūtasūkṣmātmani mayi tanmātre dhārayenmanaḥ।।
aṇimānamavāpnoti tanmātropāsako mama।। 79।।
mahatyātmani mayi ca yathāsaṃsthaṃ mano dadhat।।
mahimānamavāpnoti bhūtānāṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak।। 80।।
paramāṇumaye cittaṃ bhūtānāmanurañjayan।।
kālasūkṣmārthatāṃ yogī laghimānamavāpnuyāt।। 81।।
dhārayenmayyahaṃ tatve manovaikārike'khilam।।
sarvendriyāṇāmātmatvaṃ prāptiṃ prāpnoti manmanāḥ।। 82।।
mahatyātmani yaḥ sūtre dhārayenmayi mānasam।।
prākāmyaṃ pārameṣṭhyaṃ me vindate vyaktajanmanaḥ।। 83।।
viṣṇau tryadhīśvare cittaṃ dhārayet kālavigrahe।।
sa īśitvamavāpnoti kṣetrakṣetrajñacodanām।। 84।।
nārāyaṇe turīyākhye bhagavatpadaśabdite।।
manomayyādadhadyogīmaddharmāvaśitāmiyāt।। 85।।
nirguṇe brahmaṇi mayi dhārayedviṣadaṃ manaḥ।।
paramānandamāpnoti yatra kāmo'vasīyate।। 86।।
śvetadvīpapate cittaṃ śuddhaṃ dharmamaye mayi।।
dhārayan śvetatāṃ yāti ṣaḍūrmirahito naraḥ।। 87।।0
mayyākāśātmani prāṇe manasā ghoṣamudvahan।।
tatropalabdho bhūtānāṃ haṃso vācaḥ śṛṇotyasau।। 88।।
cakṣustvaṣṭari saṃyojya tvaṣṭāramapi cakṣuṣi।।
māṃ tatra manasā dhyāyan viśvaṃ paśyati sūkṣmadṛk।। 89।।
mano mayi susaṃyojya dehaṃ tadanu vāmunā।।
maddhāraṇānubhāvena tatrātmā yatra vai manaḥ।। 90।।
yadā mana upādāya yadyadrūpaṃ bubhūṣati।।
tattadbhavenmanorūpaṃ madyogabalamāśritaḥ।। 91।।
parakāyaṃ viśan siddha ātmānaṃ tatra bhāvayet।।
vimānenopatiṣṭhanti satvavṛttiḥ surastriyaḥ।। 92।।
yathā saṃkalpayed buddhyā yathā vā matparaḥ pumān।।
mayi satye mano yuñjan tathā tatsamupāsate।। 93।।
yo vai madbhāvamāpanno īśiturvaśituḥ pumān।।
na kutaścidvihanyeta tasya cājñā yathā mama।। 94।।
madbhaktyā śuddhasatvasya yogino dhāraṇāvidaḥ।।
tasya traikālikirbuddhirjanmamṛtyūpabṛṃhitā।। 95।।
udāsīne mayi pare prakṛterguṇavṛttiṣu।।
cinmaye dhārayecceto dvandvairnaivābhibhūyate।। 96।।
sarvabhūtahṛdisthaṃ māmātmānaṃ sarvasākṣiṇam।।
dhyāyedātmatayā cittaṃ pareṣāṃ paripaśyati।। 97।।
agnyādibhirna hanyeta muneryogamayaṃ vapuḥ।।
madyogaśrāntacittasya pādapāmudakaṃ yathā।। 98।।
madvibhūtiranudhyāyet śrīvatsāstra vibhūṣitāḥ।।
dhvajātapatravyajanaiḥ sa bhavedaparājitaḥ।। 99।।
upāsakasya māmevaṃ yogadhāraṇayā muneḥ।।
siddhayaḥ sarvakathitā upatiṣṭhantyaśeṣataḥ।। 100।।
jitendriyasya dāntasya jitaśvāsātmano muneḥ।।
maddhāraṇāṃ dhārayataḥ kā sā siddhiḥ sudurlabhā ।। 101।।
antarāyā bhavantyetā yuñjato yogamuttamam।।
mayi sampadyamānasya kālakṣapaṇa hetavaḥ।। 102।।
janmauṣadhitapomantrairyāvatīriha siddhayaḥ।।
yogenāpnoti tāḥ sarvā nānyairyogagatiṃ vrajet।। 103।।
yogacittasya tāḥ sarvāḥ upatiṣṭhanti siddhayaḥ।।
sarvāsāmapi siddhīnāṃ hetuḥ pati rahaṃ prabhuḥ।। 104।।
ahaṃ yogasya sāṃkhyasya jñānasya vividhasya ca।।
sarveṣvapi ca bhūteṣu bahirantaḥ svayaṃ tathā।। 105।।
tadatra saṃkṣepenopadeśaḥ -
yadā yadā yatra yatra yā yā siddhirabhīpsitā bhavati tadā tadā tatra tatra tattatsiddhiviśiṣṭamīśvaraṃ vibhāvya tathābhūtamevātmānaṃ vibhāvayet।। 106।।
yadā manastanmātraviṣayatayaiva sthirībhavet tadākāratāmāpnoti tadā sā siddhirjāyeta idaṃ tvavadheyam।। 107।।
tattatsiddhajanitamapi sukhaṃ deśakālāvacchinnatayālpameveti kautukāt tadindrajālavadanubhūya tatrābhiniveśaṃ parityajya deśakālānavacchinnasukharūpe brahmaṇyeva mano dhārayet।। yena sarvadā tatsukhasākṣātkāra eva syāt।। 108।।
evam aharniśaṃ tatsākṣātkurvato yoginaḥ sarvavidhe 'pi bhuktimuktikare sthite bhavataḥ।। 109।।
tathā hi gorakṣanāthaḥ -
etad vimuktisopānam etat kālasya vañcanam।।
yad vyāvṛttaṃ manobhogād āsaktaṃ paramātmani।। 110।।
yuktaṃ caitat।। tam eva viditvātimṛtyumeti nānyaḥ panthā vidyate 'yanāyetyādiśrutyā tat sākṣātkārasyaiva bhuktimuktihetutvapratipādanāt ।। 111।।
manasas tadākṛtatayā duḥkhādyanubhāvabhāvena kālavañcanasyāpi sambhavāc ca।। kiñ ca।। etad ajñānena kṛtaṃ dānādyapi na tathā phalaṃ janayati।। 112।।
tad uktaṃ bṛhadāraṇyakopaniṣadi -
yo vā etadakṣaramaviditvā gārgyasmin loke juhoti dadāti tapasyatyapi bahūni varṣa sahasrāṇyantavānevāsya sa loko bhavati।। 113।।
yo vā etadakṣaramaviditvā gārgyasmāṃllokātpraiti sa kṛpaṇo'tha ya etadakṣaraṃ gārgi viditvāsmāllokātpraiti sa brāhmaṇa ityādi।। 114।।
purāṇe'pi -
sā hānistanmahacchidraṃ sa mohassa ca vibhramaḥ।।
yanmuhūrttaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vāpi vāsudevaṃ na cintayet।। 115।।
āloḍya sarvaśāstrāṇi vicārya ca punaḥ punaḥ।।
idamekaṃ suniṣpannaṃ dhyeyo nārāyaṇaḥ sadā ।। 116।।
smarttavyaḥ sarvadā viṣṇurvismarttavyo na jātucit।।
sarve vidhiniṣedhāḥ syuretayoreva kiṃkarāḥ।। 117।।
ananyacetāḥ satataṃ yo māṃ smarati nityaśaḥ।।
tasyāhaṃ sulabhaḥ pārtha nityayuktasya yoginaḥ।। 118।।
anyatrāpi-
śayyāsanastho'pi pathi vrajan vā sukhaḥ parikṣīṇavitarkajālaḥ।।
saṃsārabījakṣayamīkṣamāṇaḥ syānnityayukto'mṛtabhogabhogī।।
ityādi vistārabhayānna tanyate।। 119।।
tasmādrājayogasyāpi evameva saṃkṣepenopadeśo yatsarvatra sarvadā sarvarūpenārūpeṇa vā sakaladikkālavartticaitanyānandasvarūpa brahmasākṣātkāra iti।। 120।।
atha haṭhayogasya saṃkṣepo'bhidhīyate -
savyena gulphena gudaṃ nipīḍya savyetareṇaiva nipīḍya sandhim।। dakṣiṇe kare savyaṃ karaṃ sthāpayitvā samāṃge netre nāsikāgre bhrūmadhye vā dhārayitvā manasā mūlādhārasthāṃ śikhāṃ paśyediti।। 121।।
evaṃ prātarmadhyāhnasandhyārdharātreṣu muhūrttamapi samabhayyasato yogino dinadaśakenaiva gudadvāranirodhādūrdhvamudgacchatāpānena pratyarpito vahnirūrdhvajvālo bhavati।। 122।।
tataḥ śarīralaghutā jāṭharānaladīptinādābhivyaktayo bhavanti।। tataḥ ṣaṇmāsena vatsareṇa vālpamūtrapurīṣatvam।। vāhanāsanādau ca nirbhayatvaṃ ca bhavati।। 123।।
tato'nilena sārdhamanalaṃ nābhau dhārayet।। tena tatrasthakuṇḍalinīrūpiṇaścakriṇaḥ prabodho bhavati ।। 124।।
tataḥ kalevare paṭe tantava iva vāyavaḥ sañcaranti।। tato vahniṃ tathaivānilena saha nābherūrdhvaṃ nayet।। 125।।
tato rogā naśyanti।। vahnivṛddhikāntyādayaśca bhavanti।। atha hṛtpaṃkajaṃ vahnisahito vāyuḥ praviśya protphullaṃ karoti।। tato vidyānidhitvaṃ bhavati।। 126।।
tataḥ sa vahniṃ vāyuṃ bhravormadhye samāropya candraṃ budhyā paśyet।। tadantare cātmānaṃ vibhāvayet।। tatra ca mano līnaṃ kuryāt।। 127।।
tatphalamuktaṃ yājñavalkyena -
mano layaṃ yadā yāti bhrūmadhye yogināṃ tadā।।
jihvāmūle'mṛtasrāvo bhrūmadhye cātmadarśanam।। 128।।
kaṃpanaṃ ca tathā mūrdhni manasaivātmadarśanam।।
devodyānāni ramyāṇi nakṣatrāṇi ca candramā।।
ṛṣayaḥ siddhagandharvāḥ prakāśante ca yoginām।। 129।।
sureśvarācāryo'pyāha-
ākuñcanamapānasya prāṇasya ca nirodhanam।।
lambikopari jihvāyāḥ sthāpanaṃ yogasādhanam।। iti ।। 130।।
śivayoge -
aṃguṣṭhābhyāmubhe śrotre tarjanībhyāṃ vilocane।।
nāsārandhre madhyamābhyām anyābhirvadanaṃ dṛḍham।। 131।।
nirudhya mārutaṃ yogī yadaiva kurute bhṛśam।।
tadā tatkṣaṇamātmānaṃ jyotirūpaṃ prapaśyati।।132।।
tattejodarśanaṃ divyaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham।।
muktidaṃ bhuktidaṃ ceti gīyante yogipuṃgavaiḥ।। 133।।
atha kālajñānādinā svīyamṛtyuṃ sambhāvya taddhāraṇopāyaṃ kuryāt।। 134।।
tatra kālajñānamuktaṃ samarasāre -
prātaḥ pṛṣṭhagate ravāvanimiṣaṃ chāyāṃ sthale svāṃ ciraṃ dṛṣṭvordhvaṃ nayanena yatsitataraṃ chāyānaraṃ paśyati tatkarṇāṃsa karāsyapārśvahṛdayābhāve kṣaṇerkā 12śva7dik10bhū1rāmā3kṣi2samāḥ śiro vigamato māsāṃstu ṣaṭ jīvati।। 135।।
etadarthastu-prātaḥkāle meghādyairanācchādite ravau pṛṣṭhataḥ sati anāvṛtasthale sthitvā prāṅmukhastiṣṭhan nimeṣaśūnye cakṣuṣī kurvan svakīyāṃ chāyāṃ ciraṃ sthale dṛṣṭvā tathābhūta evānimeṣe nayane ūrdhvaṃ nayan sitataram atiśvetaṃ chāyānaraṃ paśyati।। evaṃ prakāreṇa śaradādau candrodyote'pi chāyānaro dṛśyate।। 136।।
evaṃ dṛṣṭe phalam āha taditi-

tasya chāyānarasya karṇābhāvadarśane arkasamāḥdvādaśavarṣāṇi jīvati।। evam aṃsābhyāṃ bāhumūlābhyāṃ karābhyāṃ hastābhyām āsyena pārśvābhyāṃ hṛdayena ca vinā chāyāpuruṣadarśane kramādāyurvarṣāṇi aśva7dik10bhū1rāmā3akṣi2saṃkhyāni jīvatīti sambandhaḥ।। śirohīnachāyāpuruṣadarśane ṣaṇmāsān jīvatītyarthaḥ।। 137।।
hṛdrandhradṛṣṭyā munisaṃkhyamāsān dvidehadṛṣṭyā tu mṛtistadaiva।।
sampūrṇadṛṣṭau tu na varṣamadhye rogo mṛtirvetti vadanti satyam।। 138।।
chāyānarasya hṛdaye yadi randhro dṛśyate tadā saptamāsāvadhikamāyuḥ।। chāyānaradvayadarśane tadaiva maraṇam।। sampūrṇachāyānaradarśanaṃ varṣamadhye na rogo na mṛtītyarthaḥ।। 139।।
anyadapyāha -
snātasya pūrva karṇādeḥ śoṣe prāguktavatphalam।। sarvārdrāṃgasya hṛcchoṣe ṣaṇmāsābhyantare mṛtiḥ।। kṛtasnānamātrasya puṃsaḥ karṇādeḥ karṇāṃsahasta mukha pārśvahṛdayāntarebhyaḥ prathamataḥ śoṣe prāguktavatphalam।। dvādaśa12sapta7daśa1¬¬0eka1tri3dvivarṣāṇi jīvati।। sarvārdrāṃgasya hṛdayaśoṣo ṣaṇmāsābhyantare mṛtirityarthaḥ।। 140।।
anyadapyāha-
haste nyaste śirasi yadi na chinnadaṇḍo'sya dṛṣṭaḥ।।
ṣaṇmāsāntarna maraṇabhayaṃ sampuṭe hastayośca।।
nyaste śīrṣe yadi ca kadalīkorakābhaṃ tadantar
dṛṣṭaṃ no bhāstarati salile cetsuśepho na mṛtyuḥ।। 141।।
svakīye śirasi haste nyaste sati yadi kadalīkorakābhā chinnadaṇḍo na dṛśyate tadā na maraṇam।। evaṃ mūrdhni hastayoḥ sampuṭe nyaste sati tadantastayordvayoḥ prakoṣṭhayormadhye yadi kadalīkorakābhaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tadāpi na maraṇabhītiḥ।। śepha ścetsalile tarati tadāpi na maraṇabhītiḥ।। 142।।
evaṃ karṇau hastābhyāṃ nirudhya yaḥ śārīraḥ prāṇaghoṣaḥ sarvadā sarvaiśca śrūyate sa ceda kasmānna śrūyate yadvā akasmāt prakṛtiviparyayo bhavati tadāpi tadbhayaṃ veditavyam।। 143।।
evamevaibhyo'nyebhyo vā nimittebhyo'pamṛtyuṃ sambhāvya paramārthavinnirbhayaḥ prasannātmā jitendriyaḥ svasvakarmarataḥ śāntaḥ sarvabhūtahite rato bhūtvā yathāvadubhaya pakṣe'pi putrādīnupadiśya yogavidbhiḥ samāvṛtam।। 144।।
śuciḥ gaṃgātīrādipuṇyakṣetraṃ gatvā tatra bhūmau kuśakṛṣṇājinādīnyāstīrya prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā baddhaparyaṅka samupaviśya yathāvadbhūtaśuddhiprāṇapratiṣṭhāṃganyāsakaranyāsamātṛkānyāsaṣoḍhānyāsādi bhistadttabhāvanādibhiścātmānamajaramamaraṃ devatātmānaṃ dhyātvā gurugaṇeśabaṭukabhairavakṣetrapālayoginī praṇamyārcayitvā ca bhagavantamanāmayaṃ śrīsahitaṃ vāsudevaṃ sarvamayaṃ vibhāvya vilokya pūjayitvā ca brāhmaṇādibhyo bhūmisuvarṇavarābhayadivyauṣadharasāyanabrahmavidyādīni datvā navadvārāṇi saṃyamya prāṇāyāmaiḥ prabodhite hṛdayāmbhoje sarvakāraṇaṃ prabhārūpaṃ cidānandaṃ vyomātmakaṃ sarvaśaktikaṃ brahma bhāvayannomityekākṣaraṃ mṛtyuñjayabījapuṭitaṃ japan--।। 145।।
tālumūlasthena yathordhvīkṛtayā jihvayā sasītkāraṃ viśuddhicakrasthacandrāmṛtaṃ piban śirasthacandrabimbaṃ sravatāmṛta dhārayātmaśarīraṃ plāvayet।। paramānandarūpe ca brahmaṇi savikalpakaṃ nirvikalpakaṃ ca samādhiṃ tathā vidadhyādyathā na mano'nyaviṣayamāsādayati।। evaṃ parameśvarasamādhiṃ kurvato yogino na mṛtyuraniṣṭaṃ kurute।। 146।।
tathā ca gorakṣanāthaḥ -
sravatpīyūṣasampūrṇaṃ lambakācandramaṇḍale।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā mṛtyuṃ vimuñcati।। 147।।
vidyutprabhe ca hṛtpadme prāṇāyāmavibhedataḥ।।
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvā brahmamayo bhavet।। 148।।
yāvad baddhā nabhomudrā yāvaccittaṃ nirāmayam।।
yāvad dṛṣṭirbhruvormadhye tāvanmṛtyubhayaṃ kutaḥ।। 149।।
yāvadvāyuḥ sthito dehe tāvaddehaṃ na muñcati।।
maraṇaṃ tasya niṣkrāntistato vāyuṃ nirundhayet।। 150।।
khādyate na sa kālena bādhyate na ca karmaṇā।।
sādhyate na ca kenāpi yogī yuktaḥ samādhinā।। 151।।
abhedyaḥ sarvaśastrāṇāmavadhyaḥ sarvadehinām।।
agrāhyo mantrayantrāṇāṃ yogī yuktaḥ samādhinā।। 152।।
idaṃ tvavadheyam।। yadi deho rakṣyo bhavati tadā prāṇamanasī ekīkṛtya śarīrāntarvarttini hṛdayāmbujabhrūmadhya viśuddhicakranābhi cakrādau sthāpayitvā samādhi kuryāt।। 153।।
yadi tu dehāntaraprāptirīpsitā bhavati tadā tathābhūte dehe samādhiṃ kuryāt।। yadi tu dehāntaraprāptirīpsitā na bhavati tadā saṃhārakrameṇa pañcabhūtāni saṃhṛtya prāṇamanasī mūlādhārasvādhiṣṭhānamaṇipūrakānāhataviśuddhicakrājñācakrasahasradalakamaleṣu nītvā śuddhe cidānandamaye brahmaṇi dhārayet।। tena brahmasāyujyaṃ bhavati।। 154।।
taduktaṃ gītāyām -
prayāṇakāle manasācalena bhaktyā yukto yogabalena caiva।।
bhruvormadhye prāṇamāveśya samyak sa taṃ paraṃ puruṣamupaiti divyam।। 155।।
yadakṣaraṃ vedavido vadanti viśanti yadyatayo vītarāgāḥ।।
yadīcchanto brahmacaryaṃ caranti tatte padaṃ saṃgraheṇa pravakṣye।। 156।।
sarvadvārāṇi saṃyamya mano hṛdi nirudhya ca।।
mūrdhnyādhāyātmanaḥ prāṇamāsthito yogadhāraṇam।। 157।।
omityekākṣaraṃ brahma vyāharanmāmanusmaran।।
yaḥ prayāti tyajan dehaṃ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim।। 158।।
tathā ca -
yaṃ yaṃ bhāvaṃ smaranmartyastyajatyante kalevaram।।
taṃ tameveti kaunteya sadā tadbhāvabhāvitam।। ityādi।। 159।।
evaṃ ca dehadhāraṇāpakṣe samādhyā saṃgena apamṛtyusamaye apavāhite śiṣyaḥ sakhā vā samādhyapagamāya sādhakasya brahmarandhre navanītaṃ marddayet।। 160।।
tena tasya prabodho bhavati jihvā codhomukhī jāyate।। ityuktaṃ yogīndrairavadheyam।। mayā tvekadeśamātramuktaṃ vastuvijñānāyeti śivam।। 161।।
iṣuyugaśaracandrairlakṣite'bde śakendos
tribhuvanamayamīśaṃ vīkṣya dṛṣṭaḥ svabuddhyā।।
akṛtasukṛta siddhairyogaśāstre nibandhaṃ
mithilabhuvi bhavadevaḥ kṛṣṇa devātmajanmā।। 162।।
yadiha kimapi yuktaṃ proktametena viṣṇus
tribhuvanagururīśaḥ prīyatāṃ sarvadā me।।
atha yadiha na yuktaṃ śodhayantu pravīṇās
tadakhilamakhilajñaḥ ko vinā śrīmaheśam।। 163।।
iti maithilasanmiśra śrīkṛṣṇadevatanayasanmiśraśrībaladevāgrajamahāmahopādhyāya bhavadevaviracite yuktabhavadevanāmni yoganibandhe samādhyupadeśo nāma ekādaśopadeśaḥ।। 11।। 

śubhaṃ bhavatu।। śrīrastu ।। śrīrāmacandrārpaṇam astu।। śrīnṛsiṃhārpaṇam astu।।
